          return setStatus("idle", "not_ready");
        }
        if (!origWsSend) return setStatus("idle", "socket_unavailable");
        const economy = getTroopEconomy?.();
        if (!isInsideFarmWindow(state, settings, economy)) return setStatus("idle", "outside_window");
        if (inFlight) return setStatus("cooldown", "tile_scan");
        const now = performance.now();
        if (now - lastSentAt < BOAT_COOLDOWN_MS) {
          return setStatus("cooldown", "cooldown", { cooldownMs: Math.round(BOAT_COOLDOWN_MS - (now - lastSentAt)) });
        }
        const myState = gameState.getMyState();
        if (!myState || !myState.isAlive) return setStatus("idle", "not_alive");
        const safeSpendable = finiteOrZero(economy?.safeSpendableTroops);
        const maxTroops = finiteOrZero(economy?.maxTroops);
        if (safeSpendable <= MIN_SURPLUS || maxTroops <= 0) {
          logSkip("reserve_limit", { safeSpendable });
          return setStatus("blocked", "reserve_limit", { safeSpendable });
        }
        const troops = Math.max(MIN_BOAT_TROOPS, Math.floor(Math.min(safeSpendable, maxTroops * BOAT_WAVE_RATIO)));
        const gameView = getGameView();
        if (!gameView || typeof gameView.myPlayer !== "function") {
          logSkip("no_game_view");
          return setStatus("blocked", "no_game_view");
        }
        const myPlayer = safeMyPlayer4(gameView);
        if (!myPlayer) {
          logSkip("no_player");
          return setStatus("blocked", "no_player");
        }
        inFlight = true;
        chooseBoatDst(gameView, myPlayer, mapDataRef.current).then((dst) => {
          inFlight = false;
          if (dst == null) {
            logSkip("no_water_target");
            setStatus("blocked", "no_water_target");
            return;
          }
          origWsSend.call(
            gameState.state.gameSocket,
            JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent: { type: "boat", troops, dst } })
          );
          lastSentAt = performance.now();
          lastDst = dst;
          logger.info(\`Auto-boat expand: \${troops} troops -> tile \${dst}\`);
          roundLogger?.record("auto_boat_sent", { dst, troops, safeSpendable: Math.floor(safeSpendable) });
          setStatus("sent", \`sent_\${troops}\`, { dst, troops });
        }).catch((error) => {
          inFlight = false;
          logSkip("tile_scan_failed", { message: String(error?.message || error || "") });
          setStatus("blocked", "tile_scan_failed");
        });
        return setStatus("scanning", "scanning");
      }
    };
    function logSkip(reason, extra = {}) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (now - lastSkipAt < 5e3) return;
      lastSkipAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record("auto_boat_skipped", { reason, ...extra });
    }
    function setStatus(state, reason, extra = {}) {
      status = { state, reason, ...extra };
      return status;
    }
  }
  function chooseBoatDst(gameView, myPlayer, mapData) {
    return Promise.resolve().then(() => typeof myPlayer.borderTiles === "function" ? myPlayer.borderTiles() : null).then((info) => {
      const borders = info && info.borderTiles;
      if (!borders || typeof borders.forEach !== "function") return null;
      const width = mapData?.width;
      const height = mapData?.height;
      const terrain = mapData?.terrain;
      if (!width || !height || !terrain) return null;
      const visited = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      const queue = [];
      borders.forEach((tile) => {
        const num = Number(tile);
        if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return;
        neighborsOf(num, width, height).forEach((nb) => {
          if (!isLandByte(terrain[nb]) && !visited.has(nb)) {
            visited.add(nb);
            queue.push({ tile: nb, dist: 1 });
          }
        });
      });
      let head = 0;
      while (head < queue.length && visited.size < BFS_TILE_CAP) {
        const { tile, dist } = queue[head];
        head += 1;
        if (dist > MAX_BOAT_DISTANCE) continue;
        const nbs = neighborsOf(tile, width, height);
        for (let i = 0; i < nbs.length; i += 1) {
          const nb = nbs[i];
          if (visited.has(nb)) continue;
          if (isLandByte(terrain[nb])) {
            if (isUnclaimedLand(gameView, nb)) return nb;
            visited.add(nb);
          } else {
            visited.add(nb);
            queue.push({ tile: nb, dist: dist + 1 });
          }
        }
      }
      return null;
    });
  }
  function isUnclaimedLand(gameView, tile) {
    try {
      if (typeof gameView.hasOwner === "function") return !gameView.hasOwner(tile);
      const owner = typeof gameView.owner === "function" ? gameView.owner(tile) : null;
      if (!owner) return true;
      return typeof owner.isPlayer === "function" ? !owner.isPlayer() : false;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  function neighborsOf(tile, width, height) {
    const x = tile % width;
    const y = Math.floor(tile / width);
    const out = [];
    if (x > 0) out.push(tile - 1);
    if (x < width - 1) out.push(tile + 1);
    if (y > 0) out.push(tile - width);
    if (y < height - 1) out.push(tile + width);
    return out;
  }
  function safeMyPlayer4(gameView) {
    try {
      return gameView && typeof gameView.myPlayer === "function" ? gameView.myPlayer() : null;
    } catch (_) {
    }
    return null;
  }

  // src/page/automation/nuke-retaliation.js
  var INCOMING_MISSILE_TYPES = ["Atom Bomb", "Hydrogen Bomb", "MIRV Warhead"];
  var PREFERRED_TARGET_UNITS = [UNIT.MISSILE_SILO, UNIT.SAM_LAUNCHER, UNIT.CITY, UNIT.PORT, UNIT.FACTORY];
  var SALVO_OVERWHELM_MARGIN = 1;
  var MAX_SALVO_SIZE = 6;
  function samRangeForLevel(level) {
    const lvl = Math.max(1, finiteOrZero(level) || 1);
    return Math.max(30, 150 - 480 / (lvl + 5));
  }
  function createNukeRetaliation({ gameState, teamDetection, goldIntel = null }) {
    function buildPlan({ gameView, myState, myPlayer, threats } = {}) {
      if (!gameView || !myState) return emptyPlan("no_context");
      const incoming = detectIncomingOnMe(gameView, myState);
      if (!incoming.length) return emptyPlan("no_incoming");
      const mySilos = scanUnitsByType(gameView, UNIT.MISSILE_SILO, myState, teamDetection).filter((s) => s.isMine && !s.underConstruction);
      const launchCapacity = computeLaunchCapacity(mySilos);
      if (launchCapacity.parallel <= 0) {
        return { incoming, salvo: [], notes: ["Keine feuerbereiten Silos (Cooldown oder im Bau)."], capacity: launchCapacity };
      }
      const weapon = chooseAffordableWeapon(gameView, myPlayer, finiteOrZero(myState.gold));
      if (!weapon) {
        return { incoming, salvo: [], notes: ["Aktuell keine Waffe leistbar (Gold/Kosten unbekannt)."], capacity: launchCapacity };
      }
      const candidates = rankTargets({ gameView, myState, myPlayer, threats });
      if (!candidates.length) {
        return { incoming, salvo: [], notes: ["Keine sichtbaren feindlichen Strukturziele."], capacity: launchCapacity };
      }
      const maxSalvo = Math.min(launchCapacity.parallel, MAX_SALVO_SIZE);
      const salvo = planSalvo({ candidates, weapon, maxSalvo, gameView });
      const notes = buildNotes({ launchCapacity, weapon, salvo, candidates });
      return { incoming, salvo, notes, capacity: launchCapacity, weapon: { unit: weapon.unit, cost: weapon.cost } };
    }
    function detectIncomingOnMe(gameView, myState) {
      const out = [];
      let units = null;
      try {
        if (typeof gameView.units !== "function") return out;
        units = gameView.units(...INCOMING_MISSILE_TYPES);
      } catch (_) {
        return out;
      }
      if (!units || typeof units.forEach !== "function") return out;
      units.forEach((unit) => {
        try {
          if (typeof unit.isActive === "function" && !unit.isActive()) return;
          const targetTile = typeof unit.targetTile === "function" ? unit.targetTile() : null;
          if (targetTile == null) return;
          if (!isOwnedByMe3(gameView, targetTile, myState)) return;
          const type = typeof unit.type === "function" ? unit.type() : null;
          const owner = typeof unit.owner === "function" ? safeCall2(() => unit.owner()) : null;
          const ownerID = owner ? typeof owner.id === "function" ? safeCall2(() => owner.id()) : owner.id : null;
          out.push({
            type,
            targetTile,
            attackerID: ownerID,
            attackerName: owner ? safeName2(owner) : "Unknown",
            coords: tileCoords2(gameView, targetTile)
          });
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
      return out;
    }
    function rankTargets({ gameView, myState, myPlayer, threats }) {
      const threatRank = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      (threats || []).forEach((threat, index) => {
        if (threat && threat.id != null) threatRank.set(threat.id, index);
      });
      const enemySams = scanUnitsByType(gameView, UNIT.SAM_LAUNCHER, myState, teamDetection).filter(
        (s) => !s.isMine && !s.isAlly && !s.underConstruction && s.coords
      );
      const candidates = [];
      PREFERRED_TARGET_UNITS.forEach((unitType, typeIndex) => {
        const structureValue = readStructureValue(unitType, gameView, myPlayer);
        const units = scanUnitsByType(gameView, unitType, myState, teamDetection).filter(
          (u) => !u.isMine && !u.isAlly && !u.underConstruction && u.tile != null && u.ownerID != null
        );
        units.forEach((u) => {
          const threatPriority = threatRank.has(u.ownerID) ? threatRank.get(u.ownerID) : 999;
          const samDefense = countNearbySams(enemySams, u.coords);
          candidates.push({
            unitType,
            typeIndex,
            tile: u.tile,
            targetID: u.ownerID,
            targetName: u.ownerName,
            structureValue,
            samDefense,
            threatPriority,
            coords: u.coords
          });
        });
      });
      candidates.sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.threatPriority !== b.threatPriority) return a.threatPriority - b.threatPriority;
        if (a.typeIndex !== b.typeIndex) return a.typeIndex - b.typeIndex;
        return finiteOrZero(b.structureValue) - finiteOrZero(a.structureValue);
      });
      return candidates;
    }
    function planSalvo({ candidates, weapon, maxSalvo, gameView }) {
      const isMirv = weapon.unit === UNIT.MIRV;
      const salvo = [];
      const skippedSamWalls = [];
      const usedTiles = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length && salvo.length < maxSalvo; i += 1) {
        const c = candidates[i];
        if (usedTiles.has(c.tile)) continue;
        const remaining = maxSalvo - salvo.length;
        let count;
        if (isMirv) {
          count = 1;
        } else {
          const needToOverwhelm = c.samDefense > 0 ? c.samDefense + SALVO_OVERWHELM_MARGIN : 1;
          if (c.samDefense > 0 && needToOverwhelm > remaining) {
            skippedSamWalls.push(c);
            continue;
          }
          count = Math.max(1, Math.min(needToOverwhelm, remaining));
        }
        usedTiles.add(c.tile);
        for (let k = 0; k < count; k += 1) {
          salvo.push({
            unit: weapon.unit,
            tile: c.tile,
            targetID: c.targetID,
            targetName: c.targetName,
            reason: salvoReason(c, k, count, isMirv)
          });
        }
      }
      salvo.skippedSamWalls = skippedSamWalls;
      return salvo;
    }
    return {
      buildPlan,
      detectIncomingOnMe,
      rankTargets
    };
  }
  function salvoReason(c, index, count, isMirv) {
    const what = \`\${c.unitType} von \${c.targetName}\`;
    if (isMirv && c.samDefense > 0) return \`\${what} \\u2013 MIRV \\xFCbers\\xE4ttigt SAM (~\${c.samDefense})\`;
    if (c.samDefense > 0) {
      return count > 1 ? \`\${what} \\u2013 SAM-Verteidigung (~\${c.samDefense}) \\xFCberladen (\${index + 1}/\${count})\` : \`\${what} \\u2013 SAM in der N\\xE4he (~\${c.samDefense})\`;
    }
    return \`\${what} \\u2013 ungesch\\xFCtzt\`;
  }
  function buildNotes({ launchCapacity, weapon, salvo, candidates }) {
    const notes = [];
    notes.push(\`Feuerbereit: \${launchCapacity.parallel} Rakete(n) aus \${launchCapacity.silos} Silo(s).\`);
    notes.push(\`Waffe: \${weapon.unit} (~\${Math.round(weapon.cost).toLocaleString()} Gold/St\\xFCck).\`);
    if (salvo.length) {
      notes.push(\`Vorgeschlagene Salve: \${salvo.length} Abschuss/Absch\\xFCsse auf \${countUniqueTargets(salvo)} Ziel(e).\`);
    } else {
      notes.push("Keine sinnvolle Salve berechenbar.");
    }
    if (weapon.unit === "MIRV" && salvo.some((shot) => /SAM/.test(shot.reason || ""))) {
      notes.push("MIRV gew\\xE4hlt: \\xFCbers\\xE4ttigt SAM-Cluster (Einzelnukes w\\xFCrden abgefangen).");
    }
    const skipped = salvo.skippedSamWalls && salvo.skippedSamWalls.length || 0;
    if (skipped > 0) notes.push(\`\${skipped} SAM-gesch\\xFCtzte(s) Ziel(e) nur mit MIRV durchdringbar (Kapazit\\xE4t reicht f\\xFCr Einzelnukes nicht).\`);
    if (candidates.length > salvo.length) {
      notes.push(\`Weitere \${candidates.length} Strukturziel(e) bekannt, durch Kapazit\\xE4t begrenzt.\`);
    }
    return notes;
  }
  function countUniqueTargets(salvo) {
    const set = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    salvo.forEach((s) => set.add(s.tile));
    return set.size;
  }
  function computeLaunchCapacity(mySilos) {
    let parallel = 0;
    let ready = 0;
    mySilos.forEach((silo) => {
      const r = silo.readiness || siloReadiness(silo);
      const free = Math.max(0, finiteOrZero(r.level) - finiteOrZero(r.queueLength));
      parallel += free;
      if (free > 0) ready += 1;
    });
    return { parallel, silos: mySilos.length, readySilos: ready };
  }
  function chooseAffordableWeapon(gameView, myPlayer, gold2) {
    const order = [UNIT.MIRV, UNIT.HYDROGEN_BOMB, UNIT.ATOM_BOMB];
    for (let i = 0; i < order.length; i += 1) {
      const unit = order[i];
      const cost = getStructureCost(unit, { gameView, player: myPlayer });
      if (cost.source !== "runtime") continue;
      if (gold2 >= cost.cost + cost.reserveGold) return { unit, cost: cost.cost };
    }
    return null;
  }
  function readStructureValue(unitType, gameView, myPlayer) {
    try {
      const cost = getStructureCost(unitType, { gameView, player: myPlayer });
      return cost.source === "runtime" ? cost.cost : null;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
  function countNearbySams(enemySams, coords) {
    if (!coords) return 0;
    let n = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < enemySams.length; i += 1) {
      const sc = enemySams[i].coords;
      if (!sc) continue;
      const lvl = finiteOrZero(enemySams[i].readiness?.level) || 1;
      const dist = Math.hypot(sc.x - coords.x, sc.y - coords.y);
      if (dist <= samRangeForLevel(lvl)) n += lvl;
    }
    return n;
  }
  function emptyPlan(reason) {
    return { incoming: [], salvo: [], notes: [], reason };
  }
  function isOwnedByMe3(gameView, tile, myState) {
    try {
      if (!gameView || typeof gameView.owner !== "function") return false;
      const owner = gameView.owner(tile);
      if (!owner) return false;
      const ownerID = typeof owner.id === "function" ? owner.id() : owner.id;
      return ownerID === myState.id || ownerID === myState.smallID;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  function tileCoords2(gameView, tile) {
    try {
      if (tile == null || typeof gameView.x !== "function" || typeof gameView.y !== "function") return null;
      return { x: gameView.x(tile), y: gameView.y(tile) };
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
  function safeName2(player) {
    try {
      if (typeof player.displayName === "function") return player.displayName();
      if (typeof player.name === "function") return player.name();
    } catch (_) {
    }
    return "Unknown";
  }
  function safeCall2(fn) {
    try {
      return fn();
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // src/page/automation/auto-weapons.js
  function createAutoWeapons({ gameState, mapDataRef, OrigWS, origWsSend, settings, logger, roundLogger, teamDetection, getTroopEconomy, goldIntel = null }) {
    const retaliation = createNukeRetaliation({ gameState, teamDetection, goldIntel });
    let plan = emptyPlan2("not_started");
    let status = { state: "idle", reason: "not_started" };
    let lastFireAt = 0;
    return {
      reset() {
        plan = emptyPlan2("reset");
        status = { state: "idle", reason: "reset" };
        lastFireAt = 0;
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      // Voller Plan f\xFCr die Approval-UI: { incoming, salvo, notes, capacity, weapon, reason }.
      getPlan() {
        return plan;
      },
      run({ threats } = {}, options = {}) {
        if (!options.force && !settings.get("autoWeapons")) {
          plan = emptyPlan2("disabled");
          return setStatus("idle", "disabled");
        }
        const state = gameState.state;
        if (!state.gameStarted || !state.myPlayerID) {
          plan = emptyPlan2("not_ready");
          return setStatus("idle", "not_ready");
        }
        const myState = gameState.getMyState();
        if (!myState || !myState.isAlive) {
          plan = emptyPlan2("not_alive");
          return setStatus("idle", "not_alive");
        }
        const gameView = getGameView();
        if (!gameView || typeof gameView.myPlayer !== "function") {
          plan = emptyPlan2("no_game_view");
          return setStatus("blocked", "no_game_view");
        }
        const myPlayer = safeMyPlayer5(gameView);
        if (!myPlayer) {
          plan = emptyPlan2("no_player");
          return setStatus("blocked", "no_player");
        }
        plan = retaliation.buildPlan({ gameView, myState, myPlayer, threats: threats || [] });
        if (!plan.incoming || !plan.incoming.length) {
          return setStatus("monitoring", plan.reason || "no_incoming");
        }
        if (!plan.salvo || !plan.salvo.length) {
          return setStatus("alert_no_salvo", "incoming_no_salvo", { incoming: plan.incoming.length });
        }
        return setStatus("awaiting_approval", "salvo_ready", {
          incoming: plan.incoming.length,
          salvoSize: plan.salvo.length
        });
      },
      // Sendet die aktuell berechnete Salve \u2013 NUR auf expliziten Nutzer-Klick aufzurufen.
      // Liefert { sent, attempted, reason }. Sendet nie ohne validen, aktuellen Plan.
      approveAndFire() {
        if (!OrigWS) return fireResult(0, 0, "websocket_unavailable");
        const state = gameState.state;
        if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN) {
          return fireResult(0, 0, "socket_not_open");
        }
        if (!origWsSend) return fireResult(0, 0, "socket_unavailable");
        const salvo = plan && Array.isArray(plan.salvo) ? plan.salvo : [];
        if (!salvo.length) return fireResult(0, 0, "no_salvo");
        let sent = 0;
        salvo.forEach((shot) => {
          if (shot.unit == null || shot.tile == null) return;
          try {
            origWsSend.call(
              state.gameSocket,
              JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent: { type: "build_unit", unit: shot.unit, tile: shot.tile } })
            );
            sent += 1;
          } catch (error) {
            logSkip("fire_send_failed", { message: String(error?.message || error || "") });
          }
        });
        lastFireAt = performance.now();
        logger.info(\`Auto-weapons APPROVED salvo: \${sent}/\${salvo.length} launched\`);
        roundLogger?.record("auto_weapons_salvo_fired", {
          attempted: salvo.length,
          sent,
          targets: salvo.map((s) => ({ unit: s.unit, tile: s.tile, targetName: s.targetName, targetID: s.targetID })),
          incoming: (plan.incoming || []).map((i) => ({ type: i.type, attackerName: i.attackerName }))
        });
        plan = emptyPlan2("fired");
        setStatus("fired", "salvo_fired", { sent, attempted: salvo.length });
        return fireResult(sent, salvo.length, "ok");
      }
    };
    function logSkip(reason, extra = {}) {
      roundLogger?.record("auto_weapons_skipped", { reason, ...extra });
    }
    function setStatus(state, reason, extra = {}) {
      status = { state, reason, ...extra };
      return status;
    }
  }
  function fireResult(sent, attempted, reason) {
    return { sent, attempted, reason };
  }
  function emptyPlan2(reason) {
    return { incoming: [], salvo: [], notes: [], capacity: null, weapon: null, reason };
  }
  function safeMyPlayer5(gameView) {
    try {
      return gameView && typeof gameView.myPlayer === "function" ? gameView.myPlayer() : null;
    } catch (_) {
    }
    return null;
  }

  // src/page/automation/auto-team-support.js
  var SUPPORT_COOLDOWN_MS = 1e4;
  var TARGET_COOLDOWN_MS = 1e4;
  var MIN_DONATION_TROOPS = 5e3;
  var MAX_DONATION_OWN_SHARE = 1 / 3;
  var SUPPORT_RESERVE_RATIO = 0.55;
  var SELF_ATTACK_BLOCK_RATIO = 0.25;
  function createAutoTeamSupport({ gameState, OrigWS, origWsSend, settings, logger, roundLogger, teamDetection, getTroopEconomy }) {
    const targetCooldowns = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    let lastSentAt = 0;
    let lastSkipAt = 0;
    let lastCandidateLogAt = 0;
    let status = { state: "idle", reason: "not_started" };
    return {
      reset() {
        targetCooldowns.clear();
        lastSentAt = 0;
        lastSkipAt = 0;
        lastCandidateLogAt = 0;
        status = { state: "idle", reason: "reset" };
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      recommend() {
        return rankSupportCandidates().slice(0, 3);
      },
      run(options = {}) {
        const state = gameState.state;
        if (!options.force && !settings.get("autoTeamSupport")) return setStatus("idle", "disabled");
        if (!teamDetection?.isTeamMode) return setStatus("idle", "not_team_mode");
        if (!OrigWS) return setStatus("idle", "websocket_unavailable");
        if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN || !state.gameStarted || !state.myPlayerID) {
          return setStatus("idle", "not_ready");
        }
        if (!origWsSend) return setStatus("idle", "socket_unavailable");
        const now = performance.now();
        if (tryDonateTroops(now, options)) return status;
        if (now - lastSentAt < SUPPORT_COOLDOWN_MS) {
          return setStatus("cooldown", "cooldown", { cooldownMs: Math.round(SUPPORT_COOLDOWN_MS - (now - lastSentAt)) });
        }
        return status;
      }
    };
    function minDonation() {
      const v = Number(settings.get("teamSupportMinDonation"));
      return Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0 ? v : MIN_DONATION_TROOPS;
    }
    function reserveFloor() {
      const v = Number(settings.get("teamSupportMinOwnReserveRatio"));
      return Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0 && v < 1 ? v : SUPPORT_RESERVE_RATIO;
    }
    function tryDonateTroops(now, options = {}) {
      const state = gameState.state;
      if (now - lastSentAt < SUPPORT_COOLDOWN_MS) return false;
      const gate = supportGate(options);
      if (!gate.ok) {
        logSkip(gate.reason, gate.extra);
        setStatus("blocked", gate.reason, gate.extra);
        return false;
      }
      const candidates = rankSupportCandidates();
      logCandidates(candidates);
      const target = candidates.find((candidate) => {
        const lastTargetSentAt = targetCooldowns.get(candidate.id) || 0;
        return candidate.troops >= minDonation() && now - lastTargetSentAt >= TARGET_COOLDOWN_MS;
      });
      if (!target) {
        logSkip(candidates.length ? "cooldown_or_too_small" : "no_candidate");
        setStatus("idle", candidates.length ? "cooldown_or_too_small" : "no_candidate");
        return false;
      }
      const troops = Math.floor(target.troops);
      origWsSend.call(
        state.gameSocket,
        JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent: { type: "donate_troops", recipient: target.id, troops } })
      );
      lastSentAt = now;
      targetCooldowns.set(target.id, now);
      logger.info(\`Auto-team support \${target.name || target.id}: \${troops} troops\`);
      roundLogger?.record("auto_team_support_sent", {
        recipient: target.id,
        name: target.name || target.id,
        troops,
        reason: target.reason,
        incoming: target.incoming,
        ownReserveAfterRatio: target.ownReserveAfterRatio
      });
      setStatus("sent", \`sent_\${target.name || target.id}\`, { targetName: target.name || target.id, troops });
      return true;
    }
    function supportGate(options = {}) {
      const myState = gameState.getMyState();
      if (!myState || !myState.isAlive) return { ok: false, reason: "not_alive" };
      const myTroops = finiteOrZero(myState.troops);
      const selfIncoming = incomingAttackTroops(gameState, myState);
      if (myTroops > 0 && selfIncoming >= SELF_ATTACK_BLOCK_RATIO * myTroops) {
        return { ok: false, reason: "self_under_attack", extra: { incoming: Math.round(selfIncoming) } };
      }
      const defense = options.automationStatus?.autoDefense;
      if (defense?.emergency) {
        return { ok: false, reason: "defense_active", extra: { defenseReason: defense.reason || null } };
      }
      const expand = options.automationStatus?.autoExpand;
      const farm = options.automationStatus?.autoFarm;
      if (expand?.state === "sent") return { ok: false, reason: "expand_active" };
      if (farm?.state === "sent") return { ok: false, reason: "farm_active" };
      const aiNeighbours = countHostileAiNeighbours(options.neighbourIDs);
      if (aiNeighbours > 0) return { ok: false, reason: "early_ai_neighbours", extra: { aiNeighbours } };
      return { ok: true };
    }
    function rankSupportCandidates() {
      const myState = gameState.getMyState();
      const economy = getTroopEconomy?.();
      if (!myState || !myState.isAlive || !teamDetection?.isTeamMode) return [];
      const myTroops = finiteOrZero(myState.troops);
      const maxTroops = finiteOrZero(economy?.maxTroops);
      if (myTroops <= 0 || maxTroops <= 0) return [];
      const reserveRatio = Math.max(reserveFloor(), finiteOrZero(economy?.recommendedReserve));
      const safeAvailable = Math.max(0, myTroops - maxTroops * reserveRatio);
      if (safeAvailable < minDonation()) return [];
      const isCapWaste = economy?.state === "CAP_WASTE" || finiteOrZero(economy?.currentRatio) >= 0.85;
      const teammates = teamDetection.getMyTeamMembers().map((member) => gameState.state.playerStates.get(member.id)).filter((player) => player && player.id !== myState.id && player.isAlive);
      return teammates.map((player) => makeCandidate({ player, myTroops, maxTroops, safeAvailable, isCapWaste })).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    }
    function makeCandidate({ player, myTroops, maxTroops, safeAvailable, isCapWaste }) {
      const teammateTroops = finiteOrZero(player.troops);
      const incoming = incomingAttackTroopsForSmallID(gameState, player.smallID, player.id);
      const troubleDeficit = incoming > 0 ? Math.max(0, incoming * 1.25 - teammateTroops) : 0;
      const lowTeamDeficit = isCapWaste ? Math.max(0, myTroops * 0.25 - teammateTroops) : 0;
      const capWastePush = isCapWaste && teammateTroops < myTroops * 0.45 ? myTroops * 0.12 : 0;
      const inCombat = troopsInCombat(player);
      const focusPush = isCapWaste && inCombat > teammateTroops * 0.25 ? myTroops * 0.15 : 0;
      const desired = Math.max(troubleDeficit, lowTeamDeficit, capWastePush, focusPush);
      const maxDonation = Math.min(safeAvailable, myTroops * MAX_DONATION_OWN_SHARE);
      const troops = Math.min(desired, maxDonation);
      if (troops < minDonation()) return null;
      const ownReserveAfterRatio = (myTroops - troops) / maxTroops;
      const reason = troubleDeficit > 0 ? "under_attack" : lowTeamDeficit > 0 ? "low_team_troops" : focusPush > 0 ? "focus_push" : "cap_waste";
      const score = incoming / Math.max(1, teammateTroops) * 3 + (1 - teammateTroops / Math.max(1, myTroops)) + (reason === "under_attack" ? 2 : 0) + (reason === "focus_push" ? 1 : 0);
      return {
        id: player.id,
        name: player.name || player.displayName || player.id,
        troops,
        reason,
        incoming,
        teammateTroops,
        ownReserveAfterRatio,
        score
      };
    }
    function logCandidates(candidates) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (!candidates.length || now - lastCandidateLogAt < 5e3) return;
      lastCandidateLogAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record(
        "auto_team_support_candidate",
        candidates.slice(0, 3).map((candidate) => ({
          id: candidate.id,
          name: candidate.name,
          troops: Math.round(candidate.troops),
          reason: candidate.reason,
          incoming: Math.round(candidate.incoming || 0),
          teammateTroops: Math.round(candidate.teammateTroops || 0),
          ownReserveAfterRatio: candidate.ownReserveAfterRatio
        }))
      );
    }
    function logSkip(reason, extra = {}) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (now - lastSkipAt < 5e3) return;
      lastSkipAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record("auto_team_support_skipped", { reason, ...extra });
    }
    function setStatus(state, reason, extra = {}) {
      status = { state, reason, ...extra };
    }
    function countHostileAiNeighbours(neighbourIDs) {
      if (!neighbourIDs || typeof neighbourIDs.forEach !== "function") return 0;
      const myState = gameState.getMyState();
      let count = 0;
      neighbourIDs.forEach((id) => {
        const player = gameState.state.playerStates.get(id);
        if (!player || !player.isAlive || player.id === myState?.id) return;
        if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(player.id)) return;
        if (isAllied4(myState, player)) return;
        if (String(player.playerType || "").toUpperCase() !== "HUMAN") count += 1;
      });
      return count;
    }
    function isAllied4(myState, target) {
      const alliances = Array.isArray(myState?.alliances) ? myState.alliances : [];
      return alliances.some((alliance) => alliance && alliance.other === target.id);
    }
  }

  // src/page/automation/quick-chat.js
  var SOS_KEY = "help.troops";
  var QUICK_CHAT_COOLDOWN_MS = 1e4;
  var MIN_INCOMING_FOR_SOS = 5e3;
  function createQuickChatAutomation({ gameState, OrigWS, origWsSend, settings, logger, roundLogger, teamDetection }) {
    let lastSentAt = 0;
    let lastSkipAt = 0;
    let status = { state: "idle", reason: "not_started" };
    return {
      reset() {
        lastSentAt = 0;
        lastSkipAt = 0;
        status = { state: "idle", reason: "reset" };
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      observeIntent(intent) {
        if (!intent || intent.type !== "quick_chat") return;
        roundLogger?.record("quick_chat_observed", summarizeQuickChat(intent));
      },
      runAutoSos(context = {}) {
        const state = gameState.state;
        if (!settings.get("autoSosQuickChat")) return setStatus("idle", "disabled");
        if (!teamDetection?.isTeamMode) return setStatus("idle", "not_team_mode");
        if (!OrigWS) return setStatus("idle", "websocket_unavailable");
        if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN || !state.gameStarted || !state.myPlayerID) {
          return setStatus("idle", "not_ready");
        }
        if (!origWsSend) return setStatus("idle", "socket_unavailable");
        const now = performance.now();
        if (now - lastSentAt < QUICK_CHAT_COOLDOWN_MS) {
          return setStatus("cooldown", "cooldown", { cooldownMs: Math.round(QUICK_CHAT_COOLDOWN_MS - (now - lastSentAt)) });
        }
        const myState = gameState.getMyState();
        if (!myState || !myState.isAlive) return setStatus("idle", "not_alive");
        const incoming = incomingAttackTroops(gameState, myState);
        const threshold = Math.max(MIN_INCOMING_FOR_SOS, finiteOrZero(myState.troops) * 0.2);
        if (incoming < threshold) {
          logSkip("no_pressure", { incoming, threshold });
          return setStatus("idle", "no_pressure", { incoming });
        }
        const recipient = chooseRecipient();
        if (!recipient) {
          logSkip("no_recipient", { incoming });
          return setStatus("blocked", "no_recipient", { incoming });
        }
        const supportState = context.teamSupportStatus;
        if (supportState?.state === "sent" && incoming < threshold * 1.8) {
          logSkip("team_support_sent", { incoming, recipient: recipient.id });
          return setStatus("idle", "team_support_sent", { incoming });
        }
        const intent = { type: "quick_chat", recipient: recipient.id, quickChatKey: SOS_KEY };
        origWsSend.call(state.gameSocket, JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent }));
        lastSentAt = now;
        logger.info(\`Auto-SOS quick chat \${SOS_KEY} to \${recipient.name || recipient.id}\`);
        roundLogger?.record("quick_chat_sent", {
          quickChatKey: SOS_KEY,
          recipient: recipient.id,
          recipientName: recipient.name || recipient.id,
          incoming
        });
        return setStatus("sent", "sent_help_troops", { recipientName: recipient.name || recipient.id, incoming });
      }
    };
    function chooseRecipient() {
      const myState = gameState.getMyState();
      return teamDetection.getMyTeamMembers().map((member) => gameState.state.playerStates.get(member.id)).filter((player) => player && player.id !== myState?.id && player.isAlive).sort((a, b) => finiteOrZero(b.troops) - finiteOrZero(a.troops))[0];
    }
    function logSkip(reason, extra = {}) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (now - lastSkipAt < 5e3) return;
      lastSkipAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record("quick_chat_skipped", { reason, quickChatKey: SOS_KEY, ...extra });
    }
    function setStatus(state, reason, extra = {}) {
      status = { state, reason, ...extra };
    }
  }
  function summarizeQuickChat(intent) {
    return {
      quickChatKey: intent.quickChatKey || null,
      recipient: intent.recipient ?? null,
      target: intent.target ?? null,
      keys: Object.keys(intent)
    };
  }

  // src/page/automation/assist-actions.js
  function createAssistActions({ gameState, OrigWS, origWsSend, settings, logger, roundLogger, teamDetection }) {
    return {
      sendManualAssistAttack(target) {
        const gate = validateAttackTarget({ gameState, OrigWS, origWsSend, settings, target, allowHuman: true, teamDetection });
        if (!gate.ok) {
          roundLogger?.record("autopilot_skip", { action: "manual_assist_attack", reason: gate.reason });
          return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason };
        }
        const troops = Math.max(1, Math.floor(target.suggestedTroops));
        origWsSend.call(
          gameState.state.gameSocket,
          JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent: { type: "attack", targetID: target.id, troops } })
        );
        logger.info(\`Manual assist attack \${target.name || target.id}: \${troops} troops\`);
        roundLogger?.record("manual_assist_attack_sent", {
          targetID: target.id,
          targetName: target.name || target.id,
          troops,
          suggestedPercent: target.suggestedPercent,
          reserveAfterRatio: target.reserveAfterRatio
        });
        return { ok: true };
      }
    };
  }
  function validateAttackTarget({ gameState, OrigWS, origWsSend, settings, target, allowHuman = false, teamDetection }) {
    const state = gameState.state;
    const myState = gameState.getMyState();
    if (!target) return { ok: false, reason: "no_target" };
    if (!OrigWS) return { ok: false, reason: "websocket_unavailable" };
    if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN || !origWsSend) return { ok: false, reason: "socket_closed" };
    if (!state.gameStarted || !state.myPlayerID || !myState || !myState.isAlive) return { ok: false, reason: "not_alive_or_not_started" };
    if (target.isHuman && !allowHuman) return { ok: false, reason: "human_blocked" };
    if (target.status !== "target" && target.status !== "farm") return { ok: false, reason: \`status_\${target.status || "unknown"}\` };
    const player = state.playerStates.get(target.id);
    if (!player || !player.isAlive) return { ok: false, reason: "target_not_alive" };
    if (isAllied3(myState, player)) return { ok: false, reason: "target_allied" };
    if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(player.id)) return { ok: false, reason: "target_team" };
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(target.suggestedTroops)) || Number(target.suggestedTroops) <= 0) {
      return { ok: false, reason: "no_troops" };
    }
    const reserveAfter = Number(target.reserveAfterRatio);
    const reserveLimit = Number.isFinite(Number(target.appliedReserveRatio)) ? Number(target.appliedReserveRatio) : Number(settings.get("autoFarmReserveRatio")) || 0.55;
    if (!Number.isFinite(reserveAfter) || reserveAfter < reserveLimit) return { ok: false, reason: "reserve_limit" };
    return { ok: true };
  }
  function isAllied3(myState, target) {
    const alliances = Array.isArray(myState.alliances) ? myState.alliances : [];
    return alliances.some((alliance) => alliance && alliance.other === target.id);
  }

  // src/page/automation/autopilot-controller.js
  function createAutopilotController({ settings, gameState, autoSpawn, landAcquisition, autoAlliance, autoEco, autoDefense, roundLogger }) {
    let lastStateLogAt = 0;
    let lastSkipLogAt = 0;
    let status = { active: false, farmPaused: false, reason: null };
    return {
      reset() {
        lastStateLogAt = 0;
        lastSkipLogAt = 0;
        status = { active: false, farmPaused: false, reason: null };
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      run({ topSpot, farmRecommendations, buyRecommendations, expansion, threats, neighbourIDs, troopInfo, nukeThreat }) {
        if (!settings.get("autopilot")) {
          status = { active: false, farmPaused: false, reason: "disabled" };
          return { active: false };
        }
        const state = gameState.state;
        const myState = gameState.getMyState();
        if (!state.gameStarted || !myState || !myState.isAlive) {
          logSkip("not_ready");
          status = { active: true, farmPaused: false, reason: "not_ready" };
          return { active: true, spawnHandled: false, landHandled: false, allianceHandled: false, ecoHandled: false, defenseHandled: false };
        }
        logState({ expansion, threats });
        let spawnHandled = false;
        if (topSpot) {
          autoSpawn?.send(topSpot);
          spawnHandled = true;
        }
        const defenseStatus = autoDefense?.run({ force: true, nukeThreat, threats, neighbourIDs }) || null;
        if (defenseStatus?.emergency) {
          logSkip("defense_emergency");
          status = { active: true, farmPaused: true, reason: "defense_emergency" };
          runEco(buyRecommendations);
          return { active: true, spawnHandled, landHandled: true, allianceHandled: false, ecoHandled: true, defenseHandled: true };
        }
        const humanPressure = countHumanPressure(farmRecommendations);
        const strongThreat = isNeighbourThreatStrong(threats, neighbourIDs);
        const shouldPauseFarm = humanPressure >= 2 || strongThreat || expansion && expansion.level === "stop";
        landAcquisition?.run({ farmRecommendations, expansion, troopInfo }, { force: true, skipFarm: shouldPauseFarm });
        runEco(buyRecommendations);
        if (shouldPauseFarm) {
          const pauseReason = strongThreat ? "strong_threat" : humanPressure >= 2 ? "human_pressure" : expansion.level;
          logSkip(pauseReason);
          status = { active: true, farmPaused: true, reason: pauseReason };
          return { active: true, spawnHandled, landHandled: true, allianceHandled: false, ecoHandled: true, defenseHandled: true };
        }
        status = { active: true, farmPaused: false, reason: "farming" };
        let allianceHandled = false;
        if (settings.get("autoAlliance")) {
          autoAlliance?.run({ threats, neighbourIDs });
          allianceHandled = true;
        }
        return { active: true, spawnHandled, landHandled: true, allianceHandled, ecoHandled: true, defenseHandled: true };
      }
    };
    function runEco(buyRecommendations) {
      if (settings.get("autoEco")) autoEco?.run({ buyRecommendations }, { force: true });
    }
    function logSkip(reason) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (now - lastSkipLogAt < 3e3) return;
      lastSkipLogAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record("autopilot_skip", { reason });
    }
    function logState({ expansion, threats }) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (now - lastStateLogAt < 5e3) return;
      lastStateLogAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record("autopilot_state", {
        expansion: expansion ? expansion.level : "unknown",
        topThreat: threats && threats[0] ? { name: threats[0].name, level: threats[0].level, score: threats[0].score } : null
      });
    }
  }
  function countHumanPressure(recommendations) {
    return (recommendations || []).filter((rec) => rec.isHuman && rec.status === "danger").length;
  }
  function isNeighbourThreatStrong(threats, neighbourIDs) {
    if (!Array.isArray(threats) || !neighbourIDs || neighbourIDs.size === 0) return false;
    return threats.some(
      (threat) => (threat.level === "Dangerous" || threat.level === "Critical") && neighbourIDs.has(threat.id)
    );
  }

  // src/page/automation/smart-attack.js
  function createSmartAttackModifier({ settings, gameState, logger, roundLogger, teamDetection, getTroopEconomy }) {
    return function maybeModifyAttack(data) {
      if (!settings.get("smartAttack") || typeof data !== "string") return data;
      try {
        const message = JSON.parse(data);
        if (!message || message.type !== "intent" || !message.intent) return data;
        const intent = message.intent;
        if (intent.type !== "attack" || !intent.troops || intent.troops <= 0 || !intent.targetID) return data;
        const targetState = resolveTarget(gameState, intent.targetID);
        if (targetState && teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(targetState.id)) return data;
        const economy = getTroopEconomy?.();
        const safe = economy ? economy.safeSpendableTroops : null;
        if (!Number.isFinite(safe)) return data;
        if (safe < 1) return data;
        const originalTroops = intent.troops;
        if (originalTroops <= safe) return data;
        const newTroops = Math.max(1, Math.floor(safe));
        if (newTroops === originalTroops) return data;
        intent.troops = newTroops;
        logger.info(
          \`Smart attack cap \${targetState?.name || intent.targetID}: \${originalTroops} -> \${newTroops} (safe \${Math.floor(safe)})\`
        );
        roundLogger?.record("smart_attack_modified", {
          reason: "growth_cap",
          targetID: intent.targetID,
          targetName: targetState?.name || intent.targetID,
          safeSpendable: Math.floor(safe),
          originalTroops,
          newTroops
        });
        return JSON.stringify(message);
      } catch (_) {
        return data;
      }
    };
  }
  function resolveTarget(gameState, targetID) {
    const direct = gameState.state.playerStates.get(targetID);
    if (direct) return direct;
    let match = null;
    gameState.state.playerStates.forEach((player) => {
      if (!match && player && player.smallID === targetID) match = player;
    });
    return match;
  }

  // src/page/automation/auto-alliance.js
  var ALLIANCE_RENEW_THRESHOLD = 50;
  var ALLIANCE_RETRY_COOLDOWN_TICKS = 300;
  var MAX_ALLIANCE_ATTEMPTS = 2;
  var MIN_ALLY_VALUE_SCORE = 0.55;
  var POLICY_BLACKLIST = "blacklist";
  var POLICY_WHITELIST = "whitelist";
  function createAutoAlliance({ gameState, neighbourFetcher, OrigWS, origWsSend, logger, roundLogger, teamDetection }) {
    const allianceRequestCooldowns = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const policy = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const safeBrokenThisRound = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    let lastSkippedNeighbour = null;
    const policyApi = {
      // 'blacklist' | 'whitelist' | 'clear'
      setAlliancePolicy(playerID, mode) {
        if (playerID == null) return;
        if (mode === "clear" || mode == null) {
          policy.delete(playerID);
          return;
        }
        if (mode !== POLICY_BLACKLIST && mode !== POLICY_WHITELIST) return;
        policy.set(playerID, mode);
        if (mode === POLICY_BLACKLIST) allianceRequestCooldowns.delete(playerID);
      },
      // returns "blacklist" | "whitelist" | null
      getAlliancePolicy(playerID) {
        return policy.get(playerID) || null;
      }
    };
    return {
      ...policyApi,
      get lastSkippedNeighbour() {
        return lastSkippedNeighbour;
      },
      // True if at least one player is currently favourited (whitelist). page-main uses
      // this to keep pursuing favourites even when the global autoAlliance toggle is off.
      hasWhitelist() {
        for (const mode of policy.values()) {
          if (mode === POLICY_WHITELIST) return true;
        }
        return false;
      },
      // True if ANY per-player policy (whitelist or blacklist) is set. page-main uses
      // this to keep the alliance loop running (favourite pursuit + blacklist safe-break)
      // even when the global autoAlliance toggle is off.
      hasPolicy() {
        return policy.size > 0;
      },
      // Advice-only: which current allies are Traitors (breaking with a traitor is
      // penalty-free). Never auto-breaks \u2014 surfaced by the panel as a hint.
      getTraitorBreakHints() {
        return findTraitorAllies(gameState);
      },
      reset() {
        allianceRequestCooldowns.clear();
        policy.clear();
        safeBrokenThisRound.clear();
        lastSkippedNeighbour = null;
      },
      // context (optional): { threats, neighbourIDs, whitelistOnly }
      //   threats \u2014 Map<playerID, { score, level, adjacent, ... }> from threat-model.
      //   neighbourIDs \u2014 Set<playerID> of adjacent players.
      //   whitelistOnly \u2014 when true, ONLY the favourite/whitelist pursuit runs; the
      //     threat-based candidate targeting is skipped. page-main uses this so a set
      //     favourite keeps requesting alliances even when the global autoAlliance
      //     automation is off (the right-click "\u2605 Favorit" still works standalone).
      // Called both from page-main (with context) and the autopilot controller
      // (no args) \u2014 degrades gracefully to a tiles-based fallback without context.
      run(context = {}) {
        const state = gameState.state;
        if (!OrigWS) return;
        if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN || !state.gameStarted || !state.myPlayerID) return;
        const myState = gameState.getMyState();
        if (!myState || !myState.isAlive) return;
        const whitelistOnly = context.whitelistOnly === true;
        const threats = context.threats instanceof Map ? context.threats : null;
        const neighbourIDs = context.neighbourIDs instanceof Set ? context.neighbourIDs : null;
        neighbourFetcher.refresh();
        const cachedNeighbours = neighbourFetcher.cachedNeighbourIDs;
        if (!whitelistOnly && !cachedNeighbours && !threats) return;
        const existingAlliances = {};
        (myState.alliances || []).forEach((alliance) => {
          existingAlliances[alliance.other] = alliance;
        });
        const pendingOutgoing = {};
        (myState.outgoingAllianceRequests || []).forEach((playerID) => {
          pendingOutgoing[playerID] = true;
        });
        policy.forEach((mode, playerID) => {
          if (mode !== POLICY_BLACKLIST) return;
          if (safeBrokenThisRound.has(playerID)) return;
          if (!existingAlliances[playerID]) return;
          if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(playerID)) return;
          if (!isPenaltyFreeBreakTarget(playerID)) return;
          const p = state.playerStates.get(playerID);
          sendAllianceIntent(state, OrigWS, origWsSend, "breakAlliance", playerID, p?.name, logger, roundLogger);
          safeBrokenThisRound.add(playerID);
        });
        policy.forEach((mode, playerID) => {
          if (mode !== POLICY_WHITELIST) return;
          if (playerID === state.myPlayerID) return;
          if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(playerID)) return;
          const player = state.playerStates.get(playerID);
          if (!player || !player.isAlive || !isHumanPlayerState(player)) return;
          pursueAlliance(player);
        });
        if (!whitelistOnly) {
          const candidates = collectCandidates(state, myState, gameState, teamDetection, cachedNeighbours, threats, neighbourIDs, policy);
          candidates.sort((a, b) => b.allyValue - a.allyValue);
          const skipped = candidates.length ? candidates[candidates.length - 1] : null;
          const worthPursuing = candidates.filter((c) => c.allyValue >= MIN_ALLY_VALUE_SCORE);
          lastSkippedNeighbour = skipped && skipped.allyValue < MIN_ALLY_VALUE_SCORE ? skipped.player.name || skipped.player.id : null;
          worthPursuing.forEach((candidate) => pursueAlliance(candidate.player));
        }
        function pursueAlliance(target) {
          if (policy.get(target.id) === POLICY_BLACKLIST) return;
          const existing = existingAlliances[target.id];
          if (existing) {
            if (existing.expiresAt && existing.expiresAt - state.currentTick < ALLIANCE_RENEW_THRESHOLD && !existing.hasExtensionRequest) {
              sendAllianceIntent(state, OrigWS, origWsSend, "allianceExtension", target.id, target.name, logger, roundLogger);
            }
            return;
          }
          if (pendingOutgoing[target.id]) return;
          const isWhitelisted = policy.get(target.id) === POLICY_WHITELIST;
          const record = allianceRequestCooldowns.get(target.id);
          if (record && !isWhitelisted) {
            if (record.attempts >= MAX_ALLIANCE_ATTEMPTS) return;
          }
          if (record && state.currentTick - record.lastTick < ALLIANCE_RETRY_COOLDOWN_TICKS) return;
          sendAllianceIntent(state, OrigWS, origWsSend, "allianceRequest", target.id, target.name, logger, roundLogger);
          allianceRequestCooldowns.set(target.id, {
            lastTick: state.currentTick,
            attempts: (record?.attempts || 0) + 1
          });
        }
      }
    };
  }
  function collectCandidates(state, myState, gameState, teamDetection, cachedNeighbours, threats, neighbourIDs, policy) {
    const attacking = getAttackingPlayerIDs(state, myState);
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const candidates = [];
    const consider = (playerID) => {
      if (playerID == null || seen.has(playerID)) return;
      seen.add(playerID);
      if (playerID === state.myPlayerID || attacking[playerID]) return;
      if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(playerID)) return;
      if (policy.get(playerID) === POLICY_BLACKLIST) return;
      const player = state.playerStates.get(playerID);
      if (!player || !player.isAlive || !isHumanPlayerState(player)) return;
      candidates.push({ id: playerID, player, allyValue: allyValueFor(player, threats) });
    };
    if (cachedNeighbours) cachedNeighbours.forEach(consider);
    if (neighbourIDs) neighbourIDs.forEach(consider);
    if (threats) threats.forEach((_threat, playerID) => consider(playerID));
    return candidates;
  }
  function allyValueFor(player, threats) {
    if (threats) {
      const threat = threats.get(player.id);
      if (threat) {
        return Number(threat.score) || 0;
      }
      return 0;
    }
    return (player.tilesOwned || 0) * 0.55 * 1e-4 + (player.troops || 0) * 1e-5 + 1e-4;
  }
  function recommendAlliances(gameState, teamDetection, threats = null) {
    const myState = gameState.getMyState();
    if (!myState) return [];
    const threatMap = threats instanceof Map ? threats : null;
    const recommendations = [];
    gameState.state.playerStates.forEach((player) => {
      if (!player || !player.isAlive || player.id === myState.id || !isHumanPlayerState(player)) return;
      if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate(player.id)) return;
      const threat = threatMap ? threatMap.get(player.id) : null;
      const score = threat ? Number(threat.score) || 0 : (player.tilesOwned || 0) * 0.55 + (player.troops || 0) * 1e-5;
      const label = threat ? threat.adjacent ? t("alliance.label.highValue") : t("alliance.label.candidate") : score > (myState.tilesOwned || 0) * 0.55 ? t("alliance.label.candidate") : t("alliance.label.possibleTarget");
      recommendations.push({ id: player.id, name: player.name || player.id, score, label });
    });
    return recommendations.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 3);
  }
  function findTraitorAllies(gameState) {
    const myState = gameState.getMyState();
    const allyIDs = (myState?.alliances || []).map((a) => a && a.other).filter((id) => id != null);
    if (!allyIDs.length) return [];
    try {
      const gameView = getGameView();
      if (!gameView || typeof gameView.playerViews !== "function") return [];
      const views = gameView.playerViews();
      if (!views || typeof views.forEach !== "function") return [];
      const allySet = new Set(allyIDs);
      const hints = [];
      views.forEach((p) => {
        try {
          if (!p) return;
          const pID = typeof p.id === "function" ? p.id() : p.id;
          if (pID == null || !allySet.has(pID)) return;
          if (!readIsTraitor(p)) return;
          const name = typeof p.name === "function" ? p.name() : p.name || pID;
          hints.push({ id: pID, name });
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
      return hints;
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }
  function readIsTraitor(p) {
    try {
      if (typeof p.isTraitor === "function") return !!p.isTraitor();
      if (typeof p.isTraitor === "boolean") return p.isTraitor;
      if (p.state && typeof p.state.isTraitor !== "undefined") return !!p.state.isTraitor;
    } catch (_) {
    }
    return false;
  }
  function readIsDisconnected(p) {
    try {
      if (typeof p.isDisconnected === "function") return !!p.isDisconnected();
      if (typeof p.isDisconnected === "boolean") return p.isDisconnected;
      if (p.state && typeof p.state.isDisconnected !== "undefined") return !!p.state.isDisconnected;
    } catch (_) {
    }
    return false;
  }
  function isPenaltyFreeBreakTarget(playerID) {
    try {
      const gameView = getGameView();
      if (!gameView) return false;
      let p = null;
      if (typeof gameView.hasPlayer === "function" && typeof gameView.player === "function") {
        if (!gameView.hasPlayer(playerID)) return false;
        p = gameView.player(playerID);
      } else if (typeof gameView.playerViews === "function") {
        const views = gameView.playerViews();
        if (views && typeof views.forEach === "function") {
          views.forEach((v) => {
            try {
              const id = typeof v.id === "function" ? v.id() : v.id;
              if (id === playerID) p = v;
            } catch (_) {
            }
          });
        }
      }
      if (!p) return false;
      return readIsTraitor(p) || readIsDisconnected(p);
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  function getAttackingPlayerIDs(state, myState) {
    const smallIDtoPlayerID = {};
    state.playerStates.forEach((player) => {
      if (player.smallID != null) smallIDtoPlayerID[player.smallID] = player.id;
    });
    const attacking = {};
    (myState.outgoingAttacks || []).forEach((attack) => {
      if (!attack || attack.retreating) return;
      const targetID = smallIDtoPlayerID[attack.targetID];
      if (targetID) attacking[targetID] = true;
    });
    return attacking;
  }
  function sendAllianceIntent(state, OrigWS, origWsSend, intentType, recipientID, recipientName, logger, roundLogger) {
    if (!OrigWS) return;
    if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN || !origWsSend) return;
    origWsSend.call(
      state.gameSocket,
      JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent: { type: intentType, recipient: recipientID } })
    );
    logger.info(\`\${intentType} sent to \${recipientName || recipientID}\`);
    roundLogger?.record("auto_alliance_sent", { intentType, recipientID, recipientName: recipientName || recipientID });
  }
  function isHumanPlayerState(state) {
    return !!state && String(state.playerType || "").toUpperCase() === "HUMAN";
  }

  // src/page/automation/attack-ratio-hotkey.js
  var DEFAULT_ATTACK_RATIO_BINDINGS = Object.freeze({
    Digit1: 10,
    Digit2: 20,
    Digit3: 30,
    Digit4: 40,
    Digit5: 50,
    Digit6: 60,
    Digit7: 70,
    Digit8: 80,
    Digit9: 90,
    Digit0: 100
  });
  var REQUIRED_MODIFIER = "shiftKey";
  function createAttackRatioHotkey({ settings, logger, roundLogger }) {
    let keydownHandler = null;
    let installed2 = false;
    let status = { state: "idle", reason: "not_started" };
    return {
      reset() {
        status = { state: "idle", reason: "reset" };
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      // Im Page-Context einmal aufrufen, sobald das Dokument bereit ist. Der Listener entscheidet
      // bei jedem Tastendruck selbst, ob das Feature aktiv ist (Settings live), und greift nur dann zu.
      install() {
        if (installed2) return;
        installed2 = true;
        keydownHandler = (event) => handleKeydown(event);
        window.addEventListener("keydown", keydownHandler, true);
      },
      uninstall() {
        if (!installed2) return;
        installed2 = false;
        if (keydownHandler) window.removeEventListener("keydown", keydownHandler, true);
        keydownHandler = null;
      },
      // Direkt aufrufbar (z. B. aus dem HUD): setzt das Verh\xE4ltnis auf integer-Prozent 1..100.
      setRatioPercent(pct) {
        return applyAttackRatio(pct);
      }
    };
    function handleKeydown(event) {
      if (!settings.get("attackRatioHotkeyEnabled")) return;
      if (!event || event.repeat) return;
      if (event[REQUIRED_MODIFIER] !== true) return;
      if (event.ctrlKey || event.altKey || event.metaKey) return;
      if (isEditableTarget(event.target)) return;
      const bindings = settings.get("attackRatioHotkeyBindings") || DEFAULT_ATTACK_RATIO_BINDINGS;
      const pct = bindings[event.code];
      if (!Number.isFinite(pct)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const applied = applyAttackRatio(pct);
      if (applied) {
        setStatus("applied", "hotkey", { pct, code: event.code });
        logger.info(\`Attack ratio set to \${clampPercent(pct)}% via \${event.code}\`);
        roundLogger?.record("attack_ratio_hotkey", { pct: clampPercent(pct), code: event.code });
      } else {
        setStatus("blocked", "no_control_panel", { pct, code: event.code });
      }
    }
    function setStatus(state, reason, extra = {}) {
      status = { state, reason, ...extra };
      return status;
    }
  }
  function applyAttackRatio(pctRaw) {
    const pct = clampPercent(pctRaw);
    const ratio = pct / 100;
    let touched = false;
    try {
      const sliders = document.querySelectorAll('control-panel input[type="range"]');
      sliders.forEach((slider) => {
        try {
          slider.value = String(pct);
          slider.valueAsNumber = pct;
          slider.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent("input", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
          slider.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
          touched = true;
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
    } catch (_) {
    }
    if (!touched) {
      try {
        const panels = document.querySelectorAll("control-panel");
        panels.forEach((panel) => {
          try {
            if (typeof panel.onAttackRatioChange === "function") {
              panel.onAttackRatioChange(ratio);
              touched = true;
              return;
            }
            if ("attackRatio" in panel) {
              panel.attackRatio = ratio;
              if (typeof panel.requestUpdate === "function") panel.requestUpdate();
              touched = true;
            }
          } catch (_) {
          }
        });
      } catch (_) {
      }
    }
    return touched;
  }
  function clampPercent(pct) {
    const n = Math.round(Number(pct));
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 1;
    return Math.min(100, Math.max(1, n));
  }
  function isEditableTarget(target) {
    if (!target || typeof target.tagName !== "string") return false;
    const tag = target.tagName.toUpperCase();
    if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA" || tag === "SELECT") return true;
    if (target.isContentEditable) return true;
    return false;
  }

  // src/page/automation/alliance-auto-accept.js
  var AUTO_ACCEPT_SEND_VERIFIED = true;
  var MIN_ACCEPT_THREAT_SCORE = 0.55;
  function createAllianceAutoAccept({
    gameState,
    OrigWS,
    origWsSend,
    settings,
    logger,
    roundLogger,
    teamDetection,
    getAlliancePolicy = null,
    hasWhitelist = null
  }) {
    const acceptedThisRound = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    let lastLogAt = 0;
    let status = { state: "idle", reason: "not_started" };
    let currentThreats = null;
    const policyFor = (id) => {
      if (typeof getAlliancePolicy !== "function") return null;
      try {
        return getAlliancePolicy(id);
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    };
    return {
      reset() {
        acceptedThisRound.clear();
        lastLogAt = 0;
        currentThreats = null;
        status = { state: "idle", reason: "reset" };
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      // context (optional): { threats } \u2014 threat-Map aus threat-model f\xFCr die Heuristik.
      run(context = {}) {
        const settingOn = !!settings.get("autoAlliance");
        const hasFavourite = typeof hasWhitelist === "function" ? !!hasWhitelist() : false;
        if (!settingOn && !hasFavourite) return setStatus("idle", "disabled");
        const whitelistOnly = !settingOn;
        currentThreats = context.threats instanceof Map ? context.threats : null;
        const state = gameState.state;
        if (!OrigWS) return setStatus("idle", "websocket_unavailable");
        if (!state.gameSocket || state.gameSocket.readyState !== OrigWS.OPEN || !state.gameStarted || !state.myPlayerID) {
          return setStatus("idle", "not_ready");
        }
        if (!origWsSend) return setStatus("idle", "socket_unavailable");
        const requesters = detectIncomingRequests();
        if (!requesters) return setStatus("blocked", "no_game_view");
        if (!requesters.length) return setStatus("idle", "no_requests");
        const toAccept = requesters.filter((r) => shouldAccept(r.id, whitelistOnly));
        const ids = requesters.map((r) => r.id);
        logDetected(ids, toAccept.map((r) => r.id));
        if (!AUTO_ACCEPT_SEND_VERIFIED) {
          return setStatus("gated", "send_unverified", { count: ids.length, eligible: toAccept.length });
        }
        let accepted = 0;
        toAccept.forEach((requester) => {
          if (acceptedThisRound.has(requester.id)) return;
          origWsSend.call(
            state.gameSocket,
            JSON.stringify({ type: "intent", intent: { type: "allianceRequest", recipient: requester.id } })
          );
          acceptedThisRound.add(requester.id);
          accepted += 1;
          logger.info(\`Auto-accepted alliance from \${requester.id}\`);
          roundLogger?.record("alliance_auto_accepted", { recipientID: requester.id });
        });
        return setStatus(accepted ? "sent" : "idle", accepted ? "accepted" : "already_accepted", {
          accepted,
          count: ids.length
        });
      }
    };
    function shouldAccept(id, whitelistOnly) {
      const policy = policyFor(id);
      if (policy === "blacklist") return false;
      if (policy === "whitelist") return true;
      if (whitelistOnly) return false;
      if (!currentThreats) return true;
      const threat = currentThreats.get(id);
      if (!threat) return false;
      return (Number(threat.score) || 0) >= MIN_ACCEPT_THREAT_SCORE;
    }
    function detectIncomingRequests() {
      try {
        const gameView = getGameView();
        if (!gameView || typeof gameView.myPlayer !== "function" || typeof gameView.playerViews !== "function") return null;
        const myPlayer = gameView.myPlayer();
        if (!myPlayer) return null;
        const myID = typeof myPlayer.id === "function" ? myPlayer.id() : myPlayer.id;
        if (myID == null) return null;
        const canCheckAllied = typeof myPlayer.isAlliedWith === "function";
        const out = [];
        const views = gameView.playerViews();
        if (!views || typeof views.forEach !== "function") return [];
        views.forEach((p) => {
          try {
            if (!p) return;
            const pID = typeof p.id === "function" ? p.id() : p.id;
            if (pID == null || pID === myID) return;
            if (teamDetection?.isMyTeammate?.(pID)) return;
            const outgoing = p.state && p.state.outgoingAllianceRequests || [];
            if (!Array.isArray(outgoing) || !outgoing.includes(myID)) return;
            if (canCheckAllied) {
              try {
                if (myPlayer.isAlliedWith(p)) return;
              } catch (_) {
              }
            }
            out.push({ id: pID });
          } catch (_) {
          }
        });
        return out;
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    function logDetected(ids, eligibleIds) {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (now - lastLogAt < 5e3) return;
      lastLogAt = now;
      roundLogger?.record("alliance_request_detected", { count: ids.length, ids, eligible: eligibleIds });
    }
    function setStatus(state, reason, extra = {}) {
      status = { state, reason, ...extra };
      return status;
    }
  }

  // src/shared/html.js
  function escapeHtml(value) {
    return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (char) => {
      switch (char) {
        case "&":
          return "&amp;";
        case "<":
          return "&lt;";
        case ">":
          return "&gt;";
        case '"':
          return "&quot;";
        case "'":
          return "&#39;";
        default:
          return char;
      }
    });
  }

  // src/shared/troops.js
  function formatTroopCount(troops) {
    const num = Number(troops) / 10;
    if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return "0";
    if (num >= 1e7) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 1e5) / 10).toFixed(1)}M\`;
    if (num >= 1e6) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 1e4) / 100).toFixed(2)}M\`;
    if (num >= 1e5) return \`\${Math.floor(num / 1e3)}K\`;
    if (num >= 1e4) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 100) / 10).toFixed(1)}K\`;
    if (num >= 1e3) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 10) / 100).toFixed(2)}K\`;
    return Math.floor(num).toString();
  }

  // src/page/ui/ui-copy.js
  function reasonText(reason) {
    const value = String(reason || "");
    if (!value) return "";
    const key = \`reason.\${value}\`;
    const resolved = t(key);
    if (resolved !== key) return resolved;
    if (value.startsWith("status_")) return statusLabel(value.slice(7));
    return value.replace(/_/g, " ");
  }
  function statusText(status, fallback = "ready") {
    if (!status) return fallback;
    if (status.targetName) return status.targetName;
    if (status.lastAction) return status.lastAction;
    if (status.unit && status.target) return \`\${status.unit} -> \${status.target}\`;
    if (status.unit) return status.unit;
    if (status.reason) return reasonText(status.reason);
    if (status.state) {
      const key = \`state.\${status.state}\`;
      const resolved = t(key);
      return resolved !== key ? resolved : status.state;
    }
    return fallback;
  }
  function statusLabel(status) {
    const value = String(status || "");
    const key = \`status.\${value}\`;
    const resolved = t(key);
    if (resolved !== key) return resolved;
    return reasonText(value);
  }
  function economyLabel(state) {
    if (!state) return "unknown";
    const key = \`eco.\${state}\`;
    const resolved = t(key);
    return resolved !== key ? resolved : state;
  }
  function settingLabel(key, fallback = key) {
    const tKey = \`toggle.\${key}\`;
    const resolved = t(tKey);
    return resolved !== tKey ? resolved : fallback;
  }
  function isSpawnRelevant(vm) {
    if (!vm?.topSpots?.length) return false;
    if (!vm.spawnPhase) return true;
    return Number(vm.spawnPhase.remainingTurns || 0) > 0;
  }
  function isEconomyRelevant(eco) {
    return ["CAP_WASTE", "CRITICAL", "PUSH", "RECOVER"].includes(eco?.state);
  }
  function isThreatRelevant(vm) {
    if (vm?.nukeThreatActive) return true;
    return (vm?.topThreats || []).some((threat) => threat.level === "Dangerous" || threat.level === "Critical");
  }
  function primaryAdvice(vm, status = {}) {
    const defense = status.autoDefense || vm?.automationStatus?.autoDefense;
    if (defense?.emergency) {
      return {
        tone: "warn",
        label: t("advice.defend.label"),
        text: statusText(defense, defense.topAttacker ? t("advice.attackFrom", { name: defense.topAttacker }) : t("advice.underAttack"))
      };
    }
    const warning = (vm?.enemyIntentWarnings || [])[0];
    if (warning) {
      const verb = warning.kind === "boat" ? t("advice.verb.landing") : t("advice.verb.attack");
      const size = warning.troops > 0 ? \` ~\${formatTroopCount(warning.troops)}\` : "";
      return { tone: "warn", label: t("advice.incoming.label"), text: \`\${warning.attackerName} \${verb}\${size}\` };
    }
    if (vm?.nukeThreatActive) {
      const nukeThreat = (vm.topThreats || []).find((threat) => threat.nukePotential >= 1 || threat.nukeSoon || threat.buildingNuke);
      return { tone: "warn", label: t("advice.nuke.label"), text: nukeThreat?.name || t("advice.nuke.fallback") };
    }
    const support = (vm?.teamSupportRecommendations || [])[0];
    if (support) return { tone: "ready", label: t("advice.team.label"), text: t("advice.team.text", { name: support.name }) };
    const assist = vm?.assistTarget;
    if (assist) return { tone: "ready", label: t("advice.target.label"), text: \`\${assist.name} \${assist.label}\` };
    const farm = (vm?.farmRecommendations || []).find((rec) => rec.status === "farm");
    if (farm) return { tone: "ready", label: t("advice.farm.label"), text: \`\${farm.name} \${farm.label}\` };
    const buy = (vm?.buyRecommendations || [])[0];
    if (buy) return { tone: buy.actionAvailable ? "ready" : "hint", label: t("advice.build.label"), text: buy.label };
    if (isSpawnRelevant(vm)) {
      return { tone: "ready", label: t("advice.spawn.label"), text: t("advice.spawn.text") };
    }
    if (isEconomyRelevant(vm?.troopEconomy)) {
      return { tone: vm.troopEconomy.state === "CRITICAL" ? "warn" : "hint", label: t("advice.eco.label"), text: economyLabel(vm.troopEconomy.state) };
    }
    return { tone: "ok", label: t("advice.status.label"), text: t("advice.status.text") };
  }

  // src/page/ui/gold-ranking.js
  function formatGold(value) {
    const num = finiteOrZero(value);
    const abs = Math.abs(num);
    if (abs >= 1e6) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 1e4) / 100).toFixed(2)}M\`;
    if (abs >= 1e4) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 100) / 10).toFixed(1)}k\`;
    if (abs >= 1e3) return \`\${(Math.floor(num / 10) / 100).toFixed(2)}k\`;
    return Math.round(num).toString();
  }
  function formatMps(mps) {
    if (mps == null || !Number.isFinite(mps)) return "\\u2014";
    const sign = mps >= 0 ? "+" : "-";
    return \`\${sign}\${formatGold(Math.abs(mps))}/s\`;
  }
  function buildGoldRanking(gameState, goldIntel, limit = 5) {
    const ranking = [];
    const myID = gameState.state.myPlayerID;
    gameState.state.playerStates.forEach((player) => {
      if (!player || player.id == null || !player.isAlive) return;
      const gold2 = finiteOrZero(typeof player.gold === "bigint" ? Number(player.gold) : player.gold);
      ranking.push({
        name: player.name || "?",
        gold: gold2,
        mps: goldIntel ? goldIntel.mps(player.id) : null,
        playerType: player.playerType,
        isMe: player.id === myID
      });
    });
    ranking.sort((a, b) => b.gold - a.gold);
    return ranking.slice(0, limit);
  }
  function renderGoldRankingHtml(ranking) {
    if (!ranking || !ranking.length) return "";
    let html = \`<div class="ofat-section-title">Gold</div>\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\`;
    html += ranking.map((entry) => {
      const name = entry.isMe ? \`\\u25B8 <b>\${escapeHtml(entry.name)}</b>\` : escapeHtml(entry.name);
      return \`\${name} \${escapeHtml(formatGold(entry.gold))} <span class="ofat-muted">(\${escapeHtml(formatMps(entry.mps))})</span>\`;
    }).join(" | ");
    html += \`</div>\`;
    return html;
  }

  // src/page/ui/advisor-panel.js
  var statsOpen = false;
  function createAdvisorPanel({ app, settings, heatmap, actions = {}, topPlayerLens = null }) {
    let panel = null;
    return {
      create() {
        removeElementById("ofat-panel");
        removeElementById("ofat-heatmap");
        installPanelStyles();
        panel = document.createElement("div");
        panel.id = "ofat-panel";
        panel.style.display = settings.get("showAdvisorPanel") ? "block" : "none";
        appendWhenReady(panel, "body");
        makeDraggable(panel, { storageKey: panel.id, settings });
      },
      setVisible(visible) {
        if (panel) panel.style.display = visible ? "block" : "none";
      },
      attachHeatmap(canvas) {
        appendWhenReady(canvas, "body");
      },
      render(viewModel) {
        if (!panel) return;
        panel.innerHTML = renderPanelHtml(app, settings, viewModel, topPlayerLens);
        panel.querySelectorAll("button[data-setting]").forEach((button) => {
          button.addEventListener("click", () => settings.set(button.dataset.setting, !settings.get(button.dataset.setting)));
        });
        const closeButton = document.getElementById("ofat-close");
        if (closeButton) closeButton.addEventListener("click", () => settings.set("showAdvisorPanel", false));
        const assistButton = document.getElementById("ofat-assist-attack");
        if (assistButton && actions.onManualAssistAttack) assistButton.addEventListener("click", actions.onManualAssistAttack);
        panel.querySelectorAll(".ofat-threat-clickable[data-threat-idx]").forEach((chip) => {
          chip.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const threat = (viewModel.topThreats || [])[Number(chip.dataset.threatIdx)];
            if (threat && threat.id != null) centerCameraOnPlayer(threat.id);
          });
        });
        const findMeButton = document.getElementById("ofat-find-me");
        if (findMeButton) findMeButton.addEventListener("click", () => centerCameraOnMe());
        panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ofat-spot-click]").forEach((row) => {
          row.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const spot = (viewModel.topSpots || [])[Number(row.dataset.ofatSpotClick)];
            if (spot && Number.isFinite(spot.x) && Number.isFinite(spot.y)) centerCameraOnTile(spot.x, spot.y);
          });
        });
        const statsToggle = panel.querySelector("[data-ofat-stats-toggle]");
        if (statsToggle) {
          statsToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {
            statsOpen = !statsOpen;
            const body = panel.querySelector(".ofat-stats-body");
            if (body) body.style.display = statsOpen ? "block" : "none";
            statsToggle.textContent = t(statsOpen ? "panel.stats.toggle.open" : "panel.stats.toggle.closed");
          });
        }
        topPlayerLens?.attach?.(panel);
        heatmap.render(viewModel.scores, viewModel.topSpots, viewModel.playerSpawns);
      }
    };
  }
  function renderPanelHtml(app, settings, vm, topPlayerLens) {
    const isExpert = settings.get("viewMode") === "expert";
    const skipped = vm.lastSkippedNeighbour ? \` | \${t("panel.alliance.skip", { name: escapeHtml(vm.lastSkippedNeighbour) })}\` : "";
    const spawnRelevant = isSpawnRelevant(vm);
    const threatRelevant = isThreatRelevant(vm);
    let html = "";
    html += \`<div class="ofat-head">\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-title">\${escapeHtml(app.name)}</div>\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.subtitle"))} | v\${escapeHtml(app.version)}</div>\`;
    html += \`</div>\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-body">\`;
    if (vm.expansion && vm.expansion.level !== "normal") html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">Expansion: <b>\${escapeHtml(expansionText(vm.expansion.level))}</b></div>\`;
    if (vm.troopEconomy && settings.get("showTroopEconomy") && isEconomyRelevant(vm.troopEconomy)) html += renderEconomyHtml(vm.troopEconomy);
    if (threatRelevant && vm.topThreats.length) {
      html += \`<div class="ofat-section-title ofat-section-title-row">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.threats.title"))}<button id="ofat-find-me" title="\${escapeHtml(t("panel.findme.title"))}">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.findme"))}</button></div>\`;
      html += \`<div class="ofat-muted ofat-threat-list">\${vm.topThreats.map((thr, i) => renderThreatSummary(thr, i)).join(" ")}</div>\`;
    }
    if (settings.get("showGoldRanking") && vm.goldRanking?.length) html += renderGoldRankingHtml(vm.goldRanking);
    if (vm.farmRecommendations && vm.farmRecommendations.length) {
      const farms = vm.farmRecommendations.filter((target) => target.status === "farm" || target.status === "mark").slice(0, 2);
      if (farms.length) {
        html += \`<div class="ofat-section-title">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.targets.title"))}</div>\`;
        html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${farms.map((target) => renderTargetSummary(target)).join(" | ")}</div>\`;
      }
      const humanTargets = vm.farmRecommendations.filter((target) => target.isHuman).slice(0, 3);
      if (humanTargets.length) {
        html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.targets.humans"))} \${humanTargets.map((target) => renderTargetSummary(target, localizedStatusLabel(target.status))).join(" | ")}</div>\`;
      }
    }
    if (vm.assistTarget) {
      html += \`<div class="ofat-action-row">\`;
      html += \`<span>\${escapeHtml(t("panel.assist.label"))} <b>\${escapeHtml(vm.assistTarget.name)}</b> \${escapeHtml(vm.assistTarget.label)}</span>\`;
      html += \`<button id="ofat-assist-attack">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.assist.button"))}</button>\`;
      html += \`</div>\`;
    }
    if (spawnRelevant) html += \`<div class="ofat-section-title">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.spawn.title"))}</div>\`;
    const spots = spawnRelevant ? vm.topSpots.slice(0, 3) : [];
    for (let i = 0; i < spots.length; i += 1) {
      const spot = spots[i];
      const scorePct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(spot.score * 100)));
      const expertTooltip = isExpert ? t("panel.spawn.tooltip.expert", { land: Math.floor(spot.landDensity * 100), plains: Math.floor(spot.plainsRatio * 100), nation: Math.floor(spot.nationScore * 100), dist: Math.floor(spot.playerDistScore * 100) }) : "";
      const titleAttr = expertTooltip ? \`\${escapeHtml(t("panel.spawn.camera.title", { num: i + 1 }))} | \${escapeHtml(expertTooltip)}\` : escapeHtml(t("panel.spawn.camera.title", { num: i + 1 }));
      html += \`<div class="ofat-row ofat-spawn-clickable" data-ofat-spot-click="\${i}" title="\${titleAttr}" style="cursor:pointer">\`;
      html += \`<b>\${i + 1}</b>\`;
      html += \`<div>\`;
      html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${escapeHtml(describeSpawnSpot(spot))}</div>\`;
