      html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.spawn.quality", { pct: scorePct }))}</div>\`;
      html += \`<div class="ofat-scorebar"><span style="width:\${scorePct}%"></span></div>\`;
      html += \`</div>\`;
      html += \`<b>\${scorePct}</b>\`;
      html += \`</div>\`;
    }
    if (vm.allianceRecommendations.length) {
      html += \`<div class="ofat-section-title">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.alliance.title"))}</div>\`;
      html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${vm.allianceRecommendations.map((r) => \`\${escapeHtml(r.name)} (\${escapeHtml(r.label)})\`).join(" | ")}</div>\`;
    }
    if (vm.traitorBreakHints && vm.traitorBreakHints.length) {
      const names = vm.traitorBreakHints.map((hint) => escapeHtml(hint.name || hint.id)).join(", ");
      html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\\u26A0 \${escapeHtml(t("panel.alliance.traitor.hint"))} <b>\${names}</b></div>\`;
    }
    if (vm.allyCount || vm.lastSkippedNeighbour) html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.alliance.allies", { count: vm.allyCount }))}\${skipped}</div>\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-stats-toggle ofat-muted" data-ofat-stats-toggle style="cursor:pointer;margin-top:9px">\${escapeHtml(t(statsOpen ? "panel.stats.toggle.open" : "panel.stats.toggle.closed"))}</div>\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-stats-body ofat-muted ofat-meta" style="display:\${statsOpen ? "block" : "none"}">\`;
    html += \`\${escapeHtml(t("panel.stats.players"))} <b>\${vm.otherPlayerCount}</b> | \${escapeHtml(t("panel.stats.nations"))} <b>\${vm.nationCount}</b> | \${escapeHtml(t("panel.stats.tick"))} <b>\${vm.tick}</b>\`;
    html += \`</div>\`;
    if (topPlayerLens) html += topPlayerLens.render(vm);
    html += \`<div class="ofat-buttons">\`;
    html += \`<button id="ofat-close">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.close"))}</button>\`;
    html += \`</div></div>\`;
    return html;
  }
  function renderThreatSummary(threat, index = null) {
    const parts = [\`\${escapeHtml(threat.name)} <b>\${escapeHtml(threatLevelText(threat.level))}</b>\`];
    const nukeText = nukeThreatText(threat);
    if (nukeText) parts.push(escapeHtml(nukeText));
    const inner = parts.join(" ");
    if (index == null || threat.id == null) return \`<span class="ofat-threat-chip">\${inner}</span>\`;
    return \`<span class="ofat-threat-chip ofat-threat-clickable" data-threat-idx="\${index}" title="\${escapeHtml(t("panel.threat.camera.title", { name: threat.name }))}">\${inner}</span>\`;
  }
  function nukeThreatText(threat) {
    if (!threat) return "";
    if (threat.buildingNuke) return t("panel.threat.buildingNuke");
    if (Number(threat.nukePotential || 0) >= 1) return t("panel.threat.nukeReady");
    const eta = Number(threat.nukeEtaSec);
    if (threat.nukeSoon && Number.isFinite(eta) && eta > 0) return t("panel.threat.nukeSoon", { eta: formatEta(eta) });
    return "";
  }
  function formatEta(seconds) {
    const sec = Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds));
    if (sec < 90) return \`\${sec}s\`;
    return \`\${Math.round(sec / 60)}m\`;
  }
  function renderEconomyHtml(eco) {
    const growth = Math.round((eco.growthEfficiency || 0) * 100);
    const safety = Math.round((eco.combatSafety || 0) * 100);
    const safeSend = formatTroopCount(eco.safeSpendableTroops);
    const growthQuality = growth < 30 ? t("panel.eco.growth.low") : growth > 60 ? t("panel.eco.growth.high") : t("panel.eco.growth.ok");
    const push = eco.timeToPushSec != null ? \` | \${t("panel.eco.push", { sec: eco.timeToPushSec })}\` : "";
    let html = \`<div class="ofat-eco ofat-eco-\${economyTone(eco.state)}">\`;
    html += \`<div><b>\${escapeHtml(economyLabel(eco.state))}</b> | <span title="\${escapeHtml(t("panel.eco.growth.tooltip"))}">\${escapeHtml(t("panel.eco.growth", { quality: growthQuality, pct: growth }))}</span>\${push}</div>\`;
    html += \`<div>\${escapeHtml(t("panel.eco.safety", { pct: safety }))} | \${escapeHtml(t("panel.eco.reserve", { val: safeSend }))}</div>\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${escapeHtml(eco.hint || "")}</div>\`;
    html += \`</div>\`;
    return html;
  }
  function renderTargetSummary(target, label = localizedStatusLabel(target.status)) {
    return \`\${escapeHtml(target.name)} <b>\${escapeHtml(label)}</b>\${renderCaptureRating(target.captureRating)}\${renderTeamBackup(target.teamBackup)}\`;
  }
  function localizedStatusLabel(status) {
    const key = \`panel.status.\${status}\`;
    const val = t(key);
    if (val !== key) return val;
    return statusLabel(status);
  }
  function renderCaptureRating(rating) {
    if (!rating || !Number.isFinite(rating.score)) return "";
    const pct = Math.round(rating.score * 100);
    const tierKey = \`panel.capture.\${rating.tier}\`;
    const tierLabel = t(tierKey) !== tierKey ? t(tierKey) : rating.tier;
    return \` <span class="ofat-capture-rating" data-tier="\${escapeHtml(rating.tier)}" title="\${escapeHtml(t("panel.capture.title"))}">\${escapeHtml(tierLabel)} (\${pct}%)</span>\`;
  }
  function renderTeamBackup(teamBackup) {
    if (!teamBackup || !teamBackup.maxDonationTroops || !teamBackup.donorCount) return "";
    return \` <span class="ofat-team-backup" title="\${escapeHtml(teamBackup.donorNames?.join(", ") || t("panel.team.backup.title"))}">+\${escapeHtml(formatTroopCount(teamBackup.maxDonationTroops))} team</span>\`;
  }
  function economyTone(state) {
    if (state === "CRITICAL" || state === "RECOVER") return "low";
    if (state === "PUSH" || state === "CAP_WASTE") return "push";
    return "ok";
  }
  function expansionText(level) {
    if (level === "aggressive") return t("panel.expansion.aggressive");
    if (level === "recover") return t("panel.expansion.recover");
    if (level === "stop") return t("panel.expansion.stop");
    return level || "normal";
  }
  function threatLevelText(level) {
    if (level === "Critical") return t("panel.threat.critical");
    if (level === "Dangerous") return t("panel.threat.dangerous");
    if (level === "Watch") return t("panel.threat.watch");
    return t("panel.threat.hint");
  }
