  // src/page/ui/heatmap.js
  function createHeatmap({ mapDataRef }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let scale = 1;
    let isVisible = false;
    return {
      create(showHeatmap) {
        const mapData = mapDataRef.current;
        canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        scale = Math.min(320 / mapData.width, 220 / mapData.height);
        canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(mapData.width * scale));
        canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(mapData.height * scale));
        canvas.id = "ofat-heatmap";
        isVisible = !!showHeatmap;
        canvas.style.display = isVisible ? "block" : "none";
        return canvas;
      },
      setVisible(visible) {
        isVisible = !!visible;
        if (canvas) canvas.style.display = isVisible ? "block" : "none";
      },
      render(scores, topSpots, playerSpawns) {
        if (!canvas) return;
        if (!isVisible) return;
        const mapData = mapDataRef.current;
        if (!mapData) return;
        renderHeatmap(canvas, scale, mapData, scores, topSpots, playerSpawns);
      }
    };
  }
  function renderHeatmap(canvas, scale, mapData, scores, topSpots, playerSpawns) {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const w = canvas.width;
    const h = canvas.height;
    const scoreGrid = new Float32Array(mapData.width * mapData.height);
    let maxScore = 0;
    const half = 8;
    for (let i = 0; i < scores.length; i += 1) {
      const score = scores[i];
      const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(score.x - half));
      const x1 = Math.min(mapData.width - 1, Math.ceil(score.x + half));
      const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(score.y - half));
      const y1 = Math.min(mapData.height - 1, Math.ceil(score.y + half));
      for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y += 1) {
        const rowOffset = y * mapData.width;
        for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x += 1) scoreGrid[rowOffset + x] = score.score;
      }
      if (score.score > maxScore) maxScore = score.score;
    }
    const imageData = ctx.createImageData(w, h);
    const invMax = maxScore > 0 ? 1 / maxScore : 0;
    for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 1) {
      const my = Math.min(mapData.height - 1, Math.floor(y / scale));
      for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 1) {
        const mx = Math.min(mapData.width - 1, Math.floor(x / scale));
        const value = scoreGrid[my * mapData.width + mx] * invMax;
        const pi = (y * w + x) * 4;
        if (value > 0) {
          imageData.data[pi] = Math.floor((1 - value) * 255);
          imageData.data[pi + 1] = Math.floor(value * 255);
          imageData.data[pi + 2] = 42;
          imageData.data[pi + 3] = Math.floor(value * 180 + 50);
        } else {
          imageData.data[pi] = 8;
          imageData.data[pi + 1] = 10;
          imageData.data[pi + 2] = 14;
          imageData.data[pi + 3] = 150;
        }
      }
    }
    ctx.putImageData(imageData, 0, 0);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#71c7ff";
    mapData.nations.forEach((nation) => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(Math.floor(nation.coordinates[0] * scale), Math.floor(nation.coordinates[1] * scale), 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    });
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#ff5757";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    playerSpawns.forEach((spawn) => {
      const x = Math.floor(spawn.x * scale);
      const y = Math.floor(spawn.y * scale);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x - 4, y - 4);
      ctx.lineTo(x + 4, y + 4);
      ctx.moveTo(x + 4, y - 4);
      ctx.lineTo(x - 4, y + 4);
      ctx.stroke();
    });
    topSpots.forEach((spot, index) => {
      const x = Math.floor(spot.x * scale);
      const y = Math.floor(spot.y * scale);
      ctx.strokeStyle = index === 0 ? "#ffd54f" : index < 3 ? "#9ccc65" : "#d8dde6";
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x, y, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.72)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x, y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = index === 0 ? "#ffd54f" : "#ffffff";
      ctx.font = "bold 9px monospace";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText(String(index + 1), x, y);
    });
  }

  // src/page/ui/missile-overlay.js
  var MISSILE_TYPES = ["Atom Bomb", "Hydrogen Bomb", "MIRV Warhead"];
  var BLAST_RADII = {
    "Atom Bomb": { inner: 12, outer: 30 },
    "Hydrogen Bomb": { inner: 80, outer: 100 },
    "MIRV Warhead": { inner: 12, outer: 18 }
  };
  var TILES_PER_SECOND = 100;
  var SCAN_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
  var STALE_AFTER_MS = 750;
  var Z_INDEX = 99998;
  function createMissileOverlay({ settings }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let ctx = null;
    let scanTimer = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    const tracked = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    function ensureCanvas() {
      if (canvas) return;
      canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.id = "ofat-missile-overlay";
      canvas.style.position = "fixed";
      canvas.style.left = "0";
      canvas.style.top = "0";
      canvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      canvas.style.zIndex = String(Z_INDEX);
      resizeCanvas();
      document.body.appendChild(canvas);
      ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    }
    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.style.width = \`\${window.innerWidth}px\`;
      canvas.style.height = \`\${window.innerHeight}px\`;
    }
    function clearCanvas() {
      if (ctx && canvas) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    function scan() {
      if (!settings.get("showMissilePredictor")) {
        if (tracked.size) tracked.clear();
        return;
      }
      let units = null;
      try {
        const gameView = getGameView();
        if (!gameView || typeof gameView.units !== "function") return;
        units = gameView.units(...MISSILE_TYPES);
      } catch (_) {
        return;
      }
      if (!units || typeof units.forEach !== "function") return;
      const now = Date.now();
      units.forEach((unit) => {
        try {
          if (typeof unit.isActive === "function" && !unit.isActive()) return;
          const targetTile = typeof unit.targetTile === "function" ? unit.targetTile() : void 0;
          if (targetTile === void 0 || targetTile === null) return;
          const id = typeof unit.id === "function" ? unit.id() : null;
          if (id === null) return;
          const type = typeof unit.type === "function" ? unit.type() : null;
          const currentTile = typeof unit.tile === "function" ? unit.tile() : null;
          const existing = tracked.get(id);
          tracked.set(id, {
            type,
            currentTile,
            targetTile,
            firstSeen: existing ? existing.firstSeen : now,
            lastSeen: now
          });
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
      tracked.forEach((entry, id) => {
        if (now - entry.lastSeen > STALE_AFTER_MS) tracked.delete(id);
      });
    }
    function tileCoords3(gameView, tileRef) {
      try {
        if (tileRef === void 0 || tileRef === null) return null;
        if (typeof gameView.x !== "function" || typeof gameView.y !== "function") return null;
        const x = gameView.x(tileRef);
        const y = gameView.y(tileRef);
        if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) return null;
        return { x, y };
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    function render() {
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      if (!settings.get("showMissilePredictor")) {
        clearCanvas();
        return;
      }
      ensureCanvas();
      clearCanvas();
      if (!tracked.size) return;
      const timerOnly = !!settings.get("missilePredictorTimerOnly");
      let gameView = null;
      try {
        gameView = getGameView();
      } catch (_) {
      }
      if (!gameView) return;
      tracked.forEach((entry) => {
        const targetWorld = tileCoords3(gameView, entry.targetTile);
        if (!targetWorld) return;
        const targetScreen = worldToScreenCoordinates(targetWorld);
        if (!targetScreen) return;
        const radii = BLAST_RADII[entry.type] || null;
        const currentWorld = tileCoords3(gameView, entry.currentTile);
        const currentScreen = currentWorld ? worldToScreenCoordinates(currentWorld) : null;
        if (!timerOnly && currentScreen) {
          ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 120, 60, 0.65)";
          ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
          ctx.setLineDash([6, 5]);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(currentScreen.x, currentScreen.y);
          ctx.lineTo(targetScreen.x, targetScreen.y);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.setLineDash([]);
        }
        if (!timerOnly && radii) {
          const innerPx = worldRadiusToPixels(targetWorld, radii.inner, targetScreen);
          const outerPx = worldRadiusToPixels(targetWorld, radii.outer, targetScreen);
          if (outerPx) {
            ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 87, 87, 0.5)";
            ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 87, 87, 0.10)";
            ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(targetScreen.x, targetScreen.y, outerPx, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.stroke();
          }
          if (innerPx) {
            ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 30, 30, 0.85)";
            ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 30, 30, 0.20)";
            ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(targetScreen.x, targetScreen.y, innerPx, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 220, 80, 0.95)";
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(targetScreen.x - 7, targetScreen.y);
        ctx.lineTo(targetScreen.x + 7, targetScreen.y);
        ctx.moveTo(targetScreen.x, targetScreen.y - 7);
        ctx.lineTo(targetScreen.x, targetScreen.y + 7);
        ctx.stroke();
        if (currentWorld) {
          const dx = targetWorld.x - currentWorld.x;
          const dy = targetWorld.y - currentWorld.y;
          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          const eta = dist / TILES_PER_SECOND;
          const label = \`~\${eta.toFixed(1)}s\`;
          ctx.font = "bold 12px monospace";
          ctx.textAlign = "center";
          ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.7)";
          const tw = ctx.measureText(label).width;
          ctx.fillRect(targetScreen.x - tw / 2 - 3, targetScreen.y - 24, tw + 6, 15);
          ctx.fillStyle = "#ffdc50";
          ctx.fillText(label, targetScreen.x, targetScreen.y - 10);
        }
      });
    }
    function worldRadiusToPixels(worldCenter, radiusTiles, centerScreen) {
      const edgeScreen = worldToScreenCoordinates({ x: worldCenter.x + radiusTiles, y: worldCenter.y });
      if (!edgeScreen) return null;
      const px = Math.hypot(edgeScreen.x - centerScreen.x, edgeScreen.y - centerScreen.y);
      return Number.isFinite(px) && px > 0 ? px : null;
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureCanvas();
        if (!scanTimer) scanTimer = setInterval(scan, SCAN_INTERVAL_MS);
        if (rafHandle === null) rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      },
      reset() {
        tracked.clear();
        clearCanvas();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        if (scanTimer) {
          clearInterval(scanTimer);
          scanTimer = null;
        }
        tracked.clear();
        window.removeEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
        if (canvas) canvas.remove();
        canvas = null;
        ctx = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/timer-overlay.js
  var STRUCTURE_TYPES = ["Missile Silo", "SAM Launcher"];
  var CONSTRUCTION_DURATION = {
    "Missile Silo": 100,
    "SAM Launcher": 300
  };
  var COOLDOWN_TICKS = 90;
  var TICKS_PER_SECOND = 10;
  var SCAN_INTERVAL_MS2 = 250;
  var Z_INDEX2 = 99997;
  function createTimerOverlay({ settings }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let ctx = null;
    let scanTimer = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    const constructionFirstSeen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const labels = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    function ensureCanvas() {
      if (canvas) return;
      canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.id = "ofat-timer-overlay";
      canvas.style.position = "fixed";
      canvas.style.left = "0";
      canvas.style.top = "0";
      canvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      canvas.style.zIndex = String(Z_INDEX2);
      resizeCanvas();
      document.body.appendChild(canvas);
      ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    }
    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.style.width = \`\${window.innerWidth}px\`;
      canvas.style.height = \`\${window.innerHeight}px\`;
    }
    function clearCanvas() {
      if (ctx && canvas) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    function constructionDuration(gameView, type) {
      try {
        const config = typeof gameView.config === "function" ? gameView.config() : null;
        const info = config && typeof config.unitInfo === "function" ? config.unitInfo(type) : null;
        const dur = info && info.constructionDuration;
        if (Number.isFinite(dur) && dur > 0) return dur;
      } catch (_) {
      }
      return CONSTRUCTION_DURATION[type] || null;
    }
    function scan() {
      if (!settings.get("showBuildTimers")) {
        if (labels.size) labels.clear();
        if (constructionFirstSeen.size) constructionFirstSeen.clear();
        return;
      }
      let gameView = null;
      let units = null;
      let ticks = null;
      try {
        gameView = getGameView();
        if (!gameView || typeof gameView.units !== "function" || typeof gameView.ticks !== "function") return;
        units = gameView.units(...STRUCTURE_TYPES);
        ticks = gameView.ticks();
      } catch (_) {
        return;
      }
      if (!units || typeof units.forEach !== "function" || !Number.isFinite(ticks)) return;
      const seenIds = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      labels.clear();
      units.forEach((unit) => {
        try {
          if (typeof unit.isActive === "function" && !unit.isActive()) return;
          const id = typeof unit.id === "function" ? unit.id() : null;
          if (id === null) return;
          seenIds.add(id);
          const type = typeof unit.type === "function" ? unit.type() : null;
          const currentTile = typeof unit.tile === "function" ? unit.tile() : null;
          const underConstruction = typeof unit.isUnderConstruction === "function" && unit.isUnderConstruction();
          if (underConstruction) {
            if (!constructionFirstSeen.has(id)) constructionFirstSeen.set(id, ticks);
            const firstTick = constructionFirstSeen.get(id);
            const duration = constructionDuration(gameView, type);
            let label2 = t("timer.building");
            if (duration) {
              const remainingTicks = duration - (ticks - firstTick);
              const remainingSec = remainingTicks / TICKS_PER_SECOND;
              if (remainingSec > 0) label2 = \`~\${remainingSec.toFixed(0)}s\`;
            }
            labels.set(id, { type, currentTile, label: label2, ready: false });
            return;
          }
          constructionFirstSeen.delete(id);
          let label = t("timer.ready");
          let ready = true;
          const queue = typeof unit.missileTimerQueue === "function" ? unit.missileTimerQueue() : null;
          const level = typeof unit.level === "function" ? unit.level() : 1;
          if (queue && Array.isArray(queue) && queue.length >= level) {
            const remainingTicks = COOLDOWN_TICKS - (ticks - queue[0]);
            const remainingSec = remainingTicks / TICKS_PER_SECOND;
            if (remainingSec > 0) {
              label = \`\${remainingSec.toFixed(0)}s\`;
              ready = false;
            }
          }
          labels.set(id, { type, currentTile, label, ready });
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
      constructionFirstSeen.forEach((_tick, id) => {
        if (!seenIds.has(id)) constructionFirstSeen.delete(id);
      });
    }
    function tileCoords3(gameView, tileRef) {
      try {
        if (tileRef === void 0 || tileRef === null) return null;
        if (typeof gameView.x !== "function" || typeof gameView.y !== "function") return null;
        const x = gameView.x(tileRef);
        const y = gameView.y(tileRef);
        if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) return null;
        return { x, y };
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    function render() {
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      if (!settings.get("showBuildTimers")) {
        clearCanvas();
        return;
      }
      ensureCanvas();
      clearCanvas();
      if (!labels.size) return;
      let gameView = null;
      try {
        gameView = getGameView();
      } catch (_) {
      }
      if (!gameView) return;
      ctx.font = "bold 11px monospace";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      labels.forEach((entry) => {
        const world = tileCoords3(gameView, entry.currentTile);
        if (!world) return;
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(world);
        if (!screen) return;
        const text = entry.label;
        const tw = ctx.measureText(text).width;
        const padX = 4;
        const boxW = tw + padX * 2;
        const boxH = 15;
        const bx = screen.x - boxW / 2;
        const by = screen.y - boxH - 6;
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.72)";
        ctx.fillRect(bx, by, boxW, boxH);
        ctx.fillStyle = entry.ready ? "#9ccc65" : "#ffd54f";
        ctx.fillText(text, screen.x, by + boxH / 2 + 0.5);
      });
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureCanvas();
        if (!scanTimer) scanTimer = setInterval(scan, SCAN_INTERVAL_MS2);
        if (rafHandle === null) rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      },
      reset() {
        labels.clear();
        constructionFirstSeen.clear();
        clearCanvas();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        if (scanTimer) {
          clearInterval(scanTimer);
          scanTimer = null;
        }
        labels.clear();
        constructionFirstSeen.clear();
        window.removeEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
        if (canvas) canvas.remove();
        canvas = null;
        ctx = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/troop-bar-marker.js
  var SWEET_RATIO = Number.isFinite(PEAK_RATIO) ? PEAK_RATIO : 0.42;
  var BAND_HALF_WIDTH = 0.04;
  var MARKER_CLASS = "ofat-sweet-marker";
  var STYLE_ID = "ofat-sweet-marker-style";
  var STYLE_CSS = \`
.\${MARKER_CLASS} {
  position: absolute;
  top: -2px;
  bottom: -2px;
  left: \${(SWEET_RATIO - BAND_HALF_WIDTH) * 100}%;
  width: \${BAND_HALF_WIDTH * 2 * 100}%;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 10;
  border-left: 2px solid rgba(118, 255, 3, 0.9);
  border-right: 2px solid rgba(118, 255, 3, 0.9);
  background: rgba(118, 255, 3, 0.18);
  box-shadow: 0 0 4px rgba(118, 255, 3, 0.5);
}
\`;
  function createTroopBarMarker({ settings }) {
    let observer = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    function findBarContainer() {
      try {
        const panel = document.querySelector("control-panel");
        if (!panel) return null;
        const fill = panel.querySelector('div.bg-malibu-blue[style*="width"]');
        if (!fill) return null;
        const container = fill.closest("div.h-6.border.border-gray-600.rounded-md") || fill.parentElement;
        return container || null;
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    function inject() {
      if (!settings.get("showTroopSweetSpot")) {
        removeMarker();
        return;
      }
      const container = findBarContainer();
      if (!container) return;
      if (container.querySelector(\`.\${MARKER_CLASS}\`)) return;
      if (!container.style.position) container.style.position = "relative";
      container.style.overflow = "visible";
      const marker = document.createElement("div");
      marker.className = MARKER_CLASS;
      marker.title = \`Wachstums-Peak ~\${Math.round(SWEET_RATIO * 100)}%\`;
      container.appendChild(marker);
    }
    function removeMarker() {
      try {
        document.querySelectorAll(\`.\${MARKER_CLASS}\`).forEach((el) => el.remove());
      } catch (_) {
      }
    }
    function scheduleInject() {
      if (rafHandle !== null) return;
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        rafHandle = null;
        runInjectGuarded();
      });
    }
    function runInjectGuarded() {
      if (observer) observer.disconnect();
      try {
        inject();
      } catch (_) {
      } finally {
        reconnectObserver();
      }
    }
    function reconnectObserver() {
      try {
        const panel = document.querySelector("control-panel");
        if (observer && panel) observer.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      } catch (_) {
      }
    }
    return {
      start() {
        createStyle(STYLE_ID, STYLE_CSS);
        if (!observer && typeof MutationObserver === "function") {
          observer = new MutationObserver(() => scheduleInject());
        }
        runInjectGuarded();
      },
      reset() {
        removeMarker();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        if (observer) {
          observer.disconnect();
          observer = null;
        }
        removeMarker();
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/map-money-overlay.js
  var Z_INDEX3 = 99997;
  var SCAN_INTERVAL_MS3 = 220;
  function formatGold2(value) {
    const num = finiteOrZero(value);
    const abs = Math.abs(num);
    const sign = num < 0 ? "-" : "";
    if (abs >= 1e6) return \`\${sign}\${(Math.floor(abs / 1e4) / 100).toFixed(2)}M\`;
    if (abs >= 1e4) return \`\${sign}\${(Math.floor(abs / 100) / 10).toFixed(1)}k\`;
    if (abs >= 1e3) return \`\${sign}\${(Math.floor(abs / 10) / 100).toFixed(2)}k\`;
    return \`\${sign}\${Math.round(abs)}\`;
  }
  function callMaybe(obj, name) {
    try {
      return obj && typeof obj[name] === "function" ? obj[name]() : void 0;
    } catch (_) {
      return void 0;
    }
  }
  function getPlayerCenterWorld2(player) {
    const loc = callMaybe(player, "nameLocation");
    if (loc && Number.isFinite(loc.x) && Number.isFinite(loc.y)) return { x: loc.x, y: loc.y };
    return null;
  }
  function createMapMoneyOverlay({ settings, teamDetection }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let ctx = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    let scanTimer = null;
    let scanned = [];
    function ensureCanvas() {
      if (canvas) return;
      canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.id = "ofat-map-money-overlay";
      canvas.style.position = "fixed";
      canvas.style.left = "0";
      canvas.style.top = "0";
      canvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      canvas.style.zIndex = String(Z_INDEX3);
      resizeCanvas();
      document.body.appendChild(canvas);
      ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    }
    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.style.width = \`\${window.innerWidth}px\`;
      canvas.style.height = \`\${window.innerHeight}px\`;
    }
    function clearCanvas() {
      if (ctx && canvas) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    function classEnabled(player, playerID, myState) {
      const isBot = callMaybe(player, "type") === "BOT";
      if (isBot) return !!settings.get("showMapMoneyBots");
      const isTeammate = !!teamDetection?.isMyTeammate?.(playerID);
      const isAlly = isTeammate || myState != null && playerID != null && isAlliedWith(myState, playerID);
      if (isAlly) return !!settings.get("showMapMoneyAllies");
      return !!settings.get("showMapMoneyEnemies");
    }
    function scan() {
      if (!settings.get("showMapMoney")) {
        if (scanned.length) scanned = [];
        return;
      }
      let gameView = null;
      try {
        gameView = getGameView();
      } catch (_) {
      }
      if (!gameView || typeof gameView.playerViews !== "function") {
        scanned = [];
        return;
      }
      let players = null;
      try {
        players = gameView.playerViews() || [];
      } catch (_) {
        return;
      }
      const me = callMaybe(gameView, "myPlayer") || null;
      const myID = callMaybe(me, "id");
      const myState = me ? { id: myID, alliances: callMaybe(me, "alliances") || [] } : null;
      const next = [];
      players.forEach((player) => {
        try {
          if (!player) return;
          const alive = typeof player.isAlive === "function" ? player.isAlive() : true;
          if (!alive) return;
          const playerID = callMaybe(player, "id");
          const isMe = myID != null && playerID === myID;
          if (!isMe && !classEnabled(player, playerID, myState)) return;
          const world = getPlayerCenterWorld2(player);
          if (!world) return;
          let gold2 = callMaybe(player, "gold");
          if (gold2 === void 0) gold2 = player.state ? player.state.gold : 0;
          const goldVal = typeof gold2 === "bigint" ? Number(gold2) : finiteOrZero(gold2);
          next.push({ world, gold: goldVal, isMe });
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
      scanned = next;
    }
    function render() {
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      if (!settings.get("showMapMoney")) {
        clearCanvas();
        return;
      }
      ensureCanvas();
      clearCanvas();
      if (!scanned.length) return;
      ctx.font = "bold 12px monospace";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "top";
      scanned.forEach((entry) => {
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(entry.world);
        if (!screen) return;
        if (screen.x < -80 || screen.y < -40 || screen.x > canvas.width + 80 || screen.y > canvas.height + 40) return;
        const label = \`$\${formatGold2(entry.gold)}\`;
        const tw = ctx.measureText(label).width;
        const yOff = 22;
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.8)";
        ctx.fillRect(screen.x - tw / 2 - 4, screen.y + yOff - 2, tw + 8, 16);
        ctx.fillStyle = entry.isMe ? "#ffd54f" : "#9fe0ff";
        ctx.fillText(label, screen.x, screen.y + yOff);
      });
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureCanvas();
        if (!scanTimer) scanTimer = setInterval(scan, SCAN_INTERVAL_MS3);
        if (rafHandle === null) rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      },
      reset() {
        scanned = [];
        clearCanvas();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        if (scanTimer) {
          clearInterval(scanTimer);
          scanTimer = null;
        }
        scanned = [];
        window.removeEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
        if (canvas) canvas.remove();
        canvas = null;
        ctx = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/map-overlay-base.js
  function createMapOverlay({ id, zIndex = 99997, scanIntervalMs = 220, enabled, scan, draw, onReset }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let ctx = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    let scanTimer = null;
    function ensureCanvas() {
      if (canvas) return;
      canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.id = id;
      canvas.style.position = "fixed";
      canvas.style.left = "0";
      canvas.style.top = "0";
      canvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      canvas.style.zIndex = String(zIndex);
      resizeCanvas();
      document.body.appendChild(canvas);
      ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    }
    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.style.width = \`\${window.innerWidth}px\`;
      canvas.style.height = \`\${window.innerHeight}px\`;
    }
    function clearCanvas() {
      if (ctx && canvas) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    function runScan() {
      if (typeof enabled === "function" && !enabled()) {
        if (typeof onReset === "function") {
          try {
            onReset();
          } catch (_) {
          }
        }
        return;
      }
      let gameView = null;
      try {
        gameView = getGameView();
      } catch (_) {
      }
      if (!gameView) return;
      try {
        scan(gameView);
      } catch (_) {
      }
    }
    function frame() {
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      if (typeof enabled === "function" && !enabled()) {
        clearCanvas();
        return;
      }
      ensureCanvas();
      clearCanvas();
      try {
        draw(ctx, canvas);
      } catch (_) {
      }
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureCanvas();
        if (!scanTimer) scanTimer = setInterval(runScan, scanIntervalMs);
        if (rafHandle === null) rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      },
      reset() {
        if (typeof onReset === "function") {
          try {
            onReset();
          } catch (_) {
          }
        }
        clearCanvas();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        if (scanTimer) {
          clearInterval(scanTimer);
          scanTimer = null;
        }
        if (typeof onReset === "function") {
          try {
            onReset();
          } catch (_) {
          }
        }
        window.removeEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
        if (canvas) canvas.remove();
        canvas = null;
        ctx = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/name-overlay.js
  var CURRENT_WEAKNESS_THRESHOLD = 0.1;
  var TOTAL_POTENTIAL_THRESHOLD = 0.3;
  var DANGER_THRESHOLD = 1.35;
  var ATOM_COST2 = 75e4;
  var HYDROGEN_COST2 = 5e6;
  var ATOM_ICON = "[A]";
  var HYDROGEN_ICON = "[H]";
  var FEATURE_KEYS = [
    "showRatioBar",
    "showTroopRatios",
    "showThreatIcons",
    "showAttackBadges",
    "showWeaknessIndicator",
    "showDangerIndicator"
  ];
  var FARM_BADGE_COLORS = {
    farm: "rgba(77, 224, 117, 0.92)",
    target: "rgba(83, 181, 255, 0.94)",
    mark: "rgba(255, 190, 72, 0.94)"
  };
  function callMaybe2(obj, name, arg) {
    try {
      return obj && typeof obj[name] === "function" ? obj[name](arg) : void 0;
    } catch (_) {
      return void 0;
    }
  }
  function getPlayerCenterWorld3(player) {
    const loc = callMaybe2(player, "nameLocation");
    if (loc && Number.isFinite(loc.x) && Number.isFinite(loc.y)) return { x: loc.x, y: loc.y };
    return null;
  }
  function getOutgoingAttackTroops(player) {
    const attacks = callMaybe2(player, "outgoingAttacks");
    if (!Array.isArray(attacks)) return 0;
    return attacks.reduce((sum, attack) => sum + (attack && attack.retreating ? 0 : Number(attack && attack.troops || 0)), 0);
  }
  function getThreatIcon(player) {
    const silos = callMaybe2(player, "units", "Missile Silo");
    if (!Array.isArray(silos) || silos.length === 0) return "";
    let gold2 = callMaybe2(player, "gold");
    if (gold2 === void 0 && player.state) gold2 = player.state.gold;
    const goldVal = typeof gold2 === "bigint" ? Number(gold2) : finiteOrZero(gold2);
    if (goldVal >= HYDROGEN_COST2) return HYDROGEN_ICON;
    if (goldVal >= ATOM_COST2) return ATOM_ICON;
    return "";
  }
  function createNameOverlay({ settings, teamDetection }) {
    let farmRecs = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    let scanned = [];
    function anyEnabled() {
      return FEATURE_KEYS.some((key) => settings.get(key));
    }
    function scan(gameView) {
      if (typeof gameView.playerViews !== "function") {
        scanned = [];
        return;
      }
      let players = null;
      try {
        players = gameView.playerViews() || [];
      } catch (_) {
        return;
      }
      const me = callMaybe2(gameView, "myPlayer") || null;
      const myID = callMaybe2(me, "id");
      const myTroops = me ? Number(callMaybe2(me, "troops")) : NaN;
      const config = callMaybe2(gameView, "config") || null;
      const next = [];
      players.forEach((player) => {
        try {
          if (!player || callMaybe2(player, "isAlive") === false) return;
          const world = getPlayerCenterWorld3(player);
          if (!world) return;
          const playerID = callMaybe2(player, "id");
          const isMe = myID != null && playerID === myID;
          const isBot = String(callMaybe2(player, "type") || "").toUpperCase() === "BOT";
          const troops = Number(callMaybe2(player, "troops"));
          const maxTroops = Number(callMaybe2(config, "maxTroops", player));
          const attackingTroops = getOutgoingAttackTroops(player);
          if (!Number.isFinite(troops)) return;
          const entry = {
            world,
            isMe,
            isBot,
            troops,
            maxTroops: Number.isFinite(maxTroops) ? maxTroops : 0,
            attackingTroops: Number.isFinite(attackingTroops) ? attackingTroops : 0,
            threatIcon: isMe ? "" : getThreatIcon(player),
            weakness: false,
            danger: false,
            farm: null
          };
          if (!isMe && Number.isFinite(myTroops) && myTroops > 0 && entry.maxTroops > 0) {
            const sameTeam2 = !!teamDetection?.isMyTeammate?.(playerID);
            if (troops <= CURRENT_WEAKNESS_THRESHOLD * entry.maxTroops && troops + entry.attackingTroops < TOTAL_POTENTIAL_THRESHOLD * entry.maxTroops) {
              entry.weakness = true;
            } else if (!sameTeam2 && troops / myTroops >= DANGER_THRESHOLD) {
              entry.danger = true;
            }
          }
          const rec = playerID != null ? farmRecs.get(playerID) : null;
          if (rec && rec.label && rec.status !== "wait" && rec.status !== "hold") {
            entry.farm = { status: rec.status || "mark", label: rec.label };
          }
          next.push(entry);
        } catch (_) {
        }
      });
      scanned = next;
    }
    function draw(ctx, canvas) {
      if (!scanned.length) return;
      const showRatioBar = settings.get("showRatioBar");
      const showMax = settings.get("showTroopRatios");
      const showThreat = settings.get("showThreatIcons");
      const showBadges = settings.get("showAttackBadges");
      const showWeakness = settings.get("showWeaknessIndicator");
      const showDanger = settings.get("showDangerIndicator");
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
      scanned.forEach((entry) => {
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(entry.world);
        if (!screen) return;
        if (screen.x < -80 || screen.y < -60 || screen.x > canvas.width + 80 || screen.y > canvas.height + 60) return;
        const barTop = screen.y - 15;
        if (showRatioBar && !entry.isBot && entry.maxTroops > 0) {
          const barW = 32;
          const barH = 4;
          const barX = screen.x - barW / 2;
          const total = entry.troops + entry.attackingTroops;
          const totalRatio = Math.min(total / entry.maxTroops, 1);
          const mainRatio = entry.attackingTroops === 0 ? 1 : total > 0 ? entry.troops / total : 0;
          const bufferRatio = entry.attackingTroops === 0 ? 0 : total > 0 ? entry.attackingTroops / total : 0;
          const mainW = mainRatio * totalRatio * barW;
          const bufferW = bufferRatio * totalRatio * barW;
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(34,34,34,0.7)";
          ctx.fillRect(barX, barTop, barW, barH);
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0, 210, 82, 0.85)";
          ctx.fillRect(barX, barTop, mainW, barH);
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 166, 0, 0.7)";
          ctx.fillRect(barX + mainW, barTop, bufferW, barH);
          ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(68,68,68,0.8)";
          ctx.lineWidth = 1;
          ctx.strokeRect(barX + 0.5, barTop + 0.5, barW - 1, barH - 1);
          if (showMax) {
            ctx.font = "bold 9px monospace";
            ctx.textAlign = "left";
            ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)";
            ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.9)";
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            const maxLabel = \`/\${formatTroopCount(entry.maxTroops)}\`;
            ctx.strokeText(maxLabel, barX + barW + 3, barTop + barH + 1);
            ctx.fillText(maxLabel, barX + barW + 3, barTop + barH + 1);
            ctx.textAlign = "center";
          }
        }
        const segments = [];
        if (showThreat && entry.threatIcon) segments.push({ text: entry.threatIcon, color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)", bg: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)" });
        if (showBadges && entry.farm) segments.push({ text: entry.farm.label, color: "#05070a", bg: FARM_BADGE_COLORS[entry.farm.status] || FARM_BADGE_COLORS.mark });
        if (segments.length) {
          ctx.font = "bold 10px monospace";
          const gap = 3;
          const padX = 3;
          const lineH = 13;
          const widths = segments.map((seg) => ctx.measureText(seg.text).width + padX * 2);
          const totalW = widths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + gap * (segments.length - 1);
          let x = screen.x - totalW / 2;
          const yTop = barTop - lineH - 1;
          ctx.textAlign = "left";
          ctx.textBaseline = "top";
          segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
            ctx.fillStyle = seg.bg;
            ctx.fillRect(x, yTop, widths[i], lineH);
            ctx.fillStyle = seg.color;
            ctx.fillText(seg.text, x + padX, yTop + 2);
            x += widths[i] + gap;
          });
          ctx.textAlign = "center";
          ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
        }
        if (showWeakness && entry.weakness || showDanger && entry.danger) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(screen.x - 26, screen.y - 4, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = entry.danger ? "rgba(255, 60, 60, 0.95)" : "rgba(255, 150, 0, 0.95)";
          ctx.fill();
          ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.8)";
          ctx.lineWidth = 1;
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      });
    }
    const overlay = createMapOverlay({
      id: "ofat-name-overlay",
      zIndex: 99996,
      // knapp unter map-money (99997), beides unter Missile/HUD
      scanIntervalMs: 220,
      enabled: anyEnabled,
      scan,
      draw,
      onReset: () => {
        scanned = [];
      }
    });
    return {
      ...overlay,
      // Farm-Recommendations aus dem recalc-Loop (gleiche Daten wie das Farm-Badge fr\xFCher
      // per postMessage bekam). Erwartet ein Array von { id, status, label, ... }.
      setFarmRecommendations(recommendations) {
        if (!Array.isArray(recommendations)) {
          farmRecs = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
          return;
        }
        farmRecs = new Map(recommendations.filter((rec) => rec && rec.id != null).map((rec) => [rec.id, rec]));
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/map-heatmap-overlay.js
  var Z_INDEX4 = 99996;
  var CELL = 14;
  var MAX_CELLS = 1200;
  function createMapHeatmapOverlay({ settings }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let ctx = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    let scores = [];
    let topSpots = [];
    let maxScore = 0;
    let visible = false;
    function ensureCanvas() {
      if (canvas) return;
      canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.id = "ofat-map-heatmap-overlay";
      canvas.style.position = "fixed";
      canvas.style.left = "0";
      canvas.style.top = "0";
      canvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      canvas.style.zIndex = String(Z_INDEX4);
      resizeCanvas();
      document.body.appendChild(canvas);
      ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    }
    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.style.width = \`\${window.innerWidth}px\`;
      canvas.style.height = \`\${window.innerHeight}px\`;
    }
    function clearCanvas() {
      if (ctx && canvas) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    function isEnabled() {
      return !!(settings && settings.get("showSpawnHeatmapMap")) && visible;
    }
    function render() {
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      if (!isEnabled()) {
        clearCanvas();
        return;
      }
      ensureCanvas();
      clearCanvas();
      if (!scores.length || maxScore <= 0) return;
      const invMax = 1 / maxScore;
      const half = CELL / 2;
      const limit = Math.min(scores.length, MAX_CELLS);
      for (let i = 0; i < limit; i += 1) {
        const item = scores[i];
        if (!item) continue;
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(item);
        if (!screen) continue;
        if (screen.x < -CELL || screen.y < -CELL || screen.x > canvas.width + CELL || screen.y > canvas.height + CELL) {
          continue;
        }
        const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (item.score || 0) * invMax));
        if (value <= 0) continue;
        const r = Math.floor((1 - value) * 255);
        const g = Math.floor(value * 255);
        const a = (value * 0.45 + 0.12).toFixed(3);
        ctx.fillStyle = \`rgba(\${r},\${g},42,\${a})\`;
        ctx.fillRect(screen.x - half, screen.y - half, CELL, CELL);
      }
      drawTopSpots();
    }
    function drawTopSpots() {
      if (!topSpots.length) return;
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.font = "bold 11px monospace";
      topSpots.forEach((spot, index) => {
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(spot);
        if (!screen) return;
        if (screen.x < -20 || screen.y < -20 || screen.x > canvas.width + 20 || screen.y > canvas.height + 20) return;
        const color = index === 0 ? "#ffd54f" : index < 3 ? "#9ccc65" : "#d8dde6";
        ctx.strokeStyle = color;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(screen.x, screen.y, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.72)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(screen.x, screen.y, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = color;
        ctx.fillText(String(index + 1), screen.x, screen.y);
      });
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureCanvas();
        if (rafHandle === null) rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      },
      // Vom Lead pro Recalc gepusht. scores = [{x,y,score,...}] (Welt-Koords), topSpots gleiche Form.
      // visibleFlag steuert die Spawn-Phasen-Sichtbarkeit (true nur w\xE4hrend des Spawnens).
      setData(nextScores, nextTopSpots, visibleFlag) {
        scores = Array.isArray(nextScores) ? nextScores : [];
        topSpots = Array.isArray(nextTopSpots) ? nextTopSpots : [];
        if (arguments.length >= 3) visible = !!visibleFlag;
        maxScore = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < scores.length; i += 1) {
          const s = scores[i] && scores[i].score;
          if (typeof s === "number" && s > maxScore) maxScore = s;
        }
      },
      // Alternative zur visibleFlag in setData: Spawn-Phase explizit ein/aus.
      setVisible(visibleFlag) {
        visible = !!visibleFlag;
      },
      reset() {
        scores = [];
        topSpots = [];
        maxScore = 0;
        visible = false;
        clearCanvas();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        scores = [];
        topSpots = [];
        maxScore = 0;
        visible = false;
        window.removeEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
        if (canvas) canvas.remove();
        canvas = null;
        ctx = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/ui/attack-highlight-overlay.js
  var Z_INDEX5 = 99995;
  var SCAN_INTERVAL_MS4 = 400;
  var PULSE_PERIOD_MS = 1100;
  function callMaybe3(obj, name, arg) {
    try {
      if (obj && typeof obj[name] === "function") return arguments.length >= 3 ? obj[name](arg) : obj[name]();
    } catch (_) {
    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function worldFromNameLocation(player) {
    const loc = callMaybe3(player, "nameLocation");
    if (loc && Number.isFinite(loc.x) && Number.isFinite(loc.y)) return { x: loc.x, y: loc.y };
    return null;
  }
  function createAttackHighlightOverlay({ settings, gameState, teamDetection }) {
    let myWorld = null;
    let attackers = [];
    let totalTroops = 0;
    function isEnabled() {
      return !!(settings && settings.get("showAttackHighlight"));
    }
    function resolveAttackerWorld(viewsByID, viewsBySmallID, attacker) {
      let pv = null;
      if (attacker.id != null && viewsByID.has(attacker.id)) pv = viewsByID.get(attacker.id);
      else if (attacker.smallID != null && viewsBySmallID.has(attacker.smallID)) pv = viewsBySmallID.get(attacker.smallID);
      return pv ? worldFromNameLocation(pv) : null;
    }
    function scan(gameView) {
      if (!isEnabled()) {
        if (attackers.length) attackers = [];
        myWorld = null;
        totalTroops = 0;
        return;
      }
      const myState = gameState && typeof gameState.getMyState === "function" ? gameState.getMyState() : null;
      const list = myState ? incomingAttackers(gameState, myState) : [];
      if (!list.length) {
        attackers = [];
        myWorld = null;
        totalTroops = 0;
        return;
      }
      const me = callMaybe3(gameView, "myPlayer") || null;
      myWorld = worldFromNameLocation(me);
      const viewsByID = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      const viewsBySmallID = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      let views = null;
      try {
        views = typeof gameView.playerViews === "function" ? gameView.playerViews() || [] : [];
      } catch (_) {
        views = [];
      }
      views.forEach((pv) => {
        const id = callMaybe3(pv, "id");
        const smallID = callMaybe3(pv, "smallID");
        if (id != null) viewsByID.set(id, pv);
        if (smallID != null) viewsBySmallID.set(smallID, pv);
      });
      let sum = 0;
      const next = [];
      list.forEach((attacker) => {
        if (teamDetection && attacker.id != null && teamDetection.isMyTeammate && teamDetection.isMyTeammate(attacker.id)) {
          return;
        }
        const world = resolveAttackerWorld(viewsByID, viewsBySmallID, attacker);
        const troops = Number(attacker.troops || 0);
        sum += troops;
        next.push({ world, troops });
      });
      attackers = next;
      totalTroops = sum;
    }
    function draw(ctx, canvas) {
      if (!isEnabled() || !attackers.length) return;
      const phase = Date.now() % PULSE_PERIOD_MS / PULSE_PERIOD_MS;
      const pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(phase * Math.PI * 2);
      const center = myWorld ? worldToScreenCoordinates(myWorld) : null;
      attackers.forEach((entry) => {
        if (!entry.world) return;
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(entry.world);
        if (!screen) return;
        if (screen.x < -60 || screen.y < -60 || screen.x > canvas.width + 60 || screen.y > canvas.height + 60) return;
        const radius = 12 + pulse * 8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(screen.x, screen.y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.strokeStyle = \`rgba(255,70,70,\${(0.35 + pulse * 0.5).toFixed(3)})\`;
        ctx.lineWidth = 3;
        ctx.stroke();
        if (center) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(screen.x, screen.y);
          ctx.lineTo(center.x, center.y);
          ctx.strokeStyle = \`rgba(255,70,70,\${(0.18 + pulse * 0.25).toFixed(3)})\`;
          ctx.lineWidth = 2;
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      });
      if (center) {
        const glow = 26 + pulse * 18;
        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(center.x, center.y, 4, center.x, center.y, glow);
        grad.addColorStop(0, \`rgba(255,60,60,\${(0.3 + pulse * 0.25).toFixed(3)})\`);
        grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,60,60,0)");
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(center.x, center.y, glow, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(center.x, center.y, 22 + pulse * 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.strokeStyle = \`rgba(255,80,80,\${(0.55 + pulse * 0.4).toFixed(3)})\`;
        ctx.lineWidth = 3;
        ctx.stroke();
        if (totalTroops > 0) {
          const label = \`\\u2694 \${Math.round(totalTroops / 10)}\`;
          ctx.font = "bold 13px monospace";
          ctx.textAlign = "center";
          ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
          const tw = ctx.measureText(label).width;
          const ly = center.y - glow - 4;
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.62)";
          ctx.fillRect(center.x - tw / 2 - 4, ly - 15, tw + 8, 16);
          ctx.fillStyle = "#ff8a80";
          ctx.fillText(label, center.x, ly);
        }
      }
    }
    function onReset() {
      myWorld = null;
      attackers = [];
      totalTroops = 0;
    }
    return createMapOverlay({
      id: "ofat-attack-highlight-overlay",
      zIndex: Z_INDEX5,
      scanIntervalMs: SCAN_INTERVAL_MS4,
      enabled: isEnabled,
      scan,
      draw,
      onReset
    });
  }

  // src/page/ui/build-suggestion-overlay.js
  var Z_INDEX6 = 99996;
  var BANNER_Z = 99999;
  function createBuildSuggestionOverlay({ settings }) {
    let canvas = null;
    let ctx = null;
    let rafHandle = null;
    let banner = null;
    let suggestion = null;
    function isEnabled() {
      return !!(settings && settings.get("showBuildSuggestions"));
    }
    function ensureCanvas() {
      if (canvas) return;
      canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.id = "ofat-build-suggestion-overlay";
      canvas.style.position = "fixed";
      canvas.style.left = "0";
      canvas.style.top = "0";
      canvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      canvas.style.zIndex = String(Z_INDEX6);
      resizeCanvas();
      document.body.appendChild(canvas);
      ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    }
    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.style.width = \`\${window.innerWidth}px\`;
      canvas.style.height = \`\${window.innerHeight}px\`;
    }
    function clearCanvas() {
      if (ctx && canvas) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    function ensureBanner() {
      if (banner) return;
      banner = document.createElement("div");
      banner.id = "ofat-build-banner";
      banner.style.position = "fixed";
      banner.style.top = "10px";
      banner.style.right = "10px";
      banner.style.zIndex = String(BANNER_Z);
      banner.style.pointerEvents = "none";
      banner.style.padding = "10px 16px";
      banner.style.borderRadius = "10px";
      banner.style.boxShadow = "0 3px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)";
      banner.style.display = "none";
      banner.style.whiteSpace = "nowrap";
      banner.style.textAlign = "right";
      document.body.appendChild(banner);
    }
    function gold2(value) {
      const num = Number(value);
      if (!Number.isFinite(num) || num <= 0) return "";
      if (num >= 1e6) return \`\${Math.round(num / 1e5) / 10}M\`;
      if (num >= 1e3) return \`\${Math.round(num / 100) / 10}k\`;
      return String(Math.round(num));
    }
    function updateBanner() {
      ensureBanner();
      if (!isEnabled() || !suggestion || !suggestion.unit) {
        banner.style.display = "none";
        return;
      }
      const cost = gold2(suggestion.estimatedCost);
      if (suggestion.affordableNow) {
        banner.style.font = "bold 18px sans-serif";
        banner.style.color = "#06250f";
        banner.style.background = "linear-gradient(180deg,#a5ffb0,#46c95c)";
        banner.style.border = "1px solid #2f8f43";
        banner.innerHTML = \`\${escapeHtml(t("build.afford_now"))} <span style="font-size:20px">\${escapeHtml(suggestion.unit)}</span>\${cost ? \` <span style="opacity:0.8">(\${escapeHtml(cost)})</span>\` : ""}\`;
      } else {
        banner.style.font = "bold 13px sans-serif";
        banner.style.color = "#ffe0a3";
        banner.style.background = "rgba(20,24,30,0.86)";
        banner.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,213,79,0.45)";
        banner.innerHTML = \`\${escapeHtml(t("build.saving_for"))} <b>\${escapeHtml(suggestion.unit)}</b>\${cost ? \` (\${escapeHtml(cost)})\` : ""}\`;
      }
      banner.style.display = "block";
    }
    function render() {
      rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      if (!isEnabled()) {
        clearCanvas();
        if (banner) banner.style.display = "none";
        return;
      }
      ensureCanvas();
      clearCanvas();
      updateBanner();
      const tiles = suggestion && Array.isArray(suggestion.tiles) ? suggestion.tiles : [];
      if (!tiles.length) return;
      const affordable = !!(suggestion && suggestion.affordableNow);
      const stroke = affordable ? "#5edb6f" : "#ffd54f";
      const fill = affordable ? "rgba(94,219,111,0.22)" : "rgba(255,213,79,0.16)";
      const phase = Date.now() % 1400 / 1400;
      const pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(phase * Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "bottom";
      ctx.font = "bold 11px monospace";
      tiles.forEach((tile) => {
        const world = tile && tile.world;
        if (!world || !Number.isFinite(world.x) || !Number.isFinite(world.y)) return;
        const screen = worldToScreenCoordinates(world);
        if (!screen) return;
        if (screen.x < -40 || screen.y < -40 || screen.x > canvas.width + 40 || screen.y > canvas.height + 40) return;
        const r = 9 + (affordable ? pulse * 5 : 0);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(screen.x, screen.y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = fill;
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = stroke;
        ctx.lineWidth = affordable ? 2.5 : 2;
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(screen.x - 5, screen.y);
        ctx.lineTo(screen.x + 5, screen.y);
        ctx.moveTo(screen.x, screen.y - 5);
        ctx.lineTo(screen.x, screen.y + 5);
        ctx.stroke();
        const label = tile.label ? String(tile.label) : "";
        if (label) {
          const tw = ctx.measureText(label).width;
          const ly = screen.y - r - 3;
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.62)";
          ctx.fillRect(screen.x - tw / 2 - 3, ly - 14, tw + 6, 15);
          ctx.fillStyle = stroke;
          ctx.fillText(label, screen.x, ly);
        }
      });
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureCanvas();
        ensureBanner();
        if (rafHandle === null) rafHandle = requestAnimationFrame(render);
      },
      // Vom Lead pro Recalc gepusht. suggestion = { affordableNow, unit, tiles:[{world:{x,y},label}] }.
      setData(next) {
        suggestion = next && typeof next === "object" ? next : null;
      },
      reset() {
        suggestion = null;
        clearCanvas();
        if (banner) banner.style.display = "none";
      },
      destroy() {
        if (rafHandle !== null) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(rafHandle);
          rafHandle = null;
        }
        suggestion = null;
        window.removeEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
        if (canvas) canvas.remove();
        canvas = null;
        ctx = null;
        if (banner) banner.remove();
        banner = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/automation/land-acquisition-controller.js
  function createLandAcquisition({ autoExpand, autoFarm, autoBoat, getTroopEconomy, settings }) {
    let status = { state: "idle", reason: "not_started", mode: null };
    function humansAllowed(economy) {
      return !!(economy && (economy.safePush || economy.state === "PUSH" || economy.state === "CAP_WASTE"));
    }
    function runFarm(farmRecommendations, expansion, options, allowHumans) {
      const recs = allowHumans ? farmRecommendations : (farmRecommendations || []).filter((rec) => !rec.isHuman);
      autoFarm?.run(recs, expansion, options);
    }
    function unify(allowHumans) {
      const farm = autoFarm?.getStatus?.() || null;
      const boat = autoBoat?.getStatus?.() || null;
      const expand = autoExpand?.getStatus?.() || null;
      let mode = "idle";
      if (farm?.state === "sent") mode = "farm";
      else if (boat?.state === "sent") mode = "boat";
      else if (expand?.state === "sent") mode = "expand";
      status = {
        state: mode === "idle" ? "idle" : "sent",
        reason: mode === "idle" ? "no_action" : \`land_\${mode}\`,
        mode,
        allowHumans,
        farm,
        boat,
        expand
      };
      return status;
    }
    return {
      reset() {
        status = { state: "idle", reason: "reset", mode: null };
      },
      getStatus() {
        return status;
      },
      // context: { farmRecommendations, expansion, troopInfo }
      // options: { force, skipFarm, fastFarmOnly } \u2014 force kommt vom Autopilot, skipFarm bei Pause
      //          (z. B. Bedrohungsdruck), fastFarmOnly vom 250ms-Target-Loop.
      run(context = {}, options = {}) {
        const { farmRecommendations, expansion, troopInfo } = context;
        const force = !!options.force;
        const farmOn = force || settings.get("autoFarm");
        const expandOn = force || settings.get("autoExpand");
        const boatOn = force || settings.get("autoBoat");
        if (!farmOn && !expandOn && !boatOn) {
          status = { state: "idle", reason: "disabled", mode: null };
          return status;
        }
        const economy = getTroopEconomy?.();
        const allowHumans = humansAllowed(economy);
        if (options.fastFarmOnly) {
          if (farmOn) runFarm(farmRecommendations, expansion, options, allowHumans);
          return unify(allowHumans);
        }
        if (boatOn) autoBoat?.run(options);
        if (farmOn && !options.skipFarm) runFarm(farmRecommendations, expansion, options, allowHumans);
        const farmFired = autoFarm?.getStatus?.()?.state === "sent";
        if (expandOn && !farmFired) autoExpand?.run(troopInfo, options);
        return unify(allowHumans);
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/advisor/trade-advisor.js
  var SHORT_RANGE_DEBUFF = 300;
  var GOLD_SIGMOID_MAX = 75e3;
  var GOLD_SIGMOID_K = 0.03;
  var GOLD_PER_TILE = 50;
  function tradeShipGoldEstimate(dist, goldMultiplier = 1) {
    const d = Math.max(0, finiteOrZero(dist));
    const base = GOLD_SIGMOID_MAX / (1 + Math.exp(-GOLD_SIGMOID_K * (d - SHORT_RANGE_DEBUFF))) + GOLD_PER_TILE * d;
    return Math.floor(base * (goldMultiplier || 1));
  }
  function proximityBonusPortsNb(totalPorts) {
    return Math.max(4, Math.min(totalPorts, Math.round(totalPorts / 3)));
  }
  function manhattan(a, b) {
    return Math.abs(a.x - b.x) + Math.abs(a.y - b.y);
  }
  function evaluateTradeAdvice({ gameView, myState, teamDetection, hasCoastline = false } = {}) {
    if (!gameView || !myState) return null;
    let goldMultiplier = 1;
    try {
      const config = typeof gameView.config === "function" ? gameView.config() : null;
      if (config && typeof config.goldMultiplier === "function") {
        const m = Number(config.goldMultiplier());
        if (Number.isFinite(m) && m > 0) goldMultiplier = m;
      }
    } catch (_) {
    }
    const ports = scanUnitsByType(gameView, UNIT.PORT, myState, teamDetection);
    const mine = ports.filter((p) => p.isMine && !p.underConstruction && p.coords);
    const partners = ports.filter((p) => !p.isMine && !p.underConstruction && p.coords);
    if (!hasCoastline && mine.length === 0) return null;
    if (partners.length === 0) {
      return {
        relevant: true,
        myPortCount: mine.length,
        partnerPortCount: 0,
        goldMultiplier,
        recommendation: {
          tone: "hint",
          text: mine.length > 0 ? t("trade.noPartners.hasPort") : t("trade.noPartners.noPort")
        }
      };
    }
    const K = Math.min(partners.length, proximityBonusPortsNb(partners.length));
    let routeGoldSum = 0;
    let routeCount = 0;
    let shortRoutePorts = 0;
    mine.forEach((port) => {
      const dists = partners.map((pp) => manhattan(port.coords, pp.coords)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const nearestK = dists.slice(0, K);
      nearestK.forEach((d) => {
        routeGoldSum += tradeShipGoldEstimate(d, goldMultiplier);
        routeCount += 1;
      });
      if (dists[0] != null && dists[0] < SHORT_RANGE_DEBUFF) shortRoutePorts += 1;
    });
    const avgGoldPerTrip = routeCount > 0 ? Math.round(routeGoldSum / routeCount) : 0;
    let bestDist = 0;
    let bestPartner = null;
    const anchor = mine[0] ? mine[0].coords : null;
    partners.forEach((pp) => {
      const d = anchor ? manhattan(anchor, pp.coords) : 0;
      if (d > bestDist) {
        bestDist = d;
        bestPartner = pp;
      }
    });
    const bestRouteGold = tradeShipGoldEstimate(bestDist, goldMultiplier);
    const recommendation = buildRecommendation({
      myPortCount: mine.length,
      partnerPortCount: partners.length,
      avgGoldPerTrip,
      bestRouteGold,
      bestPartnerName: bestPartner ? bestPartner.ownerName : null,
      shortRoutePorts
    });
    return {
      relevant: true,
      myPortCount: mine.length,
      partnerPortCount: partners.length,
      goldMultiplier,
      avgGoldPerTrip,
      bestRouteGold,
      bestPartnerName: bestPartner ? bestPartner.ownerName : null,
      shortRoutePorts,
      recommendation
    };
  }
  function buildRecommendation({ myPortCount, partnerPortCount, avgGoldPerTrip, bestRouteGold, bestPartnerName, shortRoutePorts }) {
    if (myPortCount === 0) {
      return {
        tone: "hint",
        text: t("trade.noPort", { best: gold(bestRouteGold) })
      };
    }
    if (shortRoutePorts >= Math.ceil(myPortCount / 2) && avgGoldPerTrip < bestRouteGold * 0.6) {
      return {
        tone: "warn",
        text: bestPartnerName ? t("trade.shortRoutes.withPartner", { avg: gold(avgGoldPerTrip), partner: bestPartnerName, best: gold(bestRouteGold) }) : t("trade.shortRoutes", { avg: gold(avgGoldPerTrip), best: gold(bestRouteGold) })
      };
    }
    if (bestRouteGold >= avgGoldPerTrip * 1.5) {
      return {
        tone: "hint",
        text: bestPartnerName ? t("trade.potential.withPartner", { avg: gold(avgGoldPerTrip), count: myPortCount, partner: bestPartnerName, best: gold(bestRouteGold) }) : t("trade.potential", { avg: gold(avgGoldPerTrip), count: myPortCount, best: gold(bestRouteGold) })
      };
    }
    return {
      tone: "ok",
      text: t("trade.good", { avg: gold(avgGoldPerTrip), count: partnerPortCount })
    };
  }
  function gold(value) {
    const num = finiteOrZero(value);
    if (num >= 1e6) return \`\${Math.round(num / 1e5) / 10}M\`;
    if (num >= 1e3) return \`\${Math.round(num / 100) / 10}k\`;
    return String(Math.round(num));
  }

  // src/page/ui/right-click-overlay.js
  var MENU_ID = "ofat-rightclick-menu";
  var Z_INDEX7 = 100002;
  var RADIAL_RADIUS_FALLBACK = 150;
  var MENU_GAP = 12;
  function createRightClickOverlay({ settings, getViewModel, assistActions, autoAlliance, teamDetection }) {
    let menu = null;
    let current = null;
    function ensureMenu() {
      if (menu) return;
      menu = document.createElement("div");
      menu.id = MENU_ID;
      menu.style.position = "fixed";
      menu.style.zIndex = String(Z_INDEX7);
      menu.style.display = "none";
      menu.style.minWidth = "168px";
      menu.style.padding = "6px";
      menu.style.background = "rgba(8,10,13,0.94)";
      menu.style.border = "1px solid rgba(113,191,255,0.4)";
      menu.style.borderRadius = "7px";
      menu.style.boxShadow = "0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)";
      menu.style.font = "12px/1.35 system-ui, sans-serif";
      menu.style.color = "#f4f7fb";
      document.body.appendChild(menu);
      menu.addEventListener("click", onMenuClick);
      menu.addEventListener("contextmenu", (event) => event.stopPropagation());
      document.addEventListener("click", hide, true);
      document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        if (event.key === "Escape") hide();
      });
      window.addEventListener("blur", hide);
    }
    function hide() {
      if (menu) menu.style.display = "none";
      current = null;
    }
    function positionMenu(clientX, clientY) {
      menu.style.display = "block";
      const w = menu.offsetWidth || 180;
      const h = menu.offsetHeight || 120;
      let radialRect = null;
      const radial = document.querySelector(".radial-menu-container");
      if (radial) {
        const rect = radial.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (rect && rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0) radialRect = rect;
      }
      const rightEdge = radialRect ? radialRect.right : clientX + RADIAL_RADIUS_FALLBACK;
      let left = rightEdge + MENU_GAP;
      if (left + w > window.innerWidth - 8) {
        const leftEdge = radialRect ? radialRect.left : clientX - RADIAL_RADIUS_FALLBACK;
        left = Math.max(8, leftEdge - w - MENU_GAP);
      }
      let top = clientY - h / 2;
      top = Math.max(8, Math.min(top, window.innerHeight - h - 8));
      menu.style.left = \`\${left}px\`;
      menu.style.top = \`\${top}px\`;
    }
    function resolveTarget2(clientX, clientY) {
      const tile = screenToTile(clientX, clientY);
      if (tile == null) return null;
      let gameView = null;
      try {
        gameView = getGameView();
      } catch (_) {
      }
      if (!gameView || typeof gameView.owner !== "function") return null;
      let owner = null;
      try {
        owner = gameView.owner(tile);
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
      if (!owner || typeof owner.isPlayer === "function" && !owner.isPlayer()) return null;
      const id = typeof owner.id === "function" ? owner.id() : owner.id;
      let name = "";
      try {
        name = typeof owner.displayName === "function" && owner.displayName() || typeof owner.name === "function" && owner.name() || "";
      } catch (_) {
      }
      if (id == null && !name) return null;
      return { id, name: name || String(id) };
    }
    function findRec(vm, target) {
      const recs = vm && vm.farmRecommendations || [];
      return recs.find((rec) => rec.id === target.id) || recs.find((rec) => rec.name === target.name) || null;
    }
    function onContextMenu(event) {
      if (!settings.get("rightClickMenu")) return;
      const target = resolveTarget2(event.clientX, event.clientY);
      if (!target) {
        hide();
        return;
      }
      const vm = getViewModel?.() || null;
      const rec = findRec(vm, target);
      const isTeammate = !!teamDetection?.isMyTeammate?.(target.id);
      current = { id: target.id, name: target.name, rec };
      ensureMenu();
      menu.innerHTML = renderMenu(target, rec, isTeammate);
      const { clientX, clientY } = event;
      positionMenu(clientX, clientY);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (menu && menu.style.display === "block" && current && current.id === target.id) positionMenu(clientX, clientY);
      });
    }
    function captureAssessment(rec, isTeammate) {
      if (!rec) return { has: false, attackable: false, reason: t("rc.reason.not_neighbor") };
      const pct = rec.captureRating && Number.isFinite(rec.captureRating.score) ? Math.round(rec.captureRating.score * 100) : null;
      const tier = rec.captureRating?.tier;
      const tierLabel = tier === "easy" ? t("rc.tier.easy") : tier === "hard" ? t("rc.tier.hard") : t("rc.tier.medium");
      const dp = rec.captureRating?.defensePosts || 0;
      const reserveOk = !(Number.isFinite(rec.reserveAfterRatio) && Number.isFinite(rec.appliedReserveRatio)) || rec.reserveAfterRatio >= rec.appliedReserveRatio;
      const isFarmTarget = rec.status === "farm" || rec.status === "target";
      const attackable = isFarmTarget && Number(rec.suggestedTroops) > 0 && reserveOk && !isTeammate;
      let reason = null;
      if (!attackable) {
        if (isTeammate) reason = t("rc.reason.teammate");
        else if (rec.status === "danger") reason = t("rc.reason.too_strong");
        else if (rec.status === "hold") reason = t("rc.reason.not_worthwhile");
        else if (!reserveOk) reason = t("rc.reason.reserve_low");
        else if (rec.status === "wait" || rec.status === "mark") reason = t("rc.reason.not_yet");
        else reason = t("rc.reason.no_attack_now");
      }
      return { has: true, attackable, pct, tierLabel, dp, optimal: Math.floor(Number(rec.suggestedTroops) || 0), reason };
    }
    function renderMenu(target, rec, isTeammate) {
      const a = captureAssessment(rec, isTeammate);
      let html = \`<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:5px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);padding-bottom:4px">\${escapeHtml(target.name)}</div>\`;
      if (a.has) {
        const dpTxt = a.dp > 0 ? \` \\u{1F6E1}\${a.dp}\` : "";
        html += \`<div style="margin-bottom:5px">\${escapeHtml(t("rc.assess.label"))} <b>\${a.pct == null ? "?" : a.pct + "%"}</b> \${escapeHtml(a.tierLabel)}\${escapeHtml(dpTxt)}</div>\`;
      }
      if (a.attackable) {
        html += \`<button data-rc="attack" style="\${btnStyle()}">\${escapeHtml(t("rc.attack", { troops: formatTroops(a.optimal) }))}</button>\`;
      } else if (a.reason) {
        html += \`<div style="opacity:0.8;margin-bottom:5px;color:#ffc9c2">\\u2716 \${escapeHtml(a.reason)}</div>\`;
      }
      html += \`<div data-rc-status style="margin:3px 0;color:#ffc9c2;display:none"></div>\`;
      if (target.id != null && !isTeammate) {
        const policy = autoAlliance?.getAlliancePolicy?.(target.id) || null;
        html += \`<button data-rc="whitelist" style="\${btnStyle(policy === "whitelist")}">\${escapeHtml(t("rc.whitelist"))}</button>\`;
        html += \`<button data-rc="blacklist" style="\${btnStyle(policy === "blacklist")}">\${escapeHtml(t("rc.blacklist"))}</button>\`;
        if (policy) html += \`<button data-rc="clear" style="\${btnStyle()}">\${escapeHtml(t("rc.clear_policy"))}</button>\`;
      }
      return html;
    }
    function showMenuStatus(text) {
      const node = menu && menu.querySelector("[data-rc-status]");
      if (!node) return;
      node.textContent = \`\\u2716 \${text}\`;
      node.style.display = "block";
    }
    function onMenuClick(event) {
      const button = event.target.closest("button[data-rc]");
      if (!button || !current) return;
      event.stopPropagation();
      const action = button.dataset.rc;
      if (action === "attack" && current.rec) {
        const result = assistActions?.sendManualAssistAttack?.(current.rec);
        if (result && result.ok) {
          hide();
        } else {
          showMenuStatus(reasonText2(result && result.reason || "unknown"));
        }
        return;
      }
      if (action === "whitelist") autoAlliance?.setAlliancePolicy?.(current.id, "whitelist");
      else if (action === "blacklist") autoAlliance?.setAlliancePolicy?.(current.id, "blacklist");
      else if (action === "clear") autoAlliance?.setAlliancePolicy?.(current.id, "clear");
      hide();
    }
    return {
      install() {
        ensureMenu();
        document.addEventListener("contextmenu", onContextMenu);
      },
      reset() {
        hide();
      },
      destroy() {
        hide();
        document.removeEventListener("contextmenu", onContextMenu);
        if (menu) menu.remove();
        menu = null;
      }
    };
  }
  function reasonText2(reason) {
    const map = {
      reserve_limit: "rc.reason.reserve_limit",
      target_allied: "rc.reason.target_allied",
      target_team: "rc.reason.target_team",
      socket_closed: "rc.reason.socket_closed",
      websocket_unavailable: "rc.reason.socket_closed",
      not_alive_or_not_started: "rc.reason.not_alive_or_not_started",
      target_not_alive: "rc.reason.target_not_alive",
      no_troops: "rc.reason.no_troops",
      human_blocked: "rc.reason.human_blocked",
      no_target: "rc.reason.no_target"
    };
    if (map[reason]) return t(map[reason]);
    if (typeof reason === "string" && reason.indexOf("status_") === 0) return t("rc.reason.status_blocked");
    return t("rc.reason.unknown");
  }
  function btnStyle(active = false) {
    return \`display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;margin:3px 0;padding:5px 7px;border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14);background:\${active ? "rgba(46,125,50,0.6)" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)"};color:#f4f7fb;font:inherit\`;
  }
  function formatTroops(value) {
    const num = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(num) || num <= 0) return "0";
    if (num >= 1e6) return \`\${Math.round(num / 1e5) / 10}M\`;
    if (num >= 1e3) return \`\${Math.round(num / 100) / 10}k\`;
    return String(Math.round(num));
  }

  // src/page/ui/retaliation-hud.js
  var HUD_ID = "ofat-retaliation-hud";
  var POLL_MS = 600;
  function createRetaliationHud({ settings, getPlan, onApprove }) {
    let el = null;
    let timer = null;
    function ensureEl() {
      if (el) return;
      el = document.createElement("div");
      el.id = HUD_ID;
      el.style.position = "fixed";
      el.style.left = "50%";
      el.style.bottom = "96px";
      el.style.transform = "translateX(-50%)";
      el.style.zIndex = "100002";
      el.style.display = "none";
      el.style.maxWidth = "min(520px, calc(100vw - 24px))";
      el.style.padding = "9px 11px";
      el.style.background = "rgba(40,8,8,0.92)";
      el.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,120,110,0.6)";
      el.style.borderRadius = "8px";
      el.style.boxShadow = "0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)";
      el.style.font = "12px/1.35 system-ui, sans-serif";
      el.style.color = "#ffe3df";
      document.body.appendChild(el);
      el.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        const button = event.target.closest("button[data-retaliate]");
        if (!button) return;
        if (button.dataset.retaliate === "fire") onApprove?.();
        hide();
      });
    }
    function hide() {
      if (el) el.style.display = "none";
    }
    function tick() {
      if (!settings.get("autoWeapons")) {
        hide();
        return;
      }
      const plan = typeof getPlan === "function" && getPlan() || null;
      const salvo = plan && Array.isArray(plan.salvo) ? plan.salvo : [];
      if (!salvo.length) {
        hide();
        return;
      }
      ensureEl();
      const targets = salvo.slice(0, 3).map((shot) => \`\${escapeHtml(String(shot.unit || "?"))}\\u2192\${escapeHtml(String(shot.targetName || shot.targetID || "?"))}\`).join(", ");
      const incomingCount = plan.incoming && plan.incoming.length ? plan.incoming.length : 0;
      const incomingTxt = incomingCount ? \` \\xB7 \${escapeHtml(t("ret.incoming", { n: incomingCount }))}\` : "";
      const titleTxt = escapeHtml(tp("ret.title.one", "ret.title.many", salvo.length, { count: salvo.length }));
      el.innerHTML = \`<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:5px">\${titleTxt}\${incomingTxt}</div><div style="opacity:0.9;margin-bottom:6px">\${targets}</div><button data-retaliate="fire" style="margin-right:6px;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);background:rgba(183,28,28,0.85);color:#fff;font:inherit;font-weight:700">\${escapeHtml(t("ret.fire"))}</button><button data-retaliate="dismiss" style="padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);color:#fff;font:inherit">\${escapeHtml(t("ret.dismiss"))}</button>\`;
      el.style.display = "block";
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureEl();
        if (!timer) timer = setInterval(tick, POLL_MS);
      },
      reset() {
        hide();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (timer) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          timer = null;
        }
        hide();
        if (el) el.remove();
        el = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/page/advisor/game-clock.js
  var DEFAULT_TICKS_PER_SECOND = 10;
  function createGameClock({ getGameView: getGameView2, settings, roundLogger, logger }) {
    let fired = false;
    let alert = null;
    function ticksPerSecond(gameView) {
      try {
        const config = gameView && typeof gameView.config === "function" ? gameView.config() : null;
        const dur = config && typeof config.tickDuration === "function" ? config.tickDuration() : null;
        if (Number.isFinite(dur) && dur > 0) return 1e3 / dur;
      } catch (_) {
      }
      return DEFAULT_TICKS_PER_SECOND;
    }
    return {
      // Pro Recalc aufrufen. Gibt das Alarm-Objekt zur\xFCck, wenn gerade ausgel\xF6st, sonst null.
      tick() {
        if (!settings.get("combatTimeAlertEnabled")) return null;
        if (fired) return null;
        let gameView = null;
        let ticks = null;
        try {
          gameView = getGameView2?.();
          if (!gameView || typeof gameView.ticks !== "function") return null;
          ticks = gameView.ticks();
        } catch (_) {
          return null;
        }
        if (!Number.isFinite(ticks)) return null;
        const seconds = ticks / ticksPerSecond(gameView);
        const threshold = Number(settings.get("combatTimeAlertThreshold"));
        if (!Number.isFinite(threshold) || threshold <= 0) return null;
        if (seconds < threshold) return null;
        fired = true;
        alert = { seconds: Math.round(seconds), thresholdSeconds: threshold, text: formatAlert(threshold) };
        try {
          logger?.info?.(\`Game-time alert: reached \${formatClock(seconds)} (threshold \${formatClock(threshold)})\`);
        } catch (_) {
        }
        try {
          roundLogger?.record?.("game_time_alert", { seconds: alert.seconds, thresholdSeconds: threshold, ticks });
        } catch (_) {
        }
        return alert;
      },
      // Aktuelles Alarm-Objekt (null bis ausgel\xF6st) \u2014 f\xFCr die Anzeige im control-hud.
      getAlert() {
        return alert;
      },
      reset() {
        fired = false;
        alert = null;
      }
    };
  }
  function formatAlert(thresholdSeconds) {
    return t("clock.alert", { time: formatClock(thresholdSeconds) });
  }
  function formatClock(totalSeconds) {
    const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(totalSeconds) || 0));
    const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
    const rem = s % 60;
    return \`\${m}:\${String(rem).padStart(2, "0")}\`;
  }

  // src/page/logging/log-export.js
  function downloadJson(filename, data) {
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.download = filename;
    link.style.display = "none";
    document.documentElement.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1e3);
  }
  function createRoundLogFilename(startedAt = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date()) {
    const stamp = [
      startedAt.getFullYear(),
      pad2(startedAt.getMonth() + 1),
      pad2(startedAt.getDate()),
      "-",
      pad2(startedAt.getHours()),
      pad2(startedAt.getMinutes()),
      pad2(startedAt.getSeconds())
    ].join("");
    return \`openfront-round-log-\${stamp}.json\`;
  }
  function pad2(value) {
    return String(value).padStart(2, "0");
  }

  // src/page/logging/log-sanitize.js

    };
  }
  function summarizeSettings(settings) {
