  function summarizeSnapshotPlayers(gameState, keepNames, options = {}) {
    const limit = Math.max(10, Number(options.limit) || 80);
    const myPlayerID = options.myPlayerID ?? gameState.state.myPlayerID;
    const { players } = collectPlayerSummaries(gameState, keepNames);
    const selected = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    players.forEach((player) => {
      if (player.id === myPlayerID) selected.set(player.id, player);
    });
    players.forEach((player) => {
      if (player.outgoingAttackCount > 0) selected.set(player.id, player);
    });
    players.forEach((player) => {
      if (selected.size >= limit) return;
      if (player.isAlive) selected.set(player.id, player);
    });
    return {
      players: Array.from(selected.values()).sort(sortPlayerSummary),
      omittedPlayerCount: Math.max(0, players.length - selected.size),
      playerSampleLimit: limit
    };
  }
  function collectPlayerSummaries(gameState, keepNames) {
    const players = [];
    gameState.state.playerStates.forEach((player) => {
      if (!player) return;
      players.push({
        id: player.id,
        name: keepNames ? player.name || player.id : player.id,
        troops: finiteOrZero(player.troops),
        tilesOwned: finiteOrZero(player.tilesOwned),
        gold: finiteOrZero(player.gold),
        isAlive: player.isAlive,
        playerType: player.playerType,
        allianceCount: Array.isArray(player.alliances) ? player.alliances.length : 0,
        outgoingAttackCount: Array.isArray(player.outgoingAttacks) ? player.outgoingAttacks.filter((attack) => attack && !attack.retreating).length : 0
      });
    });
    return { players: players.sort(sortPlayerSummary) };
  }
  function sortPlayerSummary(a, b) {
    if (b.tilesOwned !== a.tilesOwned) return b.tilesOwned - a.tilesOwned;
    return b.troops - a.troops;
  }
  function summarizeMyState(gameState, keepNames) {
    const player = gameState.getMyState();
    if (!player) return null;
    return {
      id: player.id,
      name: keepNames ? player.name || player.id : player.id,
      troops: finiteOrZero(player.troops),
      tilesOwned: finiteOrZero(player.tilesOwned),
      gold: finiteOrZero(player.gold),
      isAlive: player.isAlive,
      allianceCount: Array.isArray(player.alliances) ? player.alliances.length : 0,
      outgoingAttackCount: Array.isArray(player.outgoingAttacks) ? player.outgoingAttacks.filter((attack) => attack && !attack.retreating).length : 0
    };
  }

    };
  }
  function round3(value) {
    const number = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return 0;
    return Math.round(number * 1e3) / 1e3;
  }

  // src/page/logging/round-logger.js
  var MAX_TIMELINE_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
  var SNAPSHOT_PLAYER_LIMIT = 80;
  var RECENT_ACTION_LIMIT = 5;
  var COMBAT_OUTCOME_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 2500;
  var COMBAT_OUTCOME_MAX_AGE_MS = 45e3;
  var PROTECTED_LOG_TYPES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
    "round_started",
    "round_ended",
    "map_loaded",
    "setting_changed",
    "auto_spawn_sent",
    "auto_alliance_sent",
    "smart_attack_modified",
    "autopilot_action",
    "autopilot_skip",
    "autopilot_state",
    "farm_recommendation",
    "auto_farm_attack_sent",
    "auto_farm_skipped",
    "auto_expand_sent",
    "auto_expand_skipped",
    "auto_eco_sent",
    "auto_eco_skipped",

  function createRoundLogger({ app, settings, gameState, mapDataRef, logger }) {
    let session = null;
    let lastSnapshotAt = 0;
    let lastAdvisorView = null;
    let lastExportAt = null;
    let truncated = false;
    let truncationSummary = null;
    let sequence = 0;
    let recentActions = [];
    const pendingCombatOutcomes = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    let lastCombatOutcomeCheckAt = 0;
    let flushingCombatOutcomes = false;
    let approxBytes = 0;
    return {
      start(reason = "start") {
        if (!settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        if (session && !session.endedAt) this.end("restarted", { autoDownload: false });
        const startedAt = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date();
        session = {
          meta: {
            appName: app.name,
            appVersion: app.version,
            createdAt: startedAt.toISOString(),
            endedAt: null,
            durationMs: null,
            reason,
            truncated: false,
            truncation: null
          },
          settings: summarizeSettings(settings.snapshot()),
          map: summarizeMap(mapDataRef.current),
          timeline: [],
          finalState: null
        };
        truncated = false;
        truncationSummary = null;
        sequence = 0;
        recentActions = [];
        pendingCombatOutcomes.clear();
        lastCombatOutcomeCheckAt = 0;
        approxBytes = 0;
        lastExportAt = null;
        lastSnapshotAt = performance.now();
        push("round_started", {});
        this.snapshot("initial");
      },
      end(reason = "socket_closed", options = {}) {
        if (!session || session.endedAt) return;
        this.snapshot("final");
        flushCombatOutcomes(Date.now() - Date.parse(session.meta.createdAt), gameState.state.currentTick, { final: true });
        const endedAt = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date();
        session.meta.endedAt = endedAt.toISOString();
        session.meta.durationMs = Date.parse(session.meta.endedAt) - Date.parse(session.meta.createdAt);
        session.meta.reason = reason;
        session.meta.truncated = truncated;
        session.meta.truncation = truncationSummary;
        session.finalState = {
          tick: gameState.state.currentTick,
          myState: summarizeMyState(gameState, settings.get("roundLogKeepPlayerNames")),
          advisor: summarizeAdvisorView(lastAdvisorView)
        };
        push("round_ended", { reason });
        const shouldDownload = options.autoDownload ?? settings.get("roundLogAutoDownload");
        if (shouldDownload && settings.get("roundLogging")) this.export("auto");
      },
      record(type, data = {}) {
        if (!session || session.endedAt || !settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        push(type, data);
      },
      recordSettingChange(key, value) {
        if (!session || session.endedAt || !settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        push("setting_changed", { key, value });
      },
      recordMapLoaded(mapData) {
        if (!settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        if (session) session.map = summarizeMap(mapData);
        push("map_loaded", summarizeMap(mapData));
      },
      recordAdvisor(viewModel) {
        lastAdvisorView = viewModel;
        if (!session || session.endedAt || !settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        push("advisor", summarizeAdvisorView(viewModel));
      },
      maybeSnapshot() {
        if (!session || session.endedAt || !settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        const now = performance.now();
        const interval = Number(settings.get("roundLogSnapshotIntervalMs")) || 5e3;
        if (now - lastSnapshotAt < interval) return;
        lastSnapshotAt = now;
        this.snapshot("interval");
      },
      snapshot(reason = "manual") {
        if (!session || session.endedAt || !settings.get("roundLogging")) return;
        const playerSnapshot = summarizeSnapshotPlayers(gameState, settings.get("roundLogKeepPlayerNames"), {
          myPlayerID: gameState.state.myPlayerID,
          limit: SNAPSHOT_PLAYER_LIMIT
        });
        push("snapshot", {
          reason,
          myClientID: gameState.state.myClientID,
          myPlayerID: gameState.state.myPlayerID,
          playerCount: gameState.state.playerStates.size,
          omittedPlayerCount: playerSnapshot.omittedPlayerCount,
          playerSampleLimit: playerSnapshot.playerSampleLimit,
          players: playerSnapshot.players
        });
      },
      export(reason = "manual") {
        if (!session) {
          logger.warn("No round log session to export");
          return;
        }
        const payload = {
          ...session,
          meta: {
            ...session.meta,
            exportedAt: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
            exportReason: reason,
            truncated,
            truncation: truncationSummary
          }
        };
        downloadJson(createRoundLogFilename(new Date(session.meta.createdAt)), payload);
        lastExportAt = payload.meta.exportedAt;
      },
      clear() {
        session = null;
        lastAdvisorView = null;
        lastSnapshotAt = 0;
        lastExportAt = null;
        truncated = false;
        truncationSummary = null;
        sequence = 0;
        recentActions = [];
        pendingCombatOutcomes.clear();
        lastCombatOutcomeCheckAt = 0;
        approxBytes = 0;
        logger.info("Round log cleared");
      },
      hasSession() {
        return !!session;
      },
      getStatus() {
        return {
          active: !!session && !session.endedAt,
          hasSession: !!session,
          eventCount: session ? session.timeline.length : 0,
          truncated,
          lastExportAt,
          endedAt: session?.meta?.endedAt || null
        };
      },
      getRecentActions() {
        return recentActions.slice();
      }
    };
    function entrySize(entry) {
      return JSON.stringify(entry).length + 1;
    }
    function push(type, data) {
      if (!session) return;
      const entry = {
        seq: ++sequence,
        timeMs: Date.now() - Date.parse(session.meta.createdAt),
        tick: gameState.state.currentTick,
        type,
        data
      };
      session.timeline.push(entry);
      approxBytes += entrySize(entry);
      captureRecentAction(entry);
      trackCombatModelSend(entry);
      enforceTimelineLimit();
      maybeFlushCombatOutcomes(entry);
    }
    function enforceTimelineLimit() {
      if (!session || approxBytes <= MAX_TIMELINE_BYTES) return;
      const removableTypes = ["network_request", "advisor", "snapshot", "log_truncated"];
      let removed = 0;
      const dropMatching = (predicate) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < session.timeline.length && approxBytes > MAX_TIMELINE_BYTES; ) {
          if (predicate(session.timeline[i])) {
            approxBytes -= entrySize(session.timeline[i]);
            session.timeline.splice(i, 1);
            removed += 1;
          } else {
            i += 1;
          }
        }
      };
      for (const removableType of removableTypes) {
        if (approxBytes <= MAX_TIMELINE_BYTES) break;
        dropMatching((entry) => entry.type === removableType);
      }
      if (approxBytes > MAX_TIMELINE_BYTES) {
        dropMatching((entry) => !PROTECTED_LOG_TYPES.has(entry.type));
      }
      if (removed === 0) return;
      truncated = true;
      truncationSummary = {
        removedEntries: (truncationSummary?.removedEntries || 0) + removed,
        events: (truncationSummary?.events || 0) + 1,
        maxTimelineBytes: MAX_TIMELINE_BYTES,
        lastTimeMs: Date.now() - Date.parse(session.meta.createdAt),
        lastTick: gameState.state.currentTick
      };
      session.meta.truncated = true;
      session.meta.truncation = truncationSummary;
    }
    function captureRecentAction(entry) {
      const action = summarizeRecentAction(entry);
      if (!action) return;
      recentActions.unshift(action);
      recentActions = recentActions.slice(0, RECENT_ACTION_LIMIT);
    }
    function summarizeRecentAction(entry) {
      const data = entry.data || {};
      if (entry.type.endsWith("_sent")) {
        return {
          seq: entry.seq,
          timeMs: entry.timeMs,
          tick: entry.tick,
          type: entry.type,
          tone: entry.type.includes("defense") || entry.type.includes("weapons") ? "warn" : "ready",
          label: recentActionLabel(entry.type),
          text: recentActionText(entry.type, data)
        };
      }
      if (entry.type === "smart_attack_modified" || entry.type === "manual_assist_attack_sent") {
        return {
          seq: entry.seq,
          timeMs: entry.timeMs,
          tick: entry.tick,
          type: entry.type,
          tone: "ready",
          label: recentActionLabel(entry.type),
          text: recentActionText(entry.type, data)
        };
      }
      if (entry.type.endsWith("_skipped") && isImportantSkip(data.reason)) {
        return {
          seq: entry.seq,
          timeMs: entry.timeMs,
          tick: entry.tick,
          type: entry.type,
          tone: "cooldown",
          label: recentActionLabel(entry.type),
          text: String(data.reason || "skipped")
        };
      }
      return null;
    }
    function isImportantSkip(reason) {
      return !!reason && !["cooldown", "no_pressure", "not_ready", "not_started", "disabled", "no_target"].includes(reason);
    }
    function recentActionLabel(type) {
      if (type.includes("farm")) return "Farm";
      if (type.includes("expand")) return "Expand";
      if (type.includes("defense")) return "Defense";
      if (type.includes("boat")) return "Boat";
      if (type.includes("weapons")) return "Weapon";
      if (type.includes("team_support")) return "Team";
      if (type.includes("eco")) return "Eco";
      if (type.includes("spawn")) return "Spawn";
      if (type.includes("alliance")) return "Alliance";
      if (type.includes("quick_chat")) return "SOS";
      if (type.includes("assist")) return "Assist";
      if (type.includes("smart_attack")) return "Attack";
      return "Action";
    }
    function recentActionText(type, data) {
      if (type === "smart_attack_modified") {
        const cap = \`\${formatTroops2(data.originalTroops)}\\u2192\${formatTroops2(data.newTroops)}\`;
        return data.targetName ? \`\${data.targetName} \${cap} cap\` : \`\${cap} cap\`;
      }
      if (data.targetName) return \`\${data.targetName} \${formatTroops2(data.troops)}\`.trim();
      if (data.unit) return data.targetName ? \`\${data.unit} \${data.targetName}\` : String(data.unit);
      if (type.includes("boat") && data.dst != null) return \`tile \${data.dst} \${formatTroops2(data.troops)}\`.trim();
      if (data.troops != null) return formatTroops2(data.troops);
      if (data.actionKey) return String(data.actionKey);
      return String(data.reason || "sent");
    }
    function formatTroops2(value) {
      const number = Number(value);
      if (!Number.isFinite(number) || number <= 0) return "";
      if (number >= 1e6) return \`\${Math.round(number / 1e5) / 10}M\`;
      if (number >= 1e3) return \`\${Math.round(number / 100) / 10}k\`;
      return String(Math.round(number));
    }
    function trackCombatModelSend(entry) {
      if (entry.type !== "auto_farm_attack_sent") return;
      const data = entry.data || {};
      if (data.targetID == null) return;
      pendingCombatOutcomes.set(\`\${entry.seq}:\${data.targetID}\`, {
        seq: entry.seq,
        targetID: data.targetID,
        targetName: data.targetName || data.targetID,
        sentAtMs: entry.timeMs,
        sentTick: entry.tick,
        troops: Math.round(Number(data.troops) || 0),
        initialTargetTroops: Math.round(Number(data.targetTroops) || 0),
        initialTargetTiles: Math.round(Number(data.targetTiles) || 0),
        captureCostEstimate: Math.round(Number(data.captureCostEstimate) || 0),
        officialCaptureCostEstimate: Math.round(Number(data.officialCaptureCostEstimate) || 0),
        officialCaptureTurns: data.officialCaptureTurns || null,
        estimatedWaves: data.estimatedWaves || null,
        sizingMode: data.sizingMode || null
      });
    }
    function maybeFlushCombatOutcomes(entry) {
      if (flushingCombatOutcomes || pendingCombatOutcomes.size === 0) return;
      if (entry.type === "combat_model_outcome") return;
      if (entry.timeMs - lastCombatOutcomeCheckAt < COMBAT_OUTCOME_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS) return;
      flushCombatOutcomes(entry.timeMs, entry.tick);
    }
    function flushCombatOutcomes(timeMs, tick, options = {}) {
      if (flushingCombatOutcomes || pendingCombatOutcomes.size === 0) return;
      flushingCombatOutcomes = true;
      lastCombatOutcomeCheckAt = timeMs;
      try {
        pendingCombatOutcomes.forEach((pending, key) => {
          const ageMs = timeMs - pending.sentAtMs;
          const target = gameState.state.playerStates.get(pending.targetID);
          const targetTroops = target ? Math.round(Number(target.troops) || 0) : 0;
          const targetTiles = target ? Math.round(Number(target.tilesOwned) || 0) : 0;
          let outcome = null;
          if (!target || !target.isAlive || targetTiles <= 0) outcome = "target_gone";
          else if (options.final) outcome = "round_end_alive";
          else if (ageMs >= COMBAT_OUTCOME_MAX_AGE_MS) outcome = "timeout_alive";
          if (!outcome) return;
          pendingCombatOutcomes.delete(key);
          push("combat_model_outcome", {
            outcome,
            sourceSeq: pending.seq,
            elapsedMs: Math.max(0, Math.round(ageMs)),
            elapsedTicks: Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(tick) || 0) - (Number(pending.sentTick) || 0))),
            targetID: pending.targetID,
            targetName: pending.targetName,
            troops: pending.troops,
            initialTargetTroops: pending.initialTargetTroops,
            initialTargetTiles: pending.initialTargetTiles,
            finalTargetTroops: targetTroops,
            finalTargetTiles: targetTiles,
            targetAlive: !!target?.isAlive,
            captureCostEstimate: pending.captureCostEstimate,
            officialCaptureCostEstimate: pending.officialCaptureCostEstimate,
            officialCaptureTurns: pending.officialCaptureTurns,
            estimatedWaves: pending.estimatedWaves,
            sizingMode: pending.sizingMode
          });
        });
      } finally {
        flushingCombatOutcomes = false;
      }
    }
  }

  // src/page/logging/network-logger.js
  function installNetworkMetadataLogger({ settings, roundLogger }) {
    installFetchLogger(settings, roundLogger);
    installXhrLogger(settings, roundLogger);
    installBeaconLogger(settings, roundLogger);
  }
  function installFetchLogger(settings, roundLogger) {
    const origFetch = window.fetch;
    if (typeof origFetch !== "function") return;
    window.fetch = function loggedFetch(input, init) {
      const startedAt = performance.now();
      const requestInfo = getFetchRequestInfo(input, init);
      return origFetch.apply(this, arguments).then(
        (response) => {
          recordNetwork(settings, roundLogger, {
            ...requestInfo,
            status: response.status,
            ok: response.ok,
            durationMs: performance.now() - startedAt,
            responseSize: getResponseSize(response),
            initiator: "fetch"
          });
          return response;
        },
        (error) => {
          recordNetwork(settings, roundLogger, {
            ...requestInfo,
            status: 0,
            ok: false,
            durationMs: performance.now() - startedAt,
            responseSize: 0,
            initiator: "fetch",
            error: error && error.name ? error.name : "FetchError"
          });
          throw error;
        }
      );
    };
  }
  function installXhrLogger(settings, roundLogger) {
    if (!window.XMLHttpRequest || !XMLHttpRequest.prototype) return;
    const origOpen = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open;
    const origSend = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send;
    XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open = function loggedOpen(method, url) {
      this.__ofatNetworkInfo = {
        method: String(method || "GET").toUpperCase(),
        url: sanitizeUrl(url),
        sameOrigin: isSameOrigin(url),
        requestType: classifyUrl(url)
      };
      return origOpen.apply(this, arguments);
    };
    XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send = function loggedSend() {
      const startedAt = performance.now();
      this.addEventListener("loadend", () => {
        const info = this.__ofatNetworkInfo || {};
        recordNetwork(settings, roundLogger, {
          method: info.method || "GET",
          url: info.url || "",
          sameOrigin: !!info.sameOrigin,
          requestType: info.requestType || "other",
          status: this.status || 0,
          ok: this.status >= 200 && this.status < 400,
          durationMs: performance.now() - startedAt,
          responseSize: getXhrResponseSize(this),
          initiator: "xhr"
        });
      });
      return origSend.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }
  function installBeaconLogger(settings, roundLogger) {
    if (!navigator.sendBeacon) return;
    const origSendBeacon = navigator.sendBeacon.bind(navigator);
    navigator.sendBeacon = function loggedSendBeacon(url, data) {
      const startedAt = performance.now();
      const ok = origSendBeacon(url, data);
      recordNetwork(settings, roundLogger, {
        method: "POST",
        url: sanitizeUrl(url),
        sameOrigin: isSameOrigin(url),
        requestType: classifyUrl(url),
        status: ok ? 204 : 0,
        ok,
        durationMs: performance.now() - startedAt,
        responseSize: 0,
        initiator: "beacon"
      });
      return ok;
    };
  }
  function recordNetwork(settings, roundLogger, event) {
    if (!settings.get("networkLogging")) return;
    roundLogger.record("network_request", {
      method: event.method || "GET",
      url: event.url || "",
      status: event.status || 0,
      ok: !!event.ok,
      durationMs: round1(event.durationMs),
      requestType: event.requestType || "other",
      responseSize: event.responseSize || 0,
      sameOrigin: !!event.sameOrigin,
      initiator: event.initiator || "unknown",
      error: event.error || void 0
    });
  }
  function getFetchRequestInfo(input, init) {
    const url = typeof input === "string" ? input : input && input.url;
    const method = init && init.method ? init.method : input && input.method ? input.method : "GET";
    return {
      method: String(method || "GET").toUpperCase(),
      url: sanitizeUrl(url),
      sameOrigin: isSameOrigin(url),
      requestType: classifyUrl(url)
    };
  }
  function sanitizeUrl(value) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(String(value || ""), location.href);
      return \`\${url.origin === location.origin ? "" : url.origin}\${url.pathname}\${url.search ? "?..." : ""}\`;
    } catch (_) {
      return String(value || "").split("?")[0];
    }
  }
  function isSameOrigin(value) {
    try {
      return new URL(String(value || ""), location.href).origin === location.origin;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  function classifyUrl(value) {
    const url = sanitizeUrl(value);
    if (/\\/maps\\//.test(url)) return "map";
    if (/\\/api\\//.test(url)) return "api";
    if (/\\/assets\\/|\\/_assets\\//.test(url)) return "asset";
    if (/\\/analytics|\\/collect|\\/beacon/.test(url)) return "telemetry";
    return "other";
  }
  function getResponseSize(response) {
    const length = response.headers && response.headers.get ? response.headers.get("content-length") : null;
    const parsed = Number(length);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
  }
  function getXhrResponseSize(xhr) {
    const length = xhr.getResponseHeader ? Number(xhr.getResponseHeader("content-length")) : 0;
    if (Number.isFinite(length) && length > 0) return length;
    if (typeof xhr.responseText === "string") return xhr.responseText.length;
    if (xhr.response instanceof ArrayBuffer) return xhr.response.byteLength;
    return 0;
  }
  function round1(value) {
    const number = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return 0;
    return Math.round(number * 10) / 10;
  }

  // src/page/ui/control-hud.js

  function createControlHud({
    app,
    settings,
    roundLogger,
    teamDetection,
    getTroopEconomy,
    getViewModel,
    getAutomationStatus
  }) {
    let el = null;
    let openSections = { A: true, B: false, C: false, D: false, E: false };
    let expertSectionsInitialized = false;
    let farmFineOpen = false;
    let legendOpen = false;
    return {
      create() {
        removeElementById("ofat-control-hud");
        installHudStyles();
        el = document.createElement("div");
        el.id = "ofat-control-hud";
        appendWhenReady(el, "body");
        makeDraggable(el, { storageKey: el.id, settings });
        this.render();
      },
      render() {
        if (!el) return;
        if (!expertSectionsInitialized && settings.get("viewMode") === "expert") {
          openSections = Object.assign({}, openSections, { B: true, C: true });
          expertSectionsInitialized = true;
        }
        const minimized = !!settings.get("hudMinimized");
        const ctx = {
          status: roundLogger.getStatus(),
          team: teamDetection?.isTeamMode ? teamDetection.teamSummary() : null,
          economy: settings.get("showTroopEconomy") ? getTroopEconomy?.() || null : null,
          vm: getViewModel?.() || null,
          automationStatus: getAutomationStatus?.() || {},
          showIntro: !settings.get("seenIntro"),
          minimized,
          openSections,
          farmFineOpen,
          legendOpen,
          viewMode: settings.get("viewMode") || "beginner",
          uiLanguage: settings.get("uiLanguage") || "de"
        };
        el.dataset.minimized = minimized ? "true" : "false";
        el.innerHTML = renderHudHtml(app, settings, ctx);
        const self = this;
        el.querySelectorAll("button[data-setting]").forEach((button) => {
          button.addEventListener("click", () => settings.set(button.dataset.setting, !settings.get(button.dataset.setting)));
        });
        el.querySelectorAll("input[data-setting-range]").forEach((input) => {
          input.addEventListener("input", (event) => {
            const out = el.querySelector(\`[data-range-value="\${event.target.dataset.settingRange}"]\`);
            if (out) out.textContent = event.target.value;
          });
          input.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
            const num = Number(event.target.value);
            if (Number.isFinite(num)) settings.set(event.target.dataset.settingRange, num);
          });
        });
        const mapInfoButton = el.querySelector("[data-map-info]");
        if (mapInfoButton) {
          mapInfoButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const anyOn = MAP_INFO_KEYS.some((key) => settings.get(key));
            MAP_INFO_KEYS.forEach((key) => settings.set(key, !anyOn));
          });
        }
        const minButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='toggle-min']");
        if (minButton) minButton.addEventListener("click", () => settings.set("hudMinimized", !settings.get("hudMinimized")));
        el.querySelectorAll("[data-section-toggle]").forEach((header) => {
          header.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const key = header.dataset.sectionToggle;
            openSections = Object.assign({}, openSections, { [key]: !openSections[key] });
            self.render();
          });
        });
        const farmFineBtn = el.querySelector("[data-action='toggle-farm-fine']");
        if (farmFineBtn) {
          farmFineBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
            farmFineOpen = !farmFineOpen;
            self.render();
          });
        }
        const legendButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='toggle-legend']");
        if (legendButton) {
          legendButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            legendOpen = !legendOpen;
            self.render();
          });
        }
        const introButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='dismiss-intro']");
        if (introButton) introButton.addEventListener("click", () => settings.set("seenIntro", true));
        const findMeButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='find-me']");
        if (findMeButton) findMeButton.addEventListener("click", () => centerCameraOnMe());
        const exportButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='export']");
        if (exportButton) {
          exportButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            roundLogger.export("hud");
            self.render();
          });
        }
        const clearButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='clear']");
        if (clearButton) {
          clearButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            roundLogger.clear();
            self.render();
          });
        }
        const resetPosButton = el.querySelector("[data-action='reset-positions']");
        if (resetPosButton) {
          resetPosButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            settings.set("panelPositions", {});
            POSITION_ELEMENT_IDS.forEach((id) => resetPosition(document.getElementById(id)));
            self.render();
          });
        }
        el.querySelectorAll("[data-lang]").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.addEventListener("click", () => settings.set("uiLanguage", btn.dataset.lang));
        });
        el.querySelectorAll("[data-viewmode]").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const next = btn.dataset.viewmode;
            settings.set("viewMode", next);
            if (next === "expert") {
              openSections = Object.assign({}, openSections, { B: true, C: true });
              expertSectionsInitialized = true;
            }
            self.render();
          });
        });
      }
    };
  }

    </div>

    if (minimized) return html;

      </div>
    \`;

    }
    html += collapsible("A", t("hud.cat.A"), openSections.A, [
      toggle(settings, "showHeatmap"),

      toggle(settings, "showTroopRatios"),
      toggle(settings, "showRatioBar"),
      toggle(settings, "showWeaknessIndicator"),
      toggle(settings, "showDangerIndicator"),
      toggle(settings, "showThreatIcons"),
      toggle(settings, "showAttackBadges"),
      // Weitere Zahlenparameter.
      rangeControl(settings, "autoSpawnDelayMs", { min: 500, max: 1e4, step: 500 }),
      rangeControl(settings, "autoDefenseIncomingRatio", { min: 0.1, max: 0.9, step: 0.05 })
    ];
    html += collapsible("D", t("hud.cat.D"), openSections.D, catDControls, "ofat-hud-section-expert");
    const logState = status.active ? t("hud.log.state.REC") : status.hasSession ? t("hud.log.state.END") : t("hud.log.state.IDLE");
    const logStatusLine = \`<div class="ofat-hud-log-status">\${escapeHtml(t("hud.log.status", { count: String(status.eventCount), state: logState }))}</div>\`;
    const catEControls = [
      logStatusLine,
      toggle(settings, "roundLogging"),
      toggle(settings, "networkLogging"),
      toggle(settings, "roundLogAutoDownload")
    ];
    html += collapsible("E", t("hud.cat.E"), openSections.E, catEControls, "ofat-hud-section-diag");
    if (openSections.E) {
      html += \`
      <div class="ofat-hud-actions">
        <button data-action="export">\${escapeHtml(t("hud.btn.export"))}</button>
        <button data-action="clear">\${escapeHtml(t("hud.btn.clear"))}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ofat-hud-actions">
        <button data-action="reset-positions">\${escapeHtml(t("hud.btn.resetPositions"))}</button>
      </div>
    \`;
    }
    const legendLabel = legendOpen ? t("hud.btn.legendHide") : t("hud.btn.legend");
    html += \`<div class="ofat-hud-legendbar"><button data-action="toggle-legend" title="\${escapeHtml(legendLabel)}">\${escapeHtml(legendLabel)}</button></div>\`;
    if (legendOpen) {
      html += \`
      <div class="ofat-hud-legend">
        <div>\${escapeHtml(t("hud.legend.colors"))}</div>
        <div>\${escapeHtml(t("hud.legend.dot"))}</div>
        <div>\${escapeHtml(t("hud.legend.heatmap"))}</div>
        <div>\${escapeHtml(t("hud.legend.badges"))}</div>
        <div>\${escapeHtml(t("hud.legend.names"))}</div>
        <div>\${escapeHtml(t("hud.legend.sweetspot"))}</div>
      </div>
    \`;
    }
    return html;
  }
  function statusDot(advice, automationStatus) {
    const defense = automationStatus?.autoDefense;
    if (defense?.emergency) return { state: "danger", label: t("hud.dot.danger") };
    if (advice?.tone === "warn") return { state: "danger", label: advice.label || t("hud.dot.danger") };
    if (advice?.tone === "ready" || advice?.tone === "ok") return { state: "ready", label: advice.label || t("hud.dot.ready") };
    return { state: "pending", label: advice?.label || t("hud.dot.pending") };
  }
  function collapsibleRaw(key, titleHtml, open, controls, extraClass) {
    const body = open ? \`<div class="ofat-hud-grid">\${controls.join("")}</div>\` : "";
    const cls = extraClass ? \` \${extraClass}\` : "";
    return \`
    <div class="ofat-hud-section\${cls}" data-open="\${open ? "true" : "false"}">
      <button class="ofat-hud-section-title" data-section-toggle="\${escapeHtml(key)}">
        <span>\${titleHtml}</span><span class="ofat-hud-caret">\${open ? "\\u25BE" : "\\u25B8"}</span>
      </button>
      \${body}
    </div>
  \`;
  }
  function collapsible(key, title, open, controls, extraClass) {
    return collapsibleRaw(key, escapeHtml(title), open, controls, extraClass);
  }
  function subgroupHeader(label) {
    return \`<div class="ofat-hud-subgroup" style="grid-column:1/-1">\${escapeHtml(label)}</div>\`;
  }
  function farmFineAccordion(settings, open) {
    const header = \`<button class="ofat-hud-fine-header" data-action="toggle-farm-fine" style="grid-column:1/-1">\${escapeHtml(t("hud.farm.fine"))} \${open ? "\\u25BE" : "\\u25B8"}</button>\`;
    if (!open) return header;
    return header + [
      rangeControl(settings, "autoFarmWindowMs", { min: 3e4, max: 6e5, step: 3e4 }),
      rangeControl(settings, "autoFarmReserveRatio", { min: 0.2, max: 0.9, step: 0.05 }),
      rangeControl(settings, "autoFarmCooldownMs", { min: 500, max: 1e4, step: 500 }),
      rangeControl(settings, "autoFarmBaseReserveRatio", { min: 0.1, max: 0.6, step: 0.05 }),
      rangeControl(settings, "autoFarmMinReserveRatio", { min: 0.1, max: 0.5, step: 0.05 }),
      rangeControl(settings, "autoFarmMaxReserveRatio", { min: 0.3, max: 0.9, step: 0.05 })
    ].join("");
  }
  function childToggles(settings, parentKey) {
    if (!settings.get(parentKey)) return [];
    return (CHILD_TOGGLES[parentKey] || []).map((childKey) => toggle(settings, childKey, true));
  }
  function mapInfoToggle(settings) {
    const anyOn = MAP_INFO_KEYS.some((key) => settings.get(key));
    const label = settingLabel("mapTroopInfo");
    const desc = toggleDesc("mapTroopInfo");
    const btn = \`<button data-map-info="true" data-on="\${anyOn ? "true" : "false"}" data-warn="false" title="\${escapeHtml(desc ? \`\${label} \\u2014 \${desc}\` : label)}">\${escapeHtml(label)}</button>\`;
    return wrapWithDesc(btn, desc, false);
  }
  function toggleDesc(key) {
    const k = \`toggle.\${key}.desc\`;
    const resolved = t(k);
    return resolved !== k ? resolved : "";
  }
  function wrapWithDesc(buttonHtml, desc, child) {
    if (activeViewMode !== "beginner" || !desc) return buttonHtml;
    const childCls = child ? " ofat-hud-toggle-row-child" : "";
    return \`<div class="ofat-hud-toggle-row\${childCls}">\${buttonHtml}<div class="ofat-hud-toggle-desc">\${escapeHtml(desc)}</div></div>\`;
  }
  function safeColor(value) {
    return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$|^[a-zA-Z]+$|^rgb/.test(String(value || "")) ? value : "#71c7ff";
  }
  function rangeControl(settings, key, { min, max, step }) {
    const raw = Number(settings.get(key));
    const value = Number.isFinite(raw) ? raw : min;
    const label = settingLabel(key);
    return \`<div class="ofat-hud-range" style="grid-column:1/-1"><label>\${escapeHtml(label)}: <span data-range-value="\${escapeHtml(key)}">\${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span></label><input type="range" min="\${min}" max="\${max}" step="\${step}" value="\${value}" data-setting-range="\${escapeHtml(key)}" /></div>\`;
  }
  function toggle(settings, key, child = false) {
    const enabled = !!settings.get(key);
    const warn = SENDING.has(key);
    const label = settingLabel(key);
    const desc = toggleDesc(key);
    const cls = child ? "ofat-hud-child" : "";
    const title = desc ? \`\${label} \\u2014 \${desc}\` : label;
    const btn = \`<button class="\${cls}" data-setting="\${escapeHtml(key)}" data-on="\${enabled ? "true" : "false"}" data-warn="\${warn && enabled ? "true" : "false"}" title="\${escapeHtml(title)}">\${escapeHtml(label)}</button>\`;
    return wrapWithDesc(btn, desc, child);
  }

  // src/page/ui/action-hud.js
  function createActionHud({ settings, getViewModel, getAutomationStatus, getRecentActions }) {
    let el = null;
    return {
      create() {
        removeElementById("ofat-action-hud");
        installActionHudStyles();
        el = document.createElement("div");
        el.id = "ofat-action-hud";
        appendWhenReady(el, "body");
        makeDraggable(el, { storageKey: el.id, settings });
        this.render();
      },
      render() {
        if (!el) return;
        if (!settings.get("showActionHud")) {
          el.style.display = "none";
          el.innerHTML = "";
          return;
        }
        el.style.display = "";
        el.innerHTML = renderActionHudHtml(
          settings,
          getViewModel?.() || null,
          getAutomationStatus?.() || {},
          getRecentActions?.() || []
        );
      }
    };
  }
  function renderActionHudHtml(settings, vm, status, recentActions) {
    const economy = vm?.troopEconomy;
    const expansionLevel = vm?.expansion?.level || "unknown";
    const expansion = expansionLabel(expansionLevel);
    const threat = vm?.topThreats?.[0] ? \`\${vm.topThreats[0].name} \${threatLevelLabel(vm.topThreats[0].level)}\` : t("hud.action.safe");
    const ecoState = economy ? \`\${economyLabel(economy.state)} \${Math.round((economy.currentRatio || 0) * 100)}%\` : t("hud.action.ready");
    const target = (vm?.farmRecommendations || []).find((rec) => rec.status === "farm") || null;
    const support = (vm?.teamSupportRecommendations || [])[0] || null;
    const advice = primaryAdvice(vm, status);
    let html = \`<div class="ofat-action-top">\`;
    html += \`<div class="ofat-action-advice" data-tone="\${escapeHtml(advice.tone)}"><b>\${escapeHtml(advice.label)}</b> \${escapeHtml(advice.text)}</div>\`;
    html += \`</div>\`;
    const trade = vm?.tradeAdvice;
    if (trade && trade.relevant && trade.recommendation && trade.recommendation.tone !== "ok") {
      const tIcon = trade.recommendation.tone === "warn" ? "\\u26A0" : "\\u{1F4A1}";
      html += \`<div class="ofat-action-trade">\${tIcon} \${escapeHtml(trade.recommendation.text)}</div>\`;
    }
    const ready = t("hud.action.ready");
    const hasThreat = !!(vm?.topThreats && vm.topThreats[0]);
    html += \`<div class="ofat-action-row ofat-action-state">\`;
    if (isInteresting(status.autoSpawn)) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.spawn"), summarizeStatus(status.autoSpawn, ready), toneFromStatus(status.autoSpawn));
    if (isEconomyRelevant(economy) || isInteresting(status.autoEco)) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.eco"), ecoState, toneFromStatus(status.autoEco));
    if (isInteresting(status.autoExpand) || expansionLevel === "aggressive") html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.expand"), summarizeStatus(status.autoExpand, expansion), toneFromStatus(status.autoExpand));
    if (target || isInteresting(status.autoFarm)) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.farm"), target ? target.label : summarizeStatus(status.autoFarm, t("hud.action.noTarget")), target ? "ready" : toneFromStatus(status.autoFarm));
    if (status.autoDefense?.emergency || isInteresting(status.autoDefense)) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.defense"), defenseSummary(status.autoDefense), status.autoDefense?.emergency ? "warn" : toneFromStatus(status.autoDefense));
    if (settings.get("autoBoat")) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.boats"), summarizeStatus(status.autoBoat, ready), toneFromStatus(status.autoBoat));
    if (settings.get("autoWeapons")) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.weapons"), weaponsSummary(status.autoWeapons), toneFromStatus(status.autoWeapons));
    if (support || isInteresting(status.autoTeamSupport)) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.team"), support ? \`\${support.name} \${Math.round(support.troops || 0)}\` : summarizeStatus(status.autoTeamSupport, ready), support ? "ready" : toneFromStatus(status.autoTeamSupport));
    if (isInteresting(status.quickChat)) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.sos"), summarizeStatus(status.quickChat, ready), toneFromStatus(status.quickChat));
    if (hasThreat) html += actionPill(t("hud.pill.threat"), threat, "warn");
    html += \`</div>\`;
    if (recentActions.length > 0) {
      html += \`<div class="ofat-action-row ofat-action-recent">\`;
      recentActions.slice(0, 5).forEach((action) => {
        html += actionPill(action.label || t("hud.action.action"), action.text || "", action.tone || "ok");
      });
      html += \`</div>\`;
    }
    return html;
  }
  function expansionLabel(level) {
    const key = \`hud.expansion.\${level}\`;
    const resolved = t(key);
    return resolved !== key ? resolved : level;
  }
  function threatLevelLabel(level) {
    const key = \`hud.threatlevel.\${String(level || "").toLowerCase()}\`;
    const resolved = t(key);
    return resolved !== key ? resolved : String(level || "");
  }
  function actionPill(label, value, tone) {
    return \`<span class="ofat-action-pill" data-tone="\${escapeHtml(tone || "ok")}"><b>\${escapeHtml(label)}</b> \${escapeHtml(String(value || ""))}</span>\`;
  }
  function weaponsSummary(status) {
    if (!status) return t("hud.action.ready");
    if (status.unit) return status.target ? \`\${status.unit}->\${status.target}\` : status.unit;
    return statusText(status, t("hud.action.ready"));
  }
  function defenseSummary(status) {
    if (!status) return t("hud.action.safe");
    if (status.emergency) return status.lastAction || (status.topAttacker ? t("hud.action.attackFrom", { name: status.topAttacker }) : t("hud.action.underAttack"));
    return status.reason === "no_pressure" ? t("hud.action.safe") : statusText(status, t("hud.action.ready"));
  }
  function summarizeStatus(status, fallback) {
    return statusText(status, fallback);
  }
  function isInteresting(status) {
    if (!status) return false;
    if (status.emergency || status.targetName || status.unit || status.lastAction) return true;
    if (status.state === "sent" || status.state === "blocked" || status.state === "cooldown") return true;
    return status.reason && !["not_started", "no_pressure", "reset"].includes(status.reason);
  }
  function toneFromStatus(status) {
    if (!status) return "ok";
    if (status.state === "sent") return "ready";
    if (status.state === "blocked") return "warn";
    if (status.state === "cooldown") return "cooldown";
    return "ok";
  }
  function installActionHudStyles() {
    createStyle(
      "ofat-action-hud-style",
      \`
      #ofat-action-hud {
        position: fixed;
        top: 10px;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        margin: 0 auto;
        z-index: 100001;
        width: min(640px, calc(100vw - 24px));
        cursor: move;
        padding: 8px;
        color: #f4f7fb;
        background: rgba(8, 10, 13, 0.82);
        border: 1px solid rgba(113, 191, 255, 0.34);
        border-radius: 8px;
        box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.24);
        font: 12px/1.3 system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
        user-select: none;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-top {
        display: block;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-advice {
        min-width: 0;
        overflow: hidden;
        text-overflow: ellipsis;
        white-space: nowrap;
        color: #f4f7fb;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-advice[data-tone="warn"] { color: #ffb1a9; }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-advice[data-tone="ready"] { color: #c8f7d1; }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-advice[data-tone="hint"] { color: #ffe0a3; }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-row {
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 6px;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-trade {
        margin-top: 4px;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: #cfe8ff;
        overflow: hidden;
        text-overflow: ellipsis;
        white-space: nowrap;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-state {
        margin-top: 5px;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-state:empty {
        display: none;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-recent {
        margin-top: 5px;
        justify-content: flex-start;
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-pill {
        max-width: 180px;
        overflow: hidden;
        text-overflow: ellipsis;
        white-space: nowrap;
        padding: 3px 7px;
        border-radius: 5px;
        background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
        color: rgba(244, 247, 251, 0.86);
      }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-pill[data-tone="ready"] { background: rgba(46, 125, 50, 0.68); }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-pill[data-tone="warn"] { background: rgba(183, 28, 28, 0.72); }
      #ofat-action-hud .ofat-action-pill[data-tone="cooldown"] { background: rgba(255, 190, 72, 0.35); }
    \`
    );
  }

  // src/page/ui/top-player-lens.js
  var WARMUP_MS = 6e4;
  var collapsed = true;
  var HUMAN_TYPE = "HUMAN";
  function createTopPlayerLens({ getGameState, goldIntel, tilesIntel }) {
    function renderPlayerRow(player, isMe) {
      const name = isMe ? t("lens.row.me") : escapeHtml(player.name || player.displayName || String(player.id));
      const tiles = finiteOrZero(player.tilesOwned);
      const troops = formatTroopCount(finiteOrZero(player.troops));
      const allianceCount = Array.isArray(player.alliances) ? player.alliances.length : 0;
      const goldRate = goldIntel ? goldIntel.mps(player.id) : null;
      const goldRateStr = formatGoldRate(goldRate);
      const rowClass = isMe ? ' class="ofat-lens-me"' : "";
      return \`<tr\${rowClass}><td>\${name}</td><td>\${tiles}</td><td>\${troops}</td><td>\${goldRateStr}</td><td>\${allianceCount}</td></tr>\`;
    }
    function renderTable(me, top3) {
      let html = \`<table class="ofat-lens-table">\`;
      html += \`<thead><tr>\`;
      html += \`<th>\${t("lens.col.name")}</th>\`;
      html += \`<th>\${t("lens.col.tiles")}</th>\`;
      html += \`<th>\${t("lens.col.troops")}</th>\`;
      html += \`<th>\${t("lens.col.goldRate")}</th>\`;
      html += \`<th>\${t("lens.col.alliances")}</th>\`;
      html += \`</tr></thead>\`;
      html += \`<tbody>\`;
      if (me) html += renderPlayerRow(me, true);
      for (let i = 0; i < top3.length; i += 1) {
        html += renderPlayerRow(top3[i], false);
      }
      html += \`</tbody></table>\`;
      return html;
    }
    function renderInsights(me, top3) {
      const insights = [];
      if (top3.length === 0) return "";
      const leader = top3[0];
      const myTiles = me ? finiteOrZero(me.tilesOwned) : 0;
      const leaderTiles = finiteOrZero(leader.tilesOwned);
      const leaderRate = tilesIntel ? tilesIntel.getRate(leader.id) : null;
      const myRate = me && tilesIntel ? tilesIntel.getRate(me.id) : null;
      if (leaderRate != null && myRate != null) {
        const delta = leaderRate - myRate;
        if (delta > 0.2 && leaderTiles > myTiles) {
          insights.push(t("lens.insight.leadExpansion", {
            name: escapeHtml(leader.name || String(leader.id)),
            delta: delta.toFixed(1)
          }));
        } else if (myTiles >= leaderTiles || myRate > leaderRate) {
          insights.push(t("lens.insight.youLeadExpansion"));
        }
      }
      const myAllianceCount = me && Array.isArray(me.alliances) ? me.alliances.length : 0;
      const leaderAllianceCount = Array.isArray(leader.alliances) ? leader.alliances.length : 0;
      if (leaderAllianceCount >= myAllianceCount + 2) {
        insights.push(t("lens.insight.moreAlliances"));
      }
      const outgoing = Array.isArray(leader.outgoingAttacks) ? leader.outgoingAttacks.length : 0;
      if (leaderAllianceCount <= 1 && outgoing >= 2) {
        insights.push(t("lens.insight.soloAggressor", {
          name: escapeHtml(leader.name || String(leader.id)),
          allies: leaderAllianceCount
        }));
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < top3.length; i += 1) {
        const rival = top3[i];
        const rate = goldIntel ? goldIntel.mps(rival.id) : null;
        const myGoldRate = me && goldIntel ? goldIntel.mps(me.id) : null;
        if (rate != null && (myGoldRate == null || rate > myGoldRate * 1.5) && rate > 500) {
          insights.push(t("lens.insight.highGoldEco", {
            name: escapeHtml(rival.name || String(rival.id)),
            rate: Math.round(rate)
          }));
          break;
        }
      }
      if (insights.length === 0) return "";
      let html = \`<div class="ofat-lens-insights">\`;
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(insights.length, 2); i += 1) {
        html += \`<div class="ofat-muted">\${insights[i]}</div>\`;
      }
      html += \`</div>\`;
      return html;
    }
    return {
      // Gibt einen HTML-String zur\xFCck (einklappbare Sektion).
      // Gibt "" zur\xFCck, wenn die Warmup-Phase noch l\xE4uft oder keine Gegner sichtbar sind.
      render(_viewModel) {
        const gameState = getGameState();
        if (!gameState) return "";
        const { roundStartedAtMs, myPlayerID, playerStates } = gameState.state;
        if (!roundStartedAtMs || performance.now() - roundStartedAtMs < WARMUP_MS) return "";
        if (!playerStates || playerStates.size === 0) return "";
        const me = myPlayerID != null ? playerStates.get(myPlayerID) : null;
        const rivals = [];
        playerStates.forEach((player) => {
          if (!player || !player.isAlive) return;
          if (String(player.playerType || "").toUpperCase() !== HUMAN_TYPE) return;
          if (myPlayerID != null && player.id === myPlayerID) return;
          rivals.push(player);
        });
        if (rivals.length === 0) return "";
        rivals.sort((a, b) => finiteOrZero(b.tilesOwned) - finiteOrZero(a.tilesOwned));
        const top3 = rivals.slice(0, 3);
        const toggleKey = collapsed ? "lens.toggle.expand" : "lens.toggle.collapse";
        let html = \`<div class="ofat-section">\`;
        html += \`<div class="ofat-section-title-row">\`;
        html += \`<button id="ofat-lens-toggle" class="ofat-lens-toggle">\${t(toggleKey)}</button>\`;
        html += \`</div>\`;
        if (!collapsed) {
          html += renderTable(me, top3);
          html += renderInsights(me, top3);
        }
        html += \`</div>\`;
        return html;
      },
      // H\xE4ngt den Klick-Handler f\xFCr den Toggle an das \xFCbergebene Root-Element.
      // Muss nach jedem Render aufgerufen werden, da innerHTML neu gesetzt wird.
      attach(rootEl) {
        if (!rootEl) return;
        const btn = rootEl.querySelector("#ofat-lens-toggle");
        if (btn) {
          btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
            collapsed = !collapsed;
          });
        }
      }
    };
  }
  function formatGoldRate(rate) {
    if (rate == null) return t("lens.val.goldRate.unknown");
    const rounded = Math.round(rate);
    if (rounded >= 0) return t("lens.val.goldRate", { rate: rounded });
    return t("lens.val.goldRate.negative", { rate: rounded });
  }

  // src/page/ui/gold-rate-hover.js
  var THROTTLE_MS = 80;
  var Z_INDEX8 = 100002;
  var OWN_UI_SELECTOR = "#ofat-control-hud, #ofat-panel, #ofat-action-hud, #ofat-build-banner";
  function createGoldRateHover({ settings, gameState, goldIntel }) {
    let tip = null;
    let onMove = null;
    let lastAt = 0;
    function ensureTip() {
      if (tip) return;
      removeElementById("ofat-gold-hover");
      createStyle(
        "ofat-gold-hover-style",
        \`#ofat-gold-hover {
        position: fixed; z-index: \${Z_INDEX8}; pointer-events: none; display: none;
        padding: 3px 8px; border-radius: 5px; white-space: nowrap;
        font: 12px/1.25 system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif; color: #06250f;
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, #ffe9a8, #ffd062); border: 1px solid #c9a032;
        box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
      }\`
      );
      tip = document.createElement("div");
      tip.id = "ofat-gold-hover";
      appendWhenReady(tip, "body");
    }
    function hide() {
      if (tip) tip.style.display = "none";
    }
    function update(event) {
      if (event.target && event.target.closest && event.target.closest(OWN_UI_SELECTOR)) return hide();
      const tile = screenToTile(event.clientX, event.clientY);
      if (tile == null) return hide();
      const gameView = getGameView();
      if (!gameView || typeof gameView.owner !== "function") return hide();
      const owner = gameView.owner(tile);
      if (!owner) return hide();
      const isPlayer = typeof owner.isPlayer === "function" ? owner.isPlayer() : true;
      if (!isPlayer) return hide();
      const ownerID = typeof owner.id === "function" ? owner.id() : owner.id;
      if (ownerID == null) return hide();
      const player = resolvePlayer(gameState, ownerID);
      if (!player) return hide();
      const rate = goldIntel.mps(player.id);
      if (rate == null) return hide();
      const perMin = Math.round(rate * 60);
      const sign = perMin >= 0 ? "+" : "\\u2212";
      const name = player.name || player.displayName || String(player.id);
      tip.innerHTML = \`<b>\${escapeHtml(name)}</b> \${sign}\${formatGold3(Math.abs(perMin))} \${escapeHtml(t("goldhover.suffix"))}\`;
      tip.style.left = \`\${event.clientX + 14}px\`;
      tip.style.top = \`\${event.clientY + 14}px\`;
      tip.style.display = "block";
    }
    return {
      start() {
        ensureTip();
        if (onMove) return;
        onMove = (event) => {
          if (!settings.get("showGoldRateHover")) return hide();
          const now = performance.now();
          if (now - lastAt < THROTTLE_MS) return;
          lastAt = now;
          try {
            update(event);
          } catch (_) {
            hide();
          }
        };
        document.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove, { passive: true });
      },
      reset() {
        hide();
      },
      destroy() {
        if (onMove) document.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
        onMove = null;
        removeElementById("ofat-gold-hover");
        tip = null;
      }
    };
  }
  function resolvePlayer(gameState, ownerID) {
    if (!gameState || ownerID == null) return null;
    const direct = gameState.state.playerStates.get(ownerID);
    if (direct) return direct;
    let found = null;
    gameState.state.playerStates.forEach((p) => {
      if (!found && p && p.smallID === ownerID) found = p;
    });
    return found;
  }
  function formatGold3(n) {
    if (n >= 1e6) return \`\${Math.round(n / 1e5) / 10}M\`;
    if (n >= 1e3) return \`\${Math.round(n / 100) / 10}k\`;
    return String(n);
  }

  // src/page/page-main.js
  var RECALC_INTERVAL = 1e3;
  var TARGET_RECALC_INTERVAL = 250;
  function startPage(payload) {
    if (!payload || !payload.appInfo) return;
    installGameViewCapture();
    const app = payload.appInfo;

    const tilesIntel = createTilesIntel();
    let topPlayerLens = null;
    let attackAdvisor = null;
    let teamDetection = null;
    let roundLogger = null;
    let recalcTimer = null;
    let targetRecalcTimer = null;

    let observedIntentCount = 0;
    const OBSERVED_INTENT_CAP = 400;
    logger.banner();

    teamDetection.observeLobby();

    });

    });
    installMapAssetHook({ bus, logger });
    installNetworkMetadataLogger({ settings, roundLogger });
    const getAutomationStatus = () => ({
      autoExpand: autoExpand?.getStatus?.() || null,
      autoFarm: autoFarm?.getStatus?.() || null,
      autoEco: autoEco?.getStatus?.() || null,
      autoDefense: autoDefense?.getStatus?.() || null,
      autoBoat: autoBoat?.getStatus?.() || null,
      autoWeapons: autoWeapons?.getStatus?.() || null,

      autoTeamSupport: autoTeamSupport?.getStatus?.() || null,
      quickChat: quickChat?.getStatus?.() || null
    });
    controlHud = createControlHud({

    });

      getRecentActions: () => roundLogger?.getRecentActions?.() || [],
      getAutomationStatus
    });
    actionHud.create();
    registerEvents();
    function registerEvents() {
      bus.on("mapLoaded", (mapData) => {
        mapDataRef.current = mapData;
        const startedAt = performance.now();
        staticCandidatesRef.current = precomputeStaticScores(mapData);
        logger.info(
          \`Static spawn analysis: \${staticCandidatesRef.current.length} candidates in \${(performance.now() - startedAt).toFixed(0)}ms\`
        );
        roundLogger.recordMapLoaded(mapData);
        heatmap = createHeatmap({ mapDataRef });

        missileOverlay.start();

        quickChat = createQuickChatAutomation({

        });
        assistActions = createAssistActions({
          gameState,
